# OVERWATCH — Feature Glossary

A section-by-section, metric-by-metric reference: **what each number shows, how it is
computed, its unit, and where the data comes from.** Built to answer a teammate's
"what does *95s* mean here?" or "what goes into *that* formula?" without reading code.

- For the one-line capability **index** (what exists and where), see [FEATURES.md](FEATURES.md).
- For architecture and data flow, see [ARCHITECTURE.md](ARCHITECTURE.md); for the alert
  catalogue and deep operational detail, see [RUNBOOK.md](RUNBOOK.md).

> Generated from the 2026-08-17 nine-page audit map (74 sections, 358 metrics). A later
> consolidation gave each duplicated metric a single **owner** page (e.g. the egress $
> story now lives on Cost, glanced from Security). Where a moved metric still appears
> under two pages below, the **owner** page named in the Conventions is authoritative.

---

## Conventions — read this first

These cross-cutting rules answer most "what does this mean / why this number" questions.

**Money & credits.** Snowflake bills **credits**; the app prices them in dollars.
- **Compute rate** = `CREDIT_PRICE_USD` (default **$3.68/credit**); **AI/Cortex rate** =
  `AI_CREDIT_PRICE_USD` (default **$2.20/credit**). Both are editable on **Admin ▸ Settings**
  (the code constants are offline fallbacks only).
- `$ = credits × rate`. AI/Cortex credits are priced at the AI rate; everything else at the
  compute rate; a **blended** figure prices each stream at its own rate.
- Dollars are always run through `format_usd`; a table's `_USD` columns get a `$`.

**Measured vs allocated $ (important for query/pattern costs).**
- **Measured** = exact `QUERY_ATTRIBUTION_HISTORY` credits for a query, **warehouse idle
  excluded** — the true cost of that query. Lags ~8h.
- **Allocated** = the query's execution-second **share** of the whole warehouse-hour bill,
  **idle included** — "who owns the bill". Reads higher than measured. The two are shown
  side by side deliberately (they answer different questions), not by mistake.

**Mart vs live.** A **mart** (`MART_*`, `FACT_*`) is a pre-aggregated table refreshed by a
scheduled task — cheap to read, may lag its cadence. A **live** read hits
`SNOWFLAKE.ACCOUNT_USAGE.*` directly — current but expensive and itself lagged by Snowflake
(~45 min, metering up to 24h). Most panels are **mart-first with a labeled live fallback**.

**Time: account vs server.** Streamlit-in-Snowflake cannot ALTER SESSION, so SQL `CURRENT_DATE()` /
`CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()` resolve in the account's default TIMEZONE. That default is **America/Chicago**
(verified 2026-09-21, level ACCOUNT), the same clock as the app's `account_now()` / `account_today()`.
New builders still pin Central explicitly (`account_today_sql()`, `account_month_start_sql()` or
`CONVERT_TIMEZONE('America/Chicago', …)`); see the TIMEZONE STANDARD in `app/data/common.py`, locked by
`tests/test_timezone_standard.py`. "Yesterday", "complete days", and the exhaustion clock use account
time. A per-day rate divides by the days actually elapsed in the window.

**Percentiles.** **p95** (e.g. "95s") = the value **95%** of measurements fall under —
the near-worst case a normal user feels, not the average. **p50** = the median. On the
mart path p95 may be the **peak daily** p95 (one bad day penalizes), labeled where shown.

**Humanization.** Bytes → `humanize_gb`/`humanize_bytes` (binary MB/GB/TB); durations →
`humanize_duration` (e.g. `95s`, `2h 3m`); a `%` column shows the `%` sign. A **dash (—)**
means telemetry was unavailable, **not zero** (v4.606: an infinite ratio or score also shows `—`, and an
empty change cell is never coloured green/red). On a table over 400 rows (or 6,000 cells) a humanized
duration column's header help says its click-sort is textual; the order as loaded and the CSV use the
real value (v4.606). Every sentence that names a duration uses the
same helpers (`tests/test_duration_format_consistency.py` sweeps app code, v4.605): "Spent 2m 30s
queued (of 3m 20s total)", "2h 25m/day queued", "Session refreshed 5m ago". The exception is a
Snowflake parameter's configured value (AUTO_SUSPEND, STATEMENT_TIMEOUT_IN_SECONDS), which stays
in the parameter's own seconds to match the ALTER statement and the alert titles.

**Company scope.** The account is shared (ALFA + Trexis). Most panels honor the **Company**
filter by warehouse/database/role ownership; account-level events ride along as `ALL`.
A per-viewer **watchlist** spans companies.

**Anomaly / robust-z.** Spikes use a **median/MAD modified z-score** (not mean/std, which a
spike inflates to hide itself). A flag needs `|z| ≥ 3.5` **and** materiality (≥ $50 on the
day, ≥ 10 active days) so an idle warehouse's trivial day doesn't cry wolf.

## Brief
*9 sections · 17 metrics.*

### Page verdict line + contract runway bar (top of scroll)
The computed 'should I worry?' opener. page_verdict() composes a worst-first Healthy/Watch/Attention sentence from the shared attention signals (open criticals + oldest age, undelivered criticals, stale telemetry, open incidents, XLAT gaps, the nightly ETL roll-up) plus the contract runway band. Directly below it, contract_runway_bar draws the contract runway: from the Snowflake billing balance when it is readable and fresh, else from the configured credit commitment (Next-Fifty #19, v4.589). Both re-state numbers shown in the KPI rows as a single glance.

| Metric | Means | Formula | Unit | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Verdict sentence (Healthy / Watch — … / Attention needed — …)** | One-line summary: label is the worst signal level; body lists the worst concerns bad-before-warn (e.g. '2 open critical alert(s), oldest 30h; 1 open incident(s); contract runway 12 days at current burn'). If nothing worse than ok, shows the healthy fallback 'no open criticals or incidents, delivery clear, telemetry fresh[; nightly ETL cycle clean / ; nightly ETL: no failures so far — only when the nightly roll-up loaded]; contract runway healthy' (or '; no contract runway configured' when no contract is configured). | logic.verdict.page_verdict(signals, healthy=...): worst level by _RANK{bad3,warn2,ok1}; body = '; '.join(concern phrases sorted by rank desc). Signals = the shared attention_signals(attention_bundle(...)) set the Control Room verdict also uses (parity-locked): open criticals with the oldest one's age, undelivered criticals, stale telemetry sources, open incidents (incident_metrics.OPEN_NOW, uncapped; len() of the LIMIT-5 open_incidents feed only when that read fails), XLAT reference gaps and the nightly ETL roll-up + cycle SLA (config-gated) — an unreadable count is a warn, never an all-clear; plus the Brief's own contract runway via verdict.contract_runway_signal — shared with Cost Intelligence since v4.606: DAYS_LEFT 0-30 bad, 31-90 warn; a negative DAYS_LEFT with severity bad (consumption past the configured credits) = bad 'contract commitment exhausted', the -1 sentinel (burn not computable) = warn, a failed runway read = warn; with no contract configured the healthy text ends 'no contract runway configured' instead of 'contract runway healthy'. | text | pure logic.verdict; inputs from health_strip (UNDELIVERED_CRITICAL, STALE_SOURCES) + open_alert_severity_counts (CRIT, OLDEST_CRIT_MIN) + incident_metrics / open_incidents + attention.etl_attention (XLAT gap + nightly ETL probes) + contract_exhaustion / the org billing balance (contract_planner.best_runway) |
| **Contract runway bar (days left, exhaust date, decide-by; % consumed on the credits basis)** | The contract runway, colored bad<=30d / warn<=90d / ok otherwise, from the Snowflake billing balance when it is readable and fresh, else the configured credit commitment (Next-Fifty #19, v4.589). The label ends with the basis (' · billing balance as of <date>' or ' · configured credits'). The billing-balance basis has no committed total, so its bar is label-only (no %-consumed fill). Hidden only when neither basis is available: with CONTRACT_CREDITS unset, a readable, fresh balance still renders it. | contract_planner.best_runway(org balance rows, logic.formulas.contract_runway(exh row)). Billing-balance basis, used when remaining_balance_summary is ok, the balance as-of date is at most BALANCE_MAX_LAG_DAYS (3) days old, and a burn was observed or the balance is already at or below 0: days_left = max(0, the balance runway_days - the as-of lag); exhaust_date = today + days_left (none beyond 3,653 days, shown '—'); decide_by = exhaust - 30 lead days; storage and transfer included, currency-neutral. Otherwise the configured-credits fallback: pct = min(CONSUMED/TOTAL*100,100) rounded 1dp; days_left = DAYS_LEFT; decide_by = EXHAUST_DATE - 30 lead days (contract_runway returns None when TOTAL<=0). | days / date (percent on the credits basis) | cost_parts.contract.org_balance_result -> cost_sql.org_remaining_balance(120) -> ORGANIZATION_USAGE.REMAINING_BALANCE_DAILY (probe); mart_sql.contract_exhaustion -> SETTINGS(CONTRACT_CREDITS, CONTRACT_START_DATE) + FACT_METERING_DAILY.CREDITS_BILLED (mart) |

### Headline KPIs (kpi_row)
Two bands of compact tiles, the numbers-first half of the brief: three FIXED headline slots (MTD credit spend / Open criticals / Nightly cycle) that never reflow, then a secondary band of conditional context tiles, each band drawn by its own components.kpi_row call (the Executive export keeps the full ordered set). A dash means telemetry unreachable, not zero.

| Metric | Means | Formula | Unit | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **MTD credit spend (account)** | Account-wide month-to-date dollar spend on credit-billed services (compute/serverless/AI). Storage, data transfer, and org currency adjustments are excluded. Badge 'mart' when strip up, 'stale' + '—' when telemetry unreachable. | If split present: blended_billed_usd(MTD_CREDITS_OTHER*rate + MTD_CREDITS_AI*ai_rate); else fallback MTD_CREDITS*rate. rate=CREDIT_PRICE_USD(3.68), ai_rate=AI_CREDIT_PRICE_USD(2.20). Delta shows '{MTD_CREDITS:,.0f} credits'. | USD (delta in credits) | health_strip() mtd CTE -> FACT_METERING_DAILY.CREDITS_BILLED SUM since account_month_start, split AI vs OTHER by ai_service_predicate (mart) |
| **Open criticals** | Count of open+ack CRITICAL alert events for the selected company plus account-level ('ALL') events — same scope as the Fires table. Badge 'live'; '—'+warn when unavailable. | int(safe_float(CRIT)) from open_alert_severity_counts: COUNT_IF(UPPER(SEVERITY)='CRITICAL') WHERE STATUS IN ('OPEN','ACK') and (COMPANY=comp OR COMPANY='ALL'). | count | mart_sql.open_alert_severity_counts -> ALERT_EVENTS (core/live) |
| **Nightly cycle** | The fixed third headline tile (v4.526: it replaced 'Open incidents', an owner ask; the open-incident count still feeds the verdict line and the Control Room owns the queue): the whole nightly ETL cycle, worst state first. Failures ('N failed task(s)' in the latest run), Missing runs ('N workflow(s) didn't run'), Not started ('cycle hasn't kicked off'), Failures ('terminal workflow failed'); '—' 'awaiting cycle data' when there is no forecast; while the latest night is INCOMPLETE: Overdue (past the target, still running), At risk (projected past the target), Running long, or In flight (projected ~HH:MM (range) / due now / cycle still running); then Late (the latest night finished after the target) and Regressing (finish trending later); On track (green) only when the latest night COMPLETED with a margin >= 0, naming a month/quarter-end spike due tonight; anything else '—' 'awaiting cycle data'. '—' 'ETL not monitored' when ETL_CONTROL_STATUS_FQN is blank. | brief._nightly_cycle_kpi(cycle forecast, failed-task count, missing-workflow count, not_started = the night roll-up's next_cycle_overdue, eta = insights.etl_cycle_eta); severities: Failures / Missing runs / Not started / Overdue / Late bad, At risk / Running long / Regressing warn, In flight and '—' info, On track ok; target = the forecast's target_hhmm (07:00 default). | status | attention.etl_attention (the read the verdict line shares) -> the ETL_CONTROL_STATUS_FQN control table: night roll-up + SLA finish forecast + tonight's ETA |
| **Stalest telemetry** | Name + age of the single worst-lagging load source by cadence ratio; 'name never loaded' when that source has no data at all; 'unknown' when the strip itself is down. | _stalest_label(vals): STALEST_SOURCE_NAME + humanize_duration(STALEST_SOURCE_H,'h'); '-1' hours => 'never loaded'. Strip picks MAX_BY(age, urgency) where urgency = age_h/limit, limit = stale_daily_fact_hours for DAILY/METERING else stale_fact_hours, never-loaded=1e9 sentinel. | source name + humanized hours | health_strip() fresh CTE -> SOURCE_FRESHNESS_STATE.LAST_LOAD_TS (core) |
| **Oldest open critical** | Time since the oldest still-open (OPEN or ACK) CRITICAL alert was raised: how long a critical has gone unhandled, which a raw count hides. Appended only when Open criticals > 0 and OLDEST_CRIT_MIN is non-NULL. Severity bad if >=24h, else warn. Scope matches Open criticals: the selected company plus account-level ('ALL') events. | max(0, OLDEST_CRIT_MIN)/60 hours, where OLDEST_CRIT_MIN = MAX(CASE WHEN UPPER(SEVERITY)='CRITICAL' THEN DATEDIFF('minute', RAISED_AT, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()) END) over the OPEN/ACK rows with no row limit; rendered humanize_duration(h,'h'). Deliberately not oldest_open_hours over the LIMIT-50 open_alert_events feed: a storm of more than 50 criticals pushes the oldest ones out of that feed, under-reporting the age. | humanized hours | mart_sql.open_alert_severity_counts(company) -> ALERT_EVENTS RAISED_AT (STATUS IN OPEN/ACK) (core/live) |
| **Contract balance exhausts / Credit commitment exhausts** | Projected date the contract runs out at current burn, with a '{days_left} days at current burn' delta; delta inverse-colored when <=90 days. Labelled 'Contract balance exhausts' on the billing-balance basis (storage and transfer included) and 'Credit commitment exhausts' on the configured-credits fallback (credit-billed services only: storage, transfer and currency adjustments excluded). Shown when a runway exists with days_left >= 0 (an overrun or uncomputable credits runway is left to the verdict line). Help = contract_planner.runway_basis_note: it names the basis; on the balance basis it adds the configured-credits model's days ('N% apart. Check CONTRACT_CREDITS in Settings.' when the two differ by more than 15%) and says the COST_CONTRACT_BREACH paging alert stays on the configured-credits basis; on the credits basis it says why the balance was not used. | The same contract_planner.best_runway dict as the runway bar (billing-balance rule there). Credits fallback, contract_exhaustion(): DAILY_BURN = SUM(CREDITS_BILLED)/NULLIF(COUNT(DISTINCT DAY),0) over DAY BETWEEN today-30 AND today-1 (complete days only); DAYS_LEFT = CEIL((TOTAL-CONSUMED)/burn); EXHAUST_DATE = DATEADD(day, DAYS_LEFT, today); CONSUMED = SUM(CREDITS_BILLED) since CONTRACT_START_DATE. Value = the exhaust date, or '>10y'. | date (delta in days) | org balance (ORGANIZATION_USAGE.REMAINING_BALANCE_DAILY) + mart_sql.contract_exhaustion -> SETTINGS + FACT_METERING_DAILY (mart) |
| **Verified savings run-rate** | Monthly run-rate of savings VERIFIED in the last 12 months (proven by before/after actuals, never mixed with estimates), with delta '/mo vs {app cost} monthly run cost'; green when the run-rate covers app cost (OVERWATCH pays for itself). Does not reset at a quarter start; this quarter's verified $ is in the help text. | savings_summary_quarter VERIFIED_ACTIVE_MONTHLY_USD = SUM(VERIFIED_USD) WHERE STATE='VERIFIED' AND VERIFIED_AT >= DATEADD('month', -SAVINGS_ACTIVE_MONTHS, account today) AND not reverted (v4.600: a booked warehouse change the daily scan later saw made costlier drops out — or a change the same scan saw with it, one measured window, was undone). app_usd = APP_CREDITS_30D * rate (None when 0). delta_color normal if verified>=app_usd else inverse. | USD/mo | mart_sql.savings_summary_quarter -> SAVINGS_LEDGER (core); app cost from app_cost_last_30d -> FACT_WAREHOUSE_DAILY |
| **Estimated pipeline** | Open ESTIMATED savings dollars awaiting the monthly verifier — deliberately shown apart from verified. Only appended when pipeline>0. | savings_summary_quarter ESTIMATED_OPEN_USD = SUM(ESTIMATED_USD) WHERE STATE='ESTIMATED' AND not a superseded twin AND not reverted (v4.600: an ESTIMATED change the daily scan already saw undone is excluded; all-time open stock, no quarter filter). | USD | mart_sql.savings_summary_quarter -> SAVINGS_LEDGER (core) |

### Undelivered-critical banner
A primary-styled button that only appears when a critical alert reached nobody — the one surface a half-awake on-call reads. Clicking navigates to Alerts > Native delivery.

| Metric | Means | Formula | Unit | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **⚠ N critical alert(s) reached nobody (oldest …)** | Count of OPEN criticals >=30 min old with no delivery row, plus the oldest such age. Shown only when count>0. | UNDELIVERED_CRITICAL / UNDELIVERED_OLDEST_MIN from health_strip crit CTE: COUNT_IF(STATUS='OPEN' AND RAISED_AT<=now-30min AND no ALERT_DELIVERIES row); MAX(DATEDIFF minute) for oldest. Age rendered humanize_duration(min,'min'). | count + humanized minutes | health_strip() -> ALERT_EVENTS LEFT JOIN DISTINCT ALERT_DELIVERIES (core) |

### Fires (open critical/high alerts table)
The top-5 open critical+high alert events, company-scoped, most-severe first. Compact verified-clean row (empty_state 'clean') when none; needs_setup empty-state when alerting not installed; v4.606: any other failed read renders unavailable, 'Couldn't read open alerts right now.', with the error. Button navigates to Alerts > Open events.

| Metric | Means | Formula | Unit | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Fires table (top 5 CRITICAL/HIGH)** | Highest-severity open events for the company + account-level, capped at 5 rows. | open_alert_events(50,company) filtered in Python to SEVERITY in {CRITICAL,HIGH}, .head(5). SQL: STATUS IN ('OPEN','ACK'), (COMPANY=comp OR COMPANY='ALL'), ORDER BY severity rank then RAISED_AT desc. | table | mart_sql.open_alert_events -> ALERT_EVENTS (core/live) |

*Columns:* **RAISED_AT** — Timestamp the alert event was raised (account-time NTZ from ALERT_EVENTS.RAISED_AT).; **SEVERITY** — Alert severity token (CRITICAL/HIGH shown here) from ALERT_EVENTS.SEVERITY.; **TITLE** — Human-readable alert title from ALERT_EVENTS.TITLE.

### Asks (top-3 action queue)
The top three owner-pending actions as a markdown list — the asks-third half of the contract. Compact verified-clean row when nothing waits ('Action queue is empty.'); blue needs_setup 'Action queue not installed yet.' when the queue is absent; v4.606: any other failed read (a timeout) renders unavailable, 'Couldn't read the action queue right now.', with the error.

| Metric | Means | Formula | Unit | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Ask line: [SEVERITY] TITLE — owner {owner} · ~{$est}{/mo\|/yr\|one-time}** | One bullet per top action: severity tag, title, owner (or 'unassigned'), and estimated dollar impact with its time basis disclosed. Top 3 by severity, then overdue, then estimated $, then age. | logic.actions.rank_actions(action_queue(100,company).df, limit=3): filter STATUS in (OPEN,IN_PROGRESS) and drop rows deferred to a future DEFER_UNTIL (actions.deferred_mask, Next-Fifty #20; a 'Deferred (N): parked, not in the top 3; next resumes D' caption counts them from the feed's uncapped DEFERRED_TOTAL / NEXT_RESUME_DATE, and action_queue sorts deferred rows last before its LIMIT); sort by _SEV(severity rank, unknown->MEDIUM) asc, _OVERDUE(due date < today) desc, _USD(ESTIMATED_USD) desc, _AGE_H desc. $ rendered format_usd + md_dollars; basis from PERIOD (MONTHLY->/mo, ANNUAL->/yr, ONE_TIME-> one-time). | text (embeds USD + period) | mart_sql.action_queue -> ACTION_QUEUE (core/live); ranking pure in logic.actions.rank_actions |

*Columns:* **SEVERITY** — ACTION_QUEUE.SEVERITY (blank/unknown re-ranked at MEDIUM and shown as 'UNSET?').; **TITLE** — ACTION_QUEUE.TITLE — the ask; treated as data and $-escaped via md_dollars.; **OWNER** — ACTION_QUEUE.OWNER, or 'unassigned' when null.; **ESTIMATED_USD** — Estimated dollar impact; shown only when >0, with PERIOD basis appended.; **PERIOD** — Time basis of the estimate (MONTHLY/ANNUAL/ONE_TIME) — controls the /mo · /yr · one-time suffix.

### Watch badge
The proactive half of 'watch': how many of the viewer's watched entities moved (cost spike/drop or health-grade drop) and which. Quiet caption when steady; a warning + 'Open Watchlist' jump on movement. Renders nothing when the viewer has no watchlist. Cross-company by design. Next-Fifty #46 (v4.601): a watched TASK flags failed runs since yesterday and a P95 runtime spike; a watched QUERY_FINGERPRINT (query family) flags an attributed-cost spike/drop and a P95 spike. Their daily-mart read fires only when the viewer watches a task or query family (a warehouse-only watchlist pays nothing).

| Metric | Means | Formula | Unit | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **👁 N of M watched entities have moved: LABEL — STATUS** | Attention-first list (top 4) of watched warehouses/entities whose cost or health changed materially; steady entities collapse to a one-line caption. | workbench.watched_attention(viewer, rate) -> watched_status over FACT_WAREHOUSE_DAILY cost (spike/drop) + MART_WAREHOUSE_EFFICIENCY_DAILY health grade; watch_summary counts ATTENTION vs total. STATUS is the movement label per entity. Task / query-family arms (v4.601): FAILS since account_today()-1 > 0 -> warn; P95_SEC robust-z spike on the last two complete loaded days -> watch (floors WATCH_TASK_MIN_P95_SEC 60 s, WATCH_FAMILY_MIN_P95_SEC 10 s); family USD = CREDITS x rate, spike -> warn / drop -> watch, floor WATCH_FAMILY_MIN_USD $10/day (not the shared $50), known month-end spikes suppressed. A watch the read did not cover stays blank. | count + per-entity text | workbench_sql.watchlist -> USER_WATCHLIST; mart_sql.fact_warehouse_daily; mart27_sql.eff_sizing_profile (marts); workbench_sql.entity_daily_signals(include_today=True) -> MART_TASK_NODE_DAILY / MART_PATTERN_COST_DAILY / MART_QUERY_FAMILY_DAILY (only when a task or family is watched) |

*Columns:* **LABEL** — Display name of the watched entity.; **STATUS** — What moved — cost spike/drop or health-grade change text.

### AI morning narrative (collapsed expander)
The daily digest, collapsed by default so open fires stay above the fold — context, not headline. Since V165 SP_DAILY_DIGEST checks every figure in the Cortex draft against the exec-board facts it gave the model; when any figure does not match, or Cortex fails, it sends a templated digest built only from those facts instead. The title and chip show that MEASURED result: "AI morning narrative" with "AI-written; all N figures match the exec-board facts" (or "it states no figures to check"), or "Morning digest (templated, not AI-written)" with the reason; a templated digest lists the unmatched figures and offers the withheld AI draft behind "Show the withheld AI draft" (never sent). Rows written before V165 read "Figures not checked".

| Metric | Means | Formula | Unit | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **AI morning narrative / Morning digest (templated, not AI-written) — {DIGEST_DATE}** | The latest digest body (the version the digest routes received) with its date and a provenance chip. | latest_digest(grounded=has_migration(165)): most recent row by DIGEST_DATE, renders BODY; digest_grounding.digest_provenance(BODY_SOURCE, GROUNDING_OK, FIGURES_CHECKED, UNGROUNDED, AI_BODY) picks the title and chip. A figure matches a fact by unit ($ only *_USD, % only *_PCT), by the noun after it, and within half a step of its shown precision (inclusive) or 0.5%. Before V165, and on the last pre-V165 row, the chip reads "Figures not checked" and no caption claims the figures are checked; the source label reads "DAILY_DIGEST (Cortex draft)" until V165 is applied (digest_grounding.digest_source, chosen from the schema gate alone). | markdown text + chip | mart_sql.latest_digest -> DAILY_DIGEST (core; FACTS, GROUNDING_OK, FIGURES_CHECKED, UNGROUNDED, BODY_SOURCE, AI_BODY since V165) |

### Executive export (collapsed expander)

|---|---|---|---|---|

## Overview
*11 sections · 21 metrics.*

### Page verdict line ("should I worry?" opener)
One severity-striped banner at the very top summarizing whether the operator needs to act, assembled from signals already computed lower on the page (platform-score band, open critical/high alerts, projected-vs-budget). No new query.

| Metric | Means | Formula | Unit | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Verdict banner (healthy / warn / bad text)** | Worst-of the score band, open alert severities, and budget pace. Renders 'platform score healthy and no open critical alerts' when all clear. | page_verdict(list of Signal): bad if score<70 or projected>=15% over budget or any open critical; warn if score 70-84 / any open high / projected 0-15% over budget / score Incomplete. Same numbers as the KPIs below. | text + severity | app.logic.verdict.page_verdict over signals built in overview.render (score.score, critical_alerts, high_alerts, forecast.projected_usd vs budget) |

### Contract runway bar
Persistent contract-runway bar an exec sees on every visit — the one committed-spend number — from the Snowflake billing balance when it is readable and fresh, else the configured credit commitment (Next-Fifty #19). Whole-account, not narrowed by the company filter; the caption appends contract_planner.runway_basis_note (the basis, and why the balance was not used).

| Metric | Means | Formula | Unit | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Contract runway: days left / exhaust / decide-by (+ % consumed on the credits basis)** | Days until the contract runs out at current burn, the projected exhaust date and a 'decide-by' date (exhaust minus 30d procurement lead); on the configured-credits basis also the share of the purchased commitment burned since contract start (the billing-balance basis has no committed total, so its bar is label-only, no %-fill). The label ends ' · billing balance as of <date>' or ' · configured credits'. | contract_planner.best_runway(org balance rows, logic.formulas.contract_runway(contract_exhaustion row)), the Brief bar's dict. Billing balance (used when remaining_balance_summary is ok, the as-of date is at most 3 days old (BALANCE_MAX_LAG_DAYS), and a burn was observed or the balance is at or below 0): days_left = max(0, balance runway_days - the as-of lag), storage and transfer included. Configured-credits fallback: pct = min(CONSUMED/TOTAL*100,100); DAYS_LEFT = ceil((TOTAL-CONSUMED)/DAILY_BURN); DAILY_BURN = SUM(CREDITS_BILLED) over DAY in [today-30, today-1] / COUNT(DISTINCT DAY); CONSUMED = SUM(CREDITS_BILLED) since CONTRACT_START_DATE; TOTAL = SETTINGS.CONTRACT_CREDITS. Severity: <=30d left bad, <=90d warn. | days / date (percent on the credits basis) | cost_parts.contract.org_balance_result -> ORGANIZATION_USAGE.REMAINING_BALANCE_DAILY; mart_sql.contract_exhaustion -> SETTINGS + FACT_METERING_DAILY (mart). The bar is hidden only when neither basis is available: contract_runway returns None when CONTRACT_CREDITS<=0, but a readable, fresh balance still renders |

### Company economics (KPI row)

| Metric | Means | Formula | Unit | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Spend, <window> (<company>)** | Total warehouse-metering dollars for the selected company and window, complete days only (today's still-filling partial excluded). | SUM over complete days of DAILY_SPEND panel VALUE_USD, where VALUE_USD = ROUND(SUM(CREDITS_TOTAL)*credit_price). App drops rows where DAY>=account_today(). Fallback when daily_complete empty: KPI 'CREDITS' panel VALUE_USD (today-inclusive). credit_price default $3.68 from SETTINGS.CREDIT_PRICE_USD. v4.606: when the spend read itself failed (board, FACT_WAREHOUSE_DAILY and the live metering fallback) the tile reads 'Unavailable' (warn, the error in its help), and the case summary and executive export say 'unavailable (spend read failed)' — never a fabricated $0.00. | USD (sub-line: credits, = USD/rate) | mart_sql.exec_board -> MART_EXEC_BOARD panel DAILY_SPEND (mart, hourly refresh); live fallback overview._live_fallback_daily -> cost_sql.warehouse_daily_credits -> ACCOUNT_USAGE.WAREHOUSE_METERING_HISTORY.CREDITS_USED |
| **vs prior <days>d (delta on Spend tile)** | Percent change of this window's warehouse credits vs the immediately preceding equal-length window. Red when up (inverse). Hidden when mart absent or prior window is zero. | pct_delta(SUM(CREDITS_CURRENT), SUM(CREDITS_PRIOR)); windows are equal-length calendar days, today excluded. None -> no delta shown. | percent | mart_sql.fact_warehouse_window_vs_prior -> FACT_WAREHOUSE_DAILY.CREDITS_TOTAL (mart only) |
| **Average per observed day** | Window warehouse spend divided by the number of complete days actually present in the served series. | window_spend / observed_days, observed_days = daily_complete['DAY'].nunique() (today excluded). Card omitted when observed_days==0. | USD | derived in overview.render from the DAILY_SPEND-panel window_spend and its distinct complete days |
| **Top warehouse driver** | The single warehouse with the most window spend (compute credits), with its dollars and share of total warehouse spend. | argmax over COST_DRIVER panel of SUM(VALUE_USD) by DIMENSION; delta = format_usd(top_usd) + top_usd/SUM(all drivers)*100 %. | warehouse name; USD + percent | mart_sql.exec_board -> MART_EXEC_BOARD panel COST_DRIVER (DIMENSION=WAREHOUSE_NAME, VALUE_USD=SUM(CREDITS_TOTAL)*credit_price), FACT_WAREHOUSE_DAILY (mart) |

Account-wide even under a company filter (metering has no company grain). | Displayed value = SUM of blended_billed_usd over days where DAY>=1st of account month; blended = OTHER_credits*rate + AI_credits*ai_rate. Pace delta = (mtd_same - prior)/prior*100 comparing equal completed-day counts (today excluded on current side). If the refresh lacks the AI/OTHER split, every credit priced at compute rate and method badge reads 'flat-rate est.'. v4.606: 'Needs daily facts' / 'n/a (daily facts not deployed)' only for a true absence or a read that succeeded empty; a failed read (a timeout) reads unavailable ('unavailable (daily facts could not be read)' in the export). | USD (sub: credits = USD/rate); delta percent | mart_sql.fact_daily_spend(150) -> FACT_METERING_DAILY (CREDITS_BILLED / _AI / _OTHER split, mart); math in formulas.mtd_pace_vs_prior_month + blended_billed_usd; 45d read (fact_daily_spend(45)) is the fallback |
| **Projected month-end credit spend** | Forecast of the full calendar month's account-wide billed credit spend with an uncertainty range. 'linear' (Theil-Sen trend) default; optional 'seasonal' (day-of-week means) or 'ml_forecast' (SNOWFLAKE.ML.FORECAST) engines. | complete-day MTD + fitted forward daily rate x (today + remaining days), floored at MTD (monotonic). Range = projected +/- residual-std band inflated for autocorrelation & parameter uncertainty. Priced with AI/OTHER split (_billed_usd_series). ML engine prorates today's remainder and prices at compute rate (disclosed); v4.606: the ML reader includes today's forecast row (account clock), so the today-remainder term is no longer always 0, and when the ML table's horizon stops before month-end Overview falls back to the seasonal engine and the basis names the table's last day and says to retrain (SP_REFRESH_ML_FORECAST, snowflake/ml_forecast_option.sql); a table with no row for today or later falls back the same way and the basis says so (with the same retrain hint) instead of naming a day. Missing MTD days (a lagging yesterday) are filled at the baseline daily mean when the account is dense over at least the trailing baseline window (v4.606: judged MTD-only before, so on the 2nd with the 1st lagging the guard refused and month-end read a day low). | USD (range low-high in help) | logic.forecast.month_end_projection over the 150d FACT_METERING_DAILY frame (proj_daily, account-wide); ML path mart_sql.ml_forecast_daily -> FORECAST_ML_DAILY |
| **Open critical / high alerts** | Count of open+ack alert events at CRITICAL and HIGH severity for this company plus account-level ('ALL') events — same scope as the Alerts queue. | COUNT_IF(UPPER(SEVERITY)='CRITICAL') / COUNT_IF(=... 'HIGH') over STATUS IN ('OPEN','ACK); uncapped aggregate (not the 500-row feed). Severity: bad if any critical, warn if any high. | count / count | mart_sql.open_alert_severity_counts(company) -> ALERT_EVENTS (live tier); fetched via first-paint run_batch |
| **Platform score** | 0-100 evidence-based operating health score, or 'Incomplete' when required health inputs didn't load. Does not move when the spend window changes (health signals read a fixed recent window). | 100 - sum of capped named penalties: budget pace (projected/budget over 100%), critical alerts (6pt ea, cap 24), high alerts (2pt, cap10), query fail % over 2 (1.5/pt, cap12), task fail % over 1 (2/pt, cap14), queue min/day over 10 (0.3, cap10), spill GB/day over 5 (0.5, cap8), stale sources (4 ea, cap12), open high/critical actions (1.5 ea, cap9). Any open critical vetoes score to 84. Weights overridable via SCORE_PTS_* settings. | score /100 (band: Healthy>=85, Watch>=70, Degraded>=50, At risk) | logic.scoring.platform_score over signals from FACT_QUERY_HOURLY (fixed 1-day window), FACT_TASK_DAILY, ALERT_EVENTS counts, health_strip STALE_SOURCES, ACTION_QUEUE, forecast/budget. Sparkline = 14d of logic.scoring.score_history over FACT_PLATFORM_SCORE_DAILY |

## Control Room
*8 sections · 34 metrics.*

### Page header + verdict banner (always-on, above the section switcher)
The DBA's first 'should I worry?' glance. Renders an undelivered-critical banner button and a one-line page verdict (Healthy/Watch/Attention) composed from the shared attention bundle the Brief also uses (parity-locked in tests/test_attention_parity.py): the shell's health strip, the company-scoped critical counts, the uncapped open-incident count (incident_metrics, the same cache entry Incidents & triage reuses) and the config-gated ETL attention reads.

| Metric | Means | Formula | Unit | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **⚠ N critical alert(s) reached nobody (…oldest Xm) — check delivery →** | Count of OPEN (unacked) CRITICAL alert events at least 30 min old that have NO row in ALERT_DELIVERIES — a page that routed to no one. Account-wide by design; clicking jumps to Alerts ▸ Native delivery. | health_strip: COUNT_IF(e.STATUS='OPEN' AND e.RAISED_AT<=DATEADD('minute',-30,CURRENT_TIMESTAMP()) AND d.EVENT_ID IS NULL) over ALERT_EVENTS LEFT JOIN DISTINCT ALERT_DELIVERIES, filtered SEVERITY='CRITICAL'. Oldest age = MAX(DATEDIFF('minute',RAISED_AT,CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())) of those rows. | count of alerts; age humanized via humanize_duration(min) | mart_sql.health_strip -> CORE ALERT_EVENTS + ALERT_DELIVERIES (live tier, shared 'health_strip' cache key with the sidebar); values parsed from METRIC=UNDELIVERED_CRITICAL / UNDELIVERED_OLDEST_MIN |
| **Page verdict line (Healthy / Watch — … / Attention needed — …)** | Worst-first sentence from the shared verdict.attention_signals (Next-Fifty #1, parity-locked with the Brief). Bad: open criticals (with the oldest one's age), criticals that reached nobody, open incidents (OPEN or MITIGATED), source codes missing XLAT translation, failed ETL tasks tonight, nightly workflows that did not run, a nightly cycle that has not started, and a cycle-SLA breach (terminal workflow failed, still running past the target, or finished after it). Warn: telemetry sources stale or not loaded, a cycle finish trending later, and 'open-critical count unavailable' / 'health telemetry unavailable' / 'open-incident count unavailable' when a read fails (never an all-clear). A MITIGATED incident with no criticals and a fresh strip reads 'Attention needed — 1 open incident(s)'. | page_verdict(attention_signals(attention_bundle(strip_vals, crit_row, open_incidents, etl)), healthy=attention_healthy(...)); healthy = 'no open criticals or incidents, delivery clear, telemetry fresh', plus '; nightly ETL cycle clean' (or '; nightly ETL: no failures so far' while workflows are running or pending) when the night roll-up loaded; worst level sets the label, phrases concatenated bad-before-warn. | text label + clauses | open_alert_severity_counts(company).CRIT / OLDEST_CRIT_MIN + health_strip UNDELIVERED_CRITICAL / STALE_SOURCES + mart_sql.incident_metrics(90, company).OPEN_NOW (uncapped, company + account-level, not limited to the 90-day window) + attention.etl_attention(settings) (reference_gap_summary, cycle_night_read, nightly_cycle_forecast) |
| **Incidents & triage pill count badge** | The section switcher tab 'Incidents & triage' carries a count badge = open CRITICAL alert events in the company scope. | int(safe_float(open_alert_severity_counts.CRIT)); shown via lazy_sections counts= | count | mart_sql.open_alert_severity_counts(company) -> CORE ALERT_EVENTS COUNT_IF(UPPER(SEVERITY)='CRITICAL') where STATUS IN('OPEN','ACK') and (COMPANY=company OR 'ALL') (live tier) |

### Action Center
The persistent DBA work queue (ACTION_QUEUE): what needs an owner decision, ranked, with lifecycle controls (assign/defer/comment/resolve) and the evidence graph. The per-action "Start optimization experiment" expander was retired in v4.597 (Option C). Query families arrive here from Operations ▸ Optimize ▸ Track as UNASSIGNED MEDIUM/LOW items whose CONFIDENCE is OVERWATCH's evidence score, not an authored belief (their DETAIL says so). Task-failure and warehouse-spend rows arrive from Control Room ▸ Incidents & triage ▸ Track as work item (v4.601) as UNASSIGNED MEDIUM/LOW, unpriced items with SOURCE 'Control Room > Triage'. Cost ▸ Chargeback & AI ▸ Track top exceptions (v4.605) writes through the same statement (SOURCE 'Cost Intelligence > Chargeback & AI > AI users'): one UNASSIGNED item per user (SOURCE_ENTITY_TYPE USER) at the strongest signal's severity, so unlike the two above it can arrive CRITICAL or HIGH, priced MONTHLY; plus one AI_BUDGET item for the all-users budget breach keyed on the Company scope, which has no Entity 360 page. A later Track whose signal outranks every open item of that user from this page raises one of them, the strongest (never lowers it; a user already holding an open item from this page at that severity is left as is, a Security item on the same user is neither counted nor touched, and a pre-v4.605 user's other per-signal items keep their severity).

| Metric | Means | Formula | Unit | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Open work** | Count of actions in OPEN or IN_PROGRESS status in scope (items deferred to a future resume date are left out and counted as Deferred). v4.606: every matching item, not the first 500 read; with Include completed work the open items list first, so closed history never pushes open work past the cap, and a capped list says 'The list shows the first 500 of N matching items'. | action_summary: KPI_OPEN_TOTAL = COUNT_IF(OPEN/IN_PROGRESS and not deferred) OVER (), an uncapped window total computed before the LIMIT 500 (the row sum only for a frame without the KPI_* columns — 'Assigned to me' drops them, so its counts cover only the rows read). | count | workbench_sql.action_center(company,include_closed,500,with_kpi_totals=True) -> CORE ACTION_QUEUE (V074 extended shape; live). v4.606: no legacy fallback — a failed read renders unavailable with the error (needs_setup only when ACTION_QUEUE is truly absent or unreadable). |
| **Critical / high** | Open actions whose SEVERITY is CRITICAL or HIGH — priority work needing a decision. | action_summary: KPI_CRITICAL_HIGH_TOTAL (window total before the LIMIT, v4.606; row fallback (active & SEVERITY in {CRITICAL,HIGH}).sum(), active = OPEN/IN_PROGRESS and not deferred) | count | ACTION_QUEUE.SEVERITY |
| **Overdue** | Open actions whose DUE_DATE is strictly before today (account tz). | action_summary: KPI_OVERDUE_TOTAL = COUNT_IF(active AND DUE_DATE < account today) OVER () (v4.606; row fallback (active & due.notna() & due.normalize() < account_today()).sum(), active = OPEN/IN_PROGRESS and not deferred) | count | ACTION_QUEUE.DUE_DATE; today = account_today() |
| **Unassigned** | Open actions with a blank, 'UNASSIGNED' or team-placeholder owner ('DBA', 'DBA TEAM', 'DBA / AI GOVERNANCE'). | action_summary: KPI_UNASSIGNED_TOTAL = COUNT_IF(active AND UPPER(TRIM(OWNER)) IN TEAM_PLACEHOLDER_OWNERS) OVER () (v4.606; row fallback the same mask over the frame) | count | ACTION_QUEUE.OWNER |
| **Estimated opportunity** | Sum of open items' authored ESTIMATED_USD (modeled, not billed). CAVEAT surfaced in help: mixes PERIOD bases (monthly run-rate / one-time / annual / unlabeled) at face value; never mixed with verified savings. | action_summary: KPI_ESTIMATED_USD_TOTAL = SUM(IFF(active, ESTIMATED_USD, 0)) OVER () (v4.606; row fallback dollars[active].sum(), active = OPEN/IN_PROGRESS and not deferred); format_usd() | USD | ACTION_QUEUE.ESTIMATED_USD (PERIOD column carries the basis but is NOT normalized before summing) |
| **Held? (with Include completed work, v4.601)** | Did the fix hold? A DONE warehouse / task / query-family item measured on its entity's own daily mart signal since it was marked done: 'Held 27 days', 'Re-broke Sep 21', 'Not fixed', 'Too early (3 of 7 days)', 'Not measurable', 'Unavailable' (the read failed) or 'Not checked' (beyond the 40 newest completions in 90 days); '—' for rows that are not DONE. The selected DONE item shows a Held? chip and the basis ('$X/day before → $Y/day after, N days measured'; 'F failed of R runs in the 28 days before (x%); f of r since (y%)'); only Re-broke (a measured regression) adds 'reopen it with Status: OPEN' — Not fixed says the level never fell 20% below its baseline (check the fix was applied). An item tracked from Control Room triage (SOURCE 'Control Room > Triage') is judged on the signal it was tracked for, so a resolved spike, collapse or failure burst whose signal is quiet reads Held, never Not fixed. Account-wide, not company-filtered. | outcomes.action_held: baseline = the 28 days before the done day (credits: zero-filled mean; task P95: median of run days); after = the days after it to min(today-1, the mart's LOADED_THROUGH) so a stalled loader never reads as a fix; failure arm (task / family with >= 2% failed runs before, and EVERY triage task item whatever its rate): the same rule on the failure RATE — the trailing 7-day rate (failed / runs) must fall >= 20% below the baseline rate (fixed), and a week back at >= 90% of it with a failed run in it = Re-broke, so one stray failure on a fixed high-volume family is not a re-break while a daily task failing again is; a week with no run has no rate (it drops out), and when no week since done had a run the item reads Not measurable (v4.606: 0 of 0 runs is not a measured 0%, so a task suspended after done no longer reads Held); else the trailing 7-day level must fall >= 20% below baseline (fixed), and climbing back to >= 90% = Re-broke (dated when the week crossed back, so it lags by up to 6 days); never dropping = Not fixed; < 7 after-days = Too early. Triage warehouse items: Re-broke on the first after-day the triage spend scan would flag again in the direction the item was tracked for (TITLE 'Spend anomaly:' = a spike, 'Spend collapse:' = a collapse) — a robust z of +3.5 or more (spike) or -3.5 or less (collapse) over the warehouse's own trailing 30 days, a spike day >= $50, a collapse on a window median >= $50, >= 10 active days, and the EXPECTED_SPIKE_CALENDAR clearing an upward hit; otherwise Held (Not measurable for another title, or for a collapse item with no spend row since). | label + basis text | workbench_sql.entity_daily_signals (FACT_WAREHOUSE_DAILY, MART_TASK_NODE_DAILY, MART_PATTERN_COST_DAILY + MART_QUERY_FAMILY_DAILY); one read, only when a DONE row of those types completed in the last 90 days |
| **Selected work item detail: Severity/Status/Owner/Due chips, Confidence badge** | Per-selected-row chips. Confidence = authored belief (0-1) set at creation by operator or recommendation engine — a stated belief, not a measurement. | status_chips + confidence_badge(row.CONFIDENCE) | labels; confidence 0-1 ratio | ACTION_QUEUE row (SEVERITY, STATUS, OWNER, DUE_DATE, CONFIDENCE) |

*Columns:* **TITLE (Decision)** — The action's title — what to decide/do.; **DETAIL (Why)** — Free-text rationale/evidence for the action.; **ESTIMATED_USD (Impact)** — Authored modeled dollar estimate (not billed); read the PERIOD basis before trusting.; **CONFIDENCE** — Authored confidence 0-1 at creation time; belief, not measurement.; **OWNER** — Accountable owner; blank, UNASSIGNED or a team placeholder (DBA, DBA TEAM, DBA / AI GOVERNANCE: workbench.TEAM_PLACEHOLDER_OWNERS) counts as unassigned.; **STATUS** — OPEN / IN_PROGRESS / DONE (+ other lifecycle states via V074).; **DUE_DATE (Next)** — Commitment date; before today = overdue.; **SEVERITY / PERIOD / SOURCE_ENTITY_TYPE / SOURCE_ENTITY_KEY (context)** — Severity band; estimate time-basis; the entity this action was raised against (drill target for Entity 360; an AI_BUDGET scope key has no Entity 360 page and does not drill).; **Activity and comments (ACTION_ACTIVITY)** — ACTIVITY_TYPE, FROM/TO_STATUS, OWNER_NAME, DUE_DATE, DEFER_UNTIL, NOTE, CREATED_AT/BY — the audit trail of lifecycle changes.; **Evidence graph (EVIDENCE_LINKS)** — LINK_ID, FROM/TO_TYPE+ID, RELATIONSHIP, LABEL, QUERY_ID (Snowsight-profile linked), EVIDENCE_NOTE, CREATED_AT/BY — typed evidence attached to the action.

### Pulse (since yesterday 00:00)
Midnight-anchored query-health pulse for the account/company: what ran and what broke since yesterday, plus a 14-day activity trend. Fact-first (FACT_QUERY_HOURLY); live QUERY_HISTORY fallback when a schema filter is active or the fact is empty.

| Metric | Means | Formula | Unit | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Queries (since yday)** | Total queries from yesterday 00:00 to now (24-48h depending on time of day). Sparkline = last 14 complete days; delta = last complete day vs the day before (neutral color). | mart: SUM(QUERY_COUNT). delta via pct_delta(last_complete_day, prior). Sparkline from fact_daily_activity QUERIES. | count; delta in % | mart_sql.fact_query_window_summary(1,company,'','',database) -> MART FACT_QUERY_HOURLY (HOUR_TS>=DATEADD('day',-1,CURRENT_DATE())); live fallback ops_sql.query_window_summary -> ACCOUNT_USAGE.QUERY_HISTORY COUNT(*); schema path mart27_sql.schema_window_summary -> FACT_QUERY_SCHEMA_HOURLY SUM(QUERIES) |
| **Failed** | Failed queries in the same window; delta shows fail rate %. Turns 'bad' when fail rate > 2%. | mart: SUM(FAILED_COUNT); fail_pct = failed/qcount*100; _fail_bad = failed/qcount > 0.02 | count; delta % | FACT_QUERY_HOURLY.FAILED_COUNT (mart) / SUM(IFF(EXECUTION_STATUS<>'SUCCESS',1,0)) (live) |
| **p95 runtime [ (peak hourly) when mart ]** | 95th-percentile query elapsed time. On the mart path it is the MAX of hourly-group p95s (a true p95 needs raw rows), labeled '(peak hourly)'; on the live path it is a real APPROX_PERCENTILE. | mart: MAX(P95_ELAPSED_SEC); live: APPROX_PERCENTILE(TOTAL_ELAPSED_TIME/1000,0.95). humanize_duration(...,'s'). | seconds (humanized H/M/S) | FACT_QUERY_HOURLY.P95_ELAPSED_SEC (mart) / QUERY_HISTORY (live) |
| **Queued** | Aggregate queued time (overload+provisioning) since yesterday. Also raised as an exception when ≥60s. | mart: SUM(QUEUED_SEC_SUM); live: SUM(QUEUED_OVERLOAD_TIME+QUEUED_PROVISIONING_TIME)/1000. humanize_duration('s'). | seconds (humanized) | FACT_QUERY_HOURLY.QUEUED_SEC_SUM (mart) / QUERY_HISTORY queued cols (live) |
| **Remote spill** | Bytes spilled to remote storage, in GiB, humanized to MB/GB/TB. Raised as an exception when > 0. | mart: SUM(SPILL_REMOTE_GB); live: SUM(BYTES_SPILLED_TO_REMOTE_STORAGE)/1024^3. humanize_gb(). | GiB (humanized bytes) | FACT_QUERY_HOURLY.SPILL_REMOTE_GB (mart) / QUERY_HISTORY (live) |
| **Exception summary (Failed queries / Queue pressure / Remote spill / Query evidence unavailable)** | Exception-first roll-up above the KPIs; each fires from the same numbers (failed>0, queued≥60s, spill>0, or qcount<=0). | components.exception_summary over locally-computed thresholds | labels + values | same pulse row |

*Columns:* **14-day query activity — Queries chart** — charts.daily_metric_line x=DAY, y=QUERIES (daily query volume, 14 complete-ish days) from fact_daily_activity(14,company,database) -> FACT_QUERY_HOURLY SUM(QUERY_COUNT) grouped by day (v4.606: under a Schema filter the trend is hidden with a caption saying the daily fact has database grain only — it used to replace the section with an error).; **14-day query activity — Failed queries chart** — Same frame, y=FAILS = SUM(FAILED_COUNT) per day.

### Incidents & triage
The morning triage core: declared incidents (lifecycle Gantt, member drill, operator close/declare) plus the ranked triage queue merging open alerts, task failures, and in-app spend anomalies.

| Metric | Means | Formula | Unit | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Open incidents (KPI + exception)** | Declared incidents still OPEN or MITIGATED in scope. Shown twice: in the exception summary and as the sole surviving KPI (lifecycle medians moved to Alerts ▸ History). | incident_metrics: (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM INCIDENTS WHERE STATUS IN('OPEN','MITIGATED') and company arm) | count | mart_sql.incident_metrics(90,company) -> CORE INCIDENTS (recent tier, 90d window for the frame; OPEN_NOW is a scope-wide subquery) |
| **Open criticals (exception)** | Open CRITICAL alert events in scope — the same _open_crit used in the header verdict/badge. | open_alert_severity_counts.CRIT | count | mart_sql.open_alert_severity_counts(company) -> ALERT_EVENTS (live) |
| **Stale sources (exception)** | Telemetry feeds past their freshness SLA (from the shell health strip). | int(safe_float(health_strip METRIC=STALE_SOURCES)) | count | mart_sql.health_strip STALE_N -> SOURCE_FRESHNESS_STATE |
| **Ready to close (caption)** | Open (OPEN/MITIGATED) incidents in scope whose every ALERT member is RESOLVED and whose SUPERSEDED / SNOOZE_SUPPRESSED members have no live same-rule same-company successor — the root cause still needs a human close. The per-incident drawer shows the same note for the selected row. | incident_metrics READY_TO_CLOSE_N from the shared _incident_ready_cte (LIVE_MEMBERS = 0 AND LIVE_SUCCESSORS = 0), counted over the uncapped table, never len() of the LIMIT-50 list; the same predicate as V154's auto-mitigate sweep minus its 1h dwell. | count | mart_sql.incident_metrics(90,company) -> INCIDENTS x INCIDENT_MEMBERS x ALERT_EVENTS (live) |
| **Acknowledge / Mark mitigated (drawer, operators)** | Forward-only incident lifecycle writes (Next-Fifty #12a). Acknowledge + take ownership stamps ACK_AT and OWNER on an un-acknowledged OPEN/MITIGATED incident (one click); Mark mitigated moves OPEN -> MITIGATED and back-fills ACK_AT/OWNER when empty (typed MITIGATE). Both pre-check the live row (INC-1), are latched, and log incident_ack / incident_mitigate. | UPDATE INCIDENTS ... WHERE STATUS IN ('OPEN','MITIGATED') AND ACK_AT IS NULL / WHERE STATUS = 'OPEN'; OWNER = COALESCE(OWNER, viewer). | write | control_room._incident_ack_sql / _incident_mitigate_sql -> INCIDENTS |
| **Incident lifecycle Gantt (CR5)** | Per-incident bar detected→resolved (open runs to now) for the last 14 days, colored by severity. | incident_gantt: STARTED=DETECTED_AT::NTZ, ENDED=COALESCE(RESOLVED_AT,CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())::NTZ, DURATION_MIN=DATEDIFF('minute',DETECTED_AT,ENDED) | time span (minutes) | mart_sql.incident_gantt(14,company) -> CORE INCIDENTS (recent). charts.incident_gantt |
| **Triage queue — item count + ranking** | One ranked list merging open alert events, task failures (summed across the window, one row per task), and in-app warehouse spend anomalies/collapses. Ranked by severity, then unowned first (an alert nobody has acknowledged; a task or warehouse with no open Action Center item), then dollars-at-risk, then KIND (v4.601). Dismissed, Done and Unknown rows sort as unowned, so a failed status read never demotes a row. v4.606: a source that failed to load (open alerts, task facts, warehouse spend fact) is named whether or not the queue is empty — needs-setup only for a true absence, else unavailable with the error — the caption lists only the sources that loaded, and an empty queue with a source unread is never an all-clear; when the alert feed returns its 500-row cap the caption says the alert rows are the 500 most severe/newest open events and gives the uncapped open count. | logic.actions.triage_queue(alerts, tasks, anomalies): severity rank -> _USD (anomaly excess_usd; alerts/tasks 0) -> KIND. Alert-feed drops COST_ANOMALY_SWEEP twins (_dedupe_alert_feed). Then fix_queue.with_triage_track_status: a stable re-sort on severity rank x 2 + owned (owned = alert STATUS 'ACK' or TRACKED 'Tracked (open)'), index reset BEFORE the table and its positional row-click are built. | count; internal $ tiebreak | alerts=open_alert_events(500,company); tasks=fact_task_daily(2,company,db) w/ ops_sql.task_runs live fallback; anomalies from fact_warehouse_daily(30,company); tracked status = workbench_sql.tracked_entity_actions(('TASK','WAREHOUSE')) (one plain read, only when a trackable row exists) |
| **Track as work item (expander, operators)** | Track a task-failure or warehouse-spend row into Action Center (v4.601, Next-Fifty #15). Pick the row by identity ('Task · DB.S.T' / 'Warehouse · WH'); the statement is shown before the one-click Track button (operator gate + C48 latch per row). A Tracked (open) row gets 'Open in Action Center →' instead. Alerts are never tracked: they are owned through Acknowledge and the incident flow (an ACTION_QUEUE item has no lifecycle link to ALERT_EVENTS). | fix_queue.track_entities_sql(entity_type TASK or WAREHOUSE, source 'Control Room > Triage', bulk=False): Optimize's idempotent INSERT keyed on UPPER(type) + UPPER(key) + OPEN/IN_PROGRESS; TASK key = DB.SCHEMA.TASK; COMPANY = the selected company, or under ALL the task's database / the warehouse's company (UNKNOWN -> 'ALL'); SEVERITY MEDIUM for a HIGH/CRITICAL row else LOW (never HIGH); unpriced; UPDATED_BY = the viewer. track_entities_sql refuses any other entity type. | write (one statement) | logic.fix_queue.triage_track_item + track_entities_sql -> ACTION_QUEUE |
| **Spend anomaly severity / z-score / excess** | Robust median/MAD modified z per warehouse over 30 complete days; HIGH at \|z\|≥7 OR excess ≥ $500/day over its own median baseline; else MEDIUM. Collapse (z<0) = 'Spend collapse' (stalled pipeline). Only the most-recent-complete-day hits surface — v4.606: that day cut runs inside anomaly_summary BEFORE its top-10, so 10+ stronger historical spikes or weekend collapses in the 30-day frame can no longer evict yesterday's spike. | anomaly.flag_anomalies(USD, group=WAREHOUSE_NAME, min_value=$50, min_active_days=10, threshold=3.5) on complete_days_only; excess_usd = value - per-wh median USD; suppress_expected_spikes(EXPECTED_SPIKE_CALENDAR); ANOMALY_HIGH_Z=7, ANOMALY_HIGH_EXCESS_USD=500 | USD/day, z (unitless) | mart_sql.fact_warehouse_daily(30,company) -> MART FACT_WAREHOUSE_DAILY CREDITS_TOTAL; credits_to_usd(rate) |
| **Baseline median $/day + thin-warehouse note (caption)** | Median of the per-warehouse median baselines; plus the count of warehouses the anomaly rule can never flag: fewer than 10 active (non-zero-spend) complete days in the 30-day window while holding a material ($50+) day — new or mostly idle (v4.606: it counted only warehouses with <5 distinct days, missing 5-9-day-old and mostly idle ones; the caption now states the 10-active-day rule). | _base_median_usd = median of per-wh USD medians; _thin_warehouses = anomaly.unscorable_groups(_wh_complete, 'USD', 'WAREHOUSE_NAME', min_active_days=ANOMALY_MIN_ACTIVE_DAYS (10), min_value=ANOMALY_MIN_USD ($50)) — flag_anomalies' own gate | USD/day; count | derived in control_room.render from _wh_complete |

*Columns:* **Triage queue: SEVERITY** — CRITICAL/HIGH/MEDIUM/LOW — alert severity, or HIGH if a task failed ≥3x else MEDIUM, or anomaly HIGH/MEDIUM per z/excess.; **Triage queue: KIND** — Alert / Task failure / Spend anomaly / Spend collapse.; **Triage queue: DATABASE** — Database for task-failure rows (blank for alerts/anomalies which lack DB grain).; **Triage queue: TITLE** — Alert title / '<db.schema>.<task> failed Nx' / '<wh> daily spend z=…'.; **Triage queue: DETAIL** — Alert detail (≤220 chars) / last task error / '$value vs robust baseline (+$excess off baseline)'.; **Triage queue: SOURCE** — ALERT_EVENTS / FACT_TASK_DAILY / FACT_WAREHOUSE_DAILY — origin feed.; **Triage queue: RAISED_AT** — Event time (alert RAISED_AT / task DAY / anomaly day), rendered as text for Arrow; drives sort/tz.; **Triage queue: AGE** — rec27 display-only 'Nh ago' companion computed via humanize_age(RAISED_AT, account_now()).; **Triage queue: TRACKED** — v4.601: Action Center status of a task / warehouse row — Tracked (open), Done or Dismissed within 90 days, Untracked, or Unknown when the status read failed; '—' for alerts.; **Triage queue: Ack by / ACK_AT** — v4.601: who acknowledged an alert (the directory name, login fallback) and when; '—' when unacknowledged or not an alert.; **Open incidents table: INCIDENT_ID / SEVERITY / STATUS / COMPANY / TITLE / DETECTED_AT / STARTED_AT / Declared by / ACK_AT / Owner / MITIGATED_AT / READY_TO_CLOSE / MEMBERS** — open_incidents(50,company,lifecycle=True): identity, severity, OPEN/MITIGATED, scope, title, detect time, first-impact time, declarer (name-resolved), first-response time, owner (name-resolved), mitigation time, every-member-resolved flag, and count of linked INCIDENT_MEMBERS (correlated subquery).; **Incident members: MEMBER_KIND / REF_ID / EVIDENCE_TS / AUTO_LINKED / Linked by / LINKED_AT / ALERT_TITLE / ALERT_STATUS** — incident_members_detail: each linked evidence row; ALERT_* joined from ALERT_EVENTS when MEMBER_KIND='ALERT'.; **Proposed incidents (operators only)** — incident_proposals: PROPOSAL_KEY, SUGGESTED_TITLE, SEVERITY, COMPANY, ENTITY_KIND/NAME, CONFIDENCE, ALERTS, MATCHED_WH/OBJECT/TASK counts, FIRST/LAST_TS, EVIDENCE — auto-correlated groupings a human must confirm; the read is incident_proposals(20, …), so a full page reads 'Proposed incidents (20+)' with a caption that more may be open (v4.606; it printed the capped count).

### Timeline & movers
Incident-correlation timeline (alerts+task failures+DDL on one axis with an hourly-spend overlay and ±30-min drill), lock-wait spikes, and warehouse spend movers (current window vs prior).

| Metric | Means | Formula | Unit | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Incident correlation timeline (48h fresh / 7d cached)** | All alerts, task failures, and DDL changes on one time axis; select a row to see everything within ±30 minutes. | 48h path: mart27_sql.incident_timeline(48,company) -> MART_INCIDENT_TIMELINE; else mart_sql.incident_timeline(days,company) UNION of ALERT_EVENTS + TASK_HISTORY(STATE='FAILED') + QUERY_HISTORY DDL. ±30-min = pandas filter on AT. | timestamped events | MART_INCIDENT_TIMELINE (rebuilt hourly) / ACCOUNT_USAGE live union; charts.operational_replay |
| **Hourly spend overlay (CR9)** | Per-hour compute credits (as USD) drawn on the timeline's time axis so a cost spike lines up with events. | cost_sql.hourly_credits(tl_hours,company): SUM(CREDITS_USED) per DATE_TRUNC('hour'); USD=credits_to_usd(rate). HOUR_TS localized same as AT. | USD/hour | ACCOUNT_USAGE.WAREHOUSE_METERING_HISTORY (recent tier) |
| **Lock-wait spikes** | Objects with ≥5 waits last day AND >3× their own prior-6-day daily average. | mart27_sql.lock_wait_spikes: LAST_DAY_WAITS vs PRIOR_DAILY_AVG=SUM(prior WAIT_EVENTS)/6; HAVING LAST_DAY_WAITS>=5 AND >3*GREATEST(PRIOR_DAILY_AVG,1) | wait-event counts | MART_LOCK_WAIT_DAILY (recent; empty pre-V035) |
| **Spend movers (window vs prior) — top-10 by \|Δ$\|** | Per-warehouse credits this window vs the immediately prior equal-length window, dollarized, top 10 by absolute dollar move; caption sums net movement. | fact_warehouse_window_vs_prior(days,company): SUM(IFF(DAY>=today-days,CREDITS_TOTAL,0)) vs prior half, today excluded, window clamped to MAX_MART_WINDOW_DAYS//2. USD via credits_to_usd; DELTA_PCT via pct_delta. | USD; Δ% | MART FACT_WAREHOUSE_DAILY (recent); live fallback cost_sql.warehouse_window_vs_prior -> WAREHOUSE_METERING_HISTORY |

*Columns:* **Timeline: AT / EVENT_TYPE / SEVERITY / LABEL (+COMPANY, REF_ID on mart)** — Event time (localized), ALERT/TASK FAILURE/DDL CHANGE (mart uses KIND), severity, and a ≤120-char label.; **±30-minute drill table** — Subset of timeline rows within ±30 min of the selected anchor's AT.; **Lock-wait: DATABASE_NAME / SCHEMA_NAME / OBJECT_NAME / LAST_DAY_WAITS / PRIOR_DAILY_AVG / LAST_DAY_NEVER_ACQ** — Object identity; waits in the last day; prior-6-day daily average; last-day locks never acquired (aborted/timed-out).; **Spend movers: WAREHOUSE_NAME / COMPANY** — Warehouse and its owning company (COMPANY_FOR_WAREHOUSE on the live path).; **Spend movers: Current $ / Prior $** — USD in the current window / prior equal-length window (credits×rate).; **Spend movers: Δ $ / Δ %** — USD_CURRENT−USD_PRIOR; percent change via pct_delta (blank when prior=0).

### Freshness & replay
Telemetry-freshness board (which loaders are on time / stale / never-loaded) plus an opt-in one-day flight recorder ('Day replay') stitching spend, activity, DDL, grants, tasks, and alerts for a chosen day.

| Metric | Means | Formula | Unit | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Freshness STATUS (OK / STALE / NOT LOADED)** | Per source: NOT LOADED if never loaded (NULL LAST_LOAD_TS), else STALE if hours-since-load exceeds its cadence limit (daily/metering sources use stale_daily_fact_hours, others stale_fact_hours), else OK. Banners count never-loaded and stale. v4.606: 'not installed yet' only for a true absence; any other failed board read renders unavailable (staleness unknown) with the error and a jump to Admin's full freshness table. | _status(row): NOT_LOADED=LAST_LOAD_TS.isna(); limit=THRESHOLDS[stale_daily_fact_hours if 'DAILY'/'METERING' in name else stale_fact_hours]; STALE if HOURS_SINCE_LOAD>limit | counts of NOT LOADED / STALE sources | run_mart_first(source_freshness_state, source_freshness) -> SOURCE_FRESHNESS_STATE (HOURS_SINCE_LOAD=DATEDIFF('minute',LAST_LOAD_TS,CURRENT_TIMESTAMP())/60) / MART_SOURCE_FRESHNESS fallback |
| **Day replay — headlines + 6 evidence panels** | For one chosen day (default yesterday): worst-first headlines plus tables for spend movers vs 14d baseline, task failures, DDL, grant changes, and alerts raised. | replay_headlines(movers, activity, ddl_count, grant_count, task_failed_sum, critical_alert_count, rate); crit_n from day_alerts SEVERITY='CRITICAL'. v4.606: every read failing renders unavailable with the error (needs-setup only when each failed as a setup absence), never 'No telemetry loaded'; 'No telemetry loaded' only when the reads succeeded with nothing for the day, and a failed read keeps the day off the green quiet verdict. | mixed (credits, counts) | run_batch of day_spend_movers/day_activity/day_task_failures/day_alerts (FACT_* + ALERT_EVENTS) + security_sql.day_ddl/day_grants (QUERY_HISTORY/GRANTS_TO_USERS). Company-scoped; alerts include account-level. |

*Columns:* **Freshness board (no per-source table here)** — only the NOT LOADED count (error banner), the STALE count (warning) or 'All telemetry sources fresh', plus a 'Full freshness table → Admin' button to Admin ▸ Migrations & freshness, which owns the per-source table (SOURCE_NAME / LAST_LOAD_TS / ROW_COUNT / HOURS_SINCE_LOAD, listed there).; **Replay spend movers: WAREHOUSE_NAME / COMPANY / CREDITS_TOTAL / BASELINE_CREDITS / DELTA_CREDITS** — day_spend_movers: the day's credits, the trailing-14d AVG baseline credits, and their difference (shown in CREDITS, not USD).; **Replay task failures: DATABASE_NAME / SCHEMA_NAME / TASK_NAME / COMPANY / RUNS / FAILED / LAST_ERROR** — day_task_failures: tasks with FAILED>0 that day from FACT_TASK_DAILY.; **Replay DDL: START_TIME / user / ROLE_NAME / QUERY_TYPE / DATABASE_NAME / SCHEMA_NAME / DDL_PREVIEW** — day_ddl: successful DDL/GRANT/REVOKE that day (role-scoped to company), user name-resolved.; **Replay grants: GRANTED_AT / DELETED_ON / ROLE / GRANTED_TO / GRANTEE_NAME / GRANTED_BY** — day_grants: role grants created or revoked that day (GRANTS_TO_USERS, role-scoped), grantee name-resolved.; **Replay alerts: RAISED_AT / SEVERITY / RULE_ID / COMPANY / TITLE / STATUS** — day_alerts: alerts raised that day (company + account-level).

### Entity 360
One context surface for any entity (warehouse/db/object/task/query-fingerprint/user/role/alert/incident/action/data-product): ownership record, measured metric snapshot, recent changes, linked work, and evidence/savings history. Drill target for cross-page jumps.

| Metric | Means | Formula | Unit | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Ownership status chips** | Criticality, Team, Owner, Data product, SLO for the selected entity (or a catalog browse table when no key chosen). | status_chips from entity_record row | labels | workbench_sql.entity_record(kind,key) -> CORE ENTITY_CATALOG (live) |
| **Entity metric snapshot cards (WAREHOUSE et al.) — Credits / Queries / Failures / P95 runtime / Remote spill** | Up-to-5 measured metrics over the page 'days' window for metric-bearing types. Credits shown as USD (credits×rate); seconds humanized; GB humanized (humanize_gb, MB/GB/TB). | entity_metric_snapshot(kind,key,days): WAREHOUSE UNION over MART_WAREHOUSE_EFFICIENCY_DAILY (SUM CREDITS_TOTAL / SUM QUERIES / SUM FAILS / MAX P95_S / SUM SPILL_GB); non-warehouse via _entity_metric_nonwarehouse. _entity_metric_card formats by UNIT. | credits→USD, count, duration, bytes | CORE MART_WAREHOUSE_EFFICIENCY_DAILY (recent); AS_OF=MAX(DAY) |
| **Watch / Unwatch toggle** | Adds/removes the entity from the viewer's personal watchlist. | watchlist_sql(viewer,kind,key,label,remove=is_watched); is_watched from USER_WATCHLIST match on (type,key) | boolean action | CORE USER_WATCHLIST |
| **Data product detail (DATA_PRODUCT type)** | For a data product: its constituent catalog entities + a criticality/owner rollup (multi-owner flagged). | product_detail(product): ENTITY_CATALOG filtered by DATA_PRODUCT, ordered most-severe first | entity list | CORE ENTITY_CATALOG |

*Columns:* **Catalog browse table** — entity_catalog: ENTITY_TYPE, ENTITY_KEY, LABEL, COMPANY, TEAM, OWNER_NAME, STEWARD, ON_CALL, CRITICALITY, DATA_PRODUCT, SLO_NAME, NOTES, UPDATED_AT/BY — shown when no entity key is selected.; **Recent changes** — entity_recent_changes: CHANGED_AT, CHANGE (warehouse setting delta, or object/proc/task deploy), CHANGED_BY, VERDICT, DETAIL (the scan's VERDICT_DETAIL; v4.606: its p95 and queue durations re-rendered in Hr/Min/Sec — 'p95 30m → 40m' — by the shared wh_change.humanize_verdict_detail, as on Operations and Control Room) — from WAREHOUSE_CHANGE_REGISTRY / OBJECT_CHANGE_REGISTRY (90d). Non-tracked types get a note.; **Work and outcomes (related_actions)** — ACTION_ID, CREATED_AT, SEVERITY, TITLE, OWNER, STATUS, DUE_DATE, DEFER_UNTIL, ESTIMATED_USD, CONFIDENCE, UPDATED_AT, COMPLETED_AT, SOURCE — ACTION_QUEUE rows keyed to this entity (open first); SOURCE names where the item came from, and a Control Room triage item's Held? is judged on the signal it was tracked for. v4.601: a warehouse / task / query-family entity with a DONE item adds Held? (the Action Center measure, one gated mart read) and a caption with the newest completed item's outcome and basis.; **Remediation history** — related_remediations: EXECUTED_AT, FINDING_TYPE, TARGET_OBJECT, EST_MONTHLY_SAVINGS_USD, STATUS, RESULT_NOTE, EXECUTED_BY (REMEDIATION_LOG).; **Savings outcomes** — related_savings: ITEM_ID, ACTION_ID, DESCRIPTION, STATE, ESTIMATED_USD, VERIFIED_USD, VERIFIED_AT/BY, FINDING_TYPE, TARGET_OBJECT, PROOF_QUERY_ID (SAVINGS_LEDGER).; **Evidence relationships** — evidence_links: LINK_ID, FROM/TO_TYPE+ID, RELATIONSHIP, LABEL, QUERY_ID (Snowsight-profile), EVIDENCE_NOTE, CREATED_AT/BY.

### Watchlist (Entity 360 ▸ Watchlist sub-tab)
The viewer's personal cross-company watchlist turned into a live monitor: each watched warehouse annotated with a cost spike/drop + health-grade status, each watched task with failed runs since yesterday / a P95 runtime spike and each watched query family with a cost spike/drop / a P95 spike (v4.601), and any breached SLO threshold; row click drills into Entity 360.

| Metric | Means | Formula | Unit | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Attention banner ('👁 N of M watched entities have moved')** | How many watched entities changed: a warehouse cost spike/drop (robust anomaly) and/or a health-grade at WATCH/DEGRADED/AT RISK; a watched task's failed runs since yesterday or P95 runtime spike; a watched query family's spend spike/drop or P95 spike (v4.601, #46). | watched_attention -> watched_status(watchlist, FACT_WAREHOUSE_DAILY 30d ALL, warehouse_health(size_recommendations(eff_sizing_profile)), entity_daily = the #46 task / query-family arms, as on the Brief Watch badge); ATTENTION=bool(parts). watch_summary counts. | count | USER_WATCHLIST + MART FACT_WAREHOUSE_DAILY + MART_WAREHOUSE_EFFICIENCY_DAILY (all probe-gated, cross-company 'ALL') + workbench_sql.entity_daily_signals(include_today=True) -> MART_TASK_NODE_DAILY / MART_PATTERN_COST_DAILY / MART_QUERY_FAMILY_DAILY (only when a task or query family is watched) |
| **Crossed-threshold banner (CR16)** | Count of watched entities whose configured SLO objective is currently in BREACH. Objectives are no longer authored in the app (the Decision Studio SLO editor was retired in v4.597), but any ACTIVE SLO_OBJECTIVES row still badges here. | watchlist_threshold_status(frame, slo_cockpit.df); crossed=BREACHING.sum(); worst status wins per entity | count | workbench_sql.slo_cockpit -> SLO_OBJECTIVES + warehouse/task/family marts (BREACH when comparator fails and evidence fresh) |

*Columns:* **Watchlist table: VIEWER_NAME / ENTITY_TYPE / ENTITY_KEY / LABEL / CREATED_AT** — USER_WATCHLIST rows for this viewer (newest first).; **THRESHOLD** — SLO objective label for the entity ('—' when none), from watchlist_threshold_status.; **STATUS** — Merged live status from watched_status. WAREHOUSE: 'spend spike/drop (z …)' and/or 'health: At risk' (or Watch / Degraded), else 'steady'. TASK (v4.601): 'N failed run(s) since yesterday' and/or 'runtime spike (P95 …, z …)', else 'steady', or 'no runs in the last 30 days'. QUERY_FINGERPRINT: 'spend spike/drop (z …)' and/or 'P95 spike (…, z …)', else 'steady', or 'not in the family marts in the last 30 days'. Blank for every other entity type, and for a task or family watch the signals read did not cover: the read failed or was probe-absent, or the watch fell past the 40-entity cap (SIGNAL_MAX_ENTITIES, newest watches first).

## Cost Intelligence
*7 sections · 79 metrics.*

### Page opener (verdict + since-last-visit, renders on every section)
A page-level 'should I worry?' line driven by contract runway, plus a 'what changed since your last visit' banner from the viewer's own usage trail. The verdict line renders in cost.py render(); the since-last-visit banner is the shared components.since_last_visit_opener (C18), rendered on Cost, Alerts, Security, Proof (Decision Studio before v4.597), and Control Room > Action Center from one cached query, with profile-gated jump buttons to Alerts > Open events / Control Room > Action Center that skip the current page.

| Metric | Means | Formula | Unit | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Contract runway verdict line (e.g. 'contract runway 42 days at current burn (configured credits)')** | Days until the contract runs out at current burn, from the billing balance when it is readable and fresh, else the configured credit commitment (Next-Fifty #19); drives a bad/warn/healthy page verdict pill, and the in-band phrase ends with the basis, '(billing balance)' or '(configured credits)'. | days_left from contract_planner.best_runway (the Brief/Overview dict): balance basis = max(0, balance runway_days - the as-of lag); configured-credits fallback DAYS_LEFT = CEIL((CONTRACT_CREDITS - CONSUMED)/DAILY_BURN); DAILY_BURN = SUM(CREDITS_BILLED over DAY BETWEEN today-30 and today-1)/COUNT(DISTINCT DAY); CONSUMED = SUM(CREDITS_BILLED since CONTRACT_START_DATE). Signal (verdict.contract_runway_signal, shared with the Brief since v4.606): bad if 0<=days_left<=30, warn if <=90; an overrun commitment (DAYS_LEFT < 0, severity bad; only the credits basis goes negative) = 'Attention needed — contract commitment exhausted — consumption is past the configured credits' (it used to read Healthy); the -1 burn-not-computable sentinel = Watch; no runway with a failed contract_exhaustion read = Watch 'contract runway unavailable — telemetry not read'; no contract configured and no usable balance -> the healthy text says 'no contract runway configured', never 'contract on track'. | days (credits or org-currency balance internally) | org balance (cost_parts.contract.org_balance_result -> ORGANIZATION_USAGE.REMAINING_BALANCE_DAILY) + run(mart_sql.contract_exhaustion) -> SETTINGS (CONTRACT_CREDITS, CONTRACT_START_DATE) + mart FACT_METERING_DAILY.CREDITS_BILLED; tier=recent, mart-only |
| **Since your last visit (banner text)** | Count of new alerts / crit / high / actions raised since the viewer's previous APP_USAGE timestamp, phrased and severity-colored by since_last_visit_summary; rendered by components.since_last_visit_opener with jump buttons for non-quiet returns. | Text + severity computed in app.logic.actions.since_last_visit_summary(NEW_ALERTS, NEW_CRIT, NEW_HIGH, NEW_ACTIONS); '_ago' = humanize_minutes_ago(MINUTES_AGO). Only shown when LAST_VISIT is not null; jumps gated on quiet=False. | counts + relative time | run(mart_sql.since_last_visit(company)) -> APP_USAGE + ALERT_EVENTS + ACTION_QUEUE; tier=recent |

### Spend & Attribution
Where the credit-billed money goes by service, how the metering lens reconciles to the invoice, cloud-services health per warehouse, exact warehouse spend and allocated user/database estimates (with the grain-coverage ratio, v4.597), storage economics, and the unmapped-entity worklist. Global company+days apply; database/schema only narrow object-grain panels.

| Metric | Means | Formula | Unit | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Credit spend, Nd (account)** | Account-wide credit-billed services (compute + serverless + AI) priced at configured rates with the cloud-services rebate applied. Metering lens only; excludes storage/transfer/marketplace. | SUM over services of CREDITS_BILLED * RATE, where RATE = ai_rate for 'AI / Cortex' category else compute rate. df['USD']=CREDITS_BILLED*RATE; billed_usd=sum(USD). | USD | run_mart(fact_metering_by_service(days)) mart FACT_METERING_DAILY (fallback cost_sql.metering_daily_by_service -> ACCOUNT_USAGE.METERING_DAILY_HISTORY); category via logic.cost_coverage.service_category; rate from SETTINGS CREDIT_PRICE_USD (3.68) / AI_CREDIT_PRICE_USD (2.20) |
| **All-in billed, Nd (currency)** | The invoice total from ORGANIZATION_USAGE for this account+window: adds storage, transfer, marketplace/other that the credit tile omits. Org currency, UTC, ~72h lag. | TOTAL_USD from org_all_in_window_usd(days); help shows STORAGE_USD/TRANSFER_USD/OTHER_USD. Non-USD rendered as '{value} {CUR}', USD via format_usd. | USD or org currency | run(cost_sql.org_all_in_window_usd(days)) -> ORGANIZATION_USAGE.USAGE_IN_CURRENCY_DAILY; tier=historical, degrades if org not visible |
| **Cloud-services rebate applied** | The cloud-services adjustment Snowflake rebates before billing (<=10% of daily compute). | abs(sum(CREDITS_ADJUSTMENT * RATE)); rebate_usd is negative/zero. | USD | fact_metering_by_service CREDITS_ADJUSTMENT column * per-category rate |
| **Total credits, Nd** | Billed credits across all services this window (rebate applied); the dollar split is on the Credit-spend tile. | sum(CREDITS_BILLED) across services; format_credits. | credits | fact_metering_by_service CREDITS_BILLED |
| **— of which CoCo, Nd** | Cortex Code (Snowsight+CLI) token credits x AI rate. NON-ADDITIVE subset already inside Credit-spend/Total-credits; do not add to left tiles. | credits_to_usd(sum(TOTAL_CREDITS), ai_rate); '—' until fact loads. | USD | run(mart27_sql.ai_code_daily(days,'ALL')) -> FACT_AI_USAGE_DAILY; account-wide |
| **Spend with a native drill path** | Share of billed metering dollars backed by a native Snowflake object-level view (drill-ready coverage). | drill_ready_spend_share(service_coverage_inventory(df, rate, ai_rate)) — ready BILLED_USD / total BILLED_USD * 100. | % | logic.cost_coverage.service_coverage_inventory + drill_ready_spend_share; derived in-app from the metering frame |
| **Material coverage gaps** | Count of service totals above $500 or 1% of spend with no native object-level drill. | len(coverage[MATERIALITY=='Material' & DRILL_STATUS=='Service total only']). | count | service_coverage_inventory (material_usd=500, material_share_pct=1.0) |
| **Daily spend stacked chart** | Per-day USD stacked by service category (Warehouse / serverless / AI-Cortex etc.). | charts.daily_stacked_usd(df, DAY, CATEGORY, USD) where USD=CREDITS_BILLED*category rate. | USD/day | fact_metering_by_service |
| **Replication detail / Detail vs billed credits (drill)** | Native per-secondary-database replication credits priced, and the ratio of usage-history credits to billed metering credits. | USD=CREDITS*compute rate; ratio=detail_credits/metered_replication_credits*100. Empty -> org-currency fallback for REPLICATION/DATA_TRANSFER. | USD / % / count | run(cost_sql.replication_by_database) -> DATABASE_REPLICATION_USAGE_HISTORY; fallback cost_sql.org_usage_in_currency(30) |
| **SPCS spend / Notebook subset (drill)** | Snowpark Container Services pool credits and notebook container credits priced; notebooks are a subset of SPCS and must not be summed with pools. | USD=CREDITS*compute rate per row. v4.606: a pool's per-user drill says 'the feed has no rows' only for a successful empty notebook read; a failed notebook read renders unavailable (needs-setup when the view is not readable), with the error under Notebook subset. | USD | cost_sql.compute_pool_usage -> SNOWPARK_CONTAINER_SERVICES_HISTORY; cost_sql.notebook_container_usage -> NOTEBOOKS_CONTAINER_RUNTIME_HISTORY |
| **Total transferred / Estimated egress / Billable transfer / Effective $/TB (drill)** | All outbound+cross-region bytes (reconciles to Snowsight), the billable slice priced, and the implied $/TB. | total_bytes=sum(BYTES) humanized; EST_USD=(TB*rate_per_tb) where BILLABLE; rate_per_tb,used_org=egress_effective_rate_per_tb(org_transfer_usd, billable_tb, DATA_TRANSFER_USD_PER_TB[20]) — implied org rate unless >10x fallback (billable under-count) then setting. | bytes / USD / $ per TB | cost_sql.transfer_egress_priced -> DATA_TRANSFER_HISTORY; org truth cost_sql.org_all_in_window_usd TRANSFER_USD |
| **Marketplace (currency) tiles (drill)** | Paid Marketplace charges per currency, kept native (not converted through credit rate). | totals=groupby(CURRENCY).CHARGE.sum(); top 4 shown as '{amount:.2f} {currency}'. | native currency | cost_sql.marketplace_paid_usage -> ORGANIZATION_USAGE.MARKETPLACE_PAID_USAGE_DAILY |
