| **Cloud-services health STATUS per warehouse** | Per-warehouse cloud-services credit share with WATCH (>10%) / ELEVATED (>20%) banding — reading bands only since V157 (the fixed-ratio COST_CLOUD_SVC_RATIO alert was retired; COST_CLOUD_SVC_ANOMALY fires on a per-warehouse 28-day baseline step-change). | CLOUD_SVC_PCT = SUM(CREDITS_TOTAL-CREDITS_COMPUTE)/NULLIF(SUM(CREDITS_TOTAL),0)*100; STATUS CASE >0.20 ELEVATED, >0.10 WATCH else NORMAL. HAVING total>=0.5, excludes CLOUD_SERVICES_ONLY. | % + status | run_mart(fact_cloud_services_ratio(days,company)) FACT_WAREHOUSE_DAILY; fallback cost_sql.cloud_services_ratio_by_warehouse -> WAREHOUSE_METERING_HISTORY |
| **Compile-heavy families / CS credits by statement type / top shapes & users (drills, ELEVATED only)** | Why a warehouse is elevated: query families with heavy compile time, cloud-services credits by QUERY_TYPE, and per-shape/per-user CS credits. | CS_CREDITS = SUM(CREDITS_USED_CLOUD_SERVICES); per-1k = /COUNT*1000. Shapes gross (before 10% rebate). | credits / seconds / % | mart27_sql.family_compile_heavy / cost_sql.compile_heavy_families; mart_sql.cs_by_query_type_mart / cost_sql.cs_by_query_type; mart_sql.cloud_svc_top_shapes + cloud_svc_by_user -> MART_CLOUD_SVC_DAILY (V055) |
| **Which statement families bill the most cloud services (v4.595, every warehouse)** | Statement families (hash x type x warehouse x user) ranked by the cloud-services credits they used, not by compile time, with each family's driver class (incl. Sleep polling = a SYSTEM$WAIT loop), owner hint, next step, hours slept and marginal billed $. KPIs: sleep polling $ / CS credits / hours slept; share of CS in families averaging ≤0.5 s compile (invisible to the compile-heavy list); statement CS vs metered. | BILLED_CS_CREDITS = Σday LEAST(family CS, GREATEST(0, account CS + adjustment that day)) — what the bill drops by if that family alone stopped; complete metering days only (the newest, in-progress UTC day and today are NULL = unpriced). The Sleep polling KPI caps Σ sleep CS per day as a group. BILLED_CS_USD = credits_to_usd(BILLED_CS_CREDITS, compute rate). SLEEP_SEC = runs x the parsed SYSTEM$WAIT argument (unit-aware, system_wait.wait_seconds), or runs x average elapsed when the wait varies. Window totals are SUM() OVER () before the top-50 cut. | credits / USD / seconds (humanized) / % | mart_sql.cloud_svc_billed_families -> MART_CLOUD_SVC_DAILY x FACT_METERING_DAILY x MART_QUERY_FAMILY_DAILY x FACT_APP_COST_DAILY (mart only; rides the Spend first-paint batch); logic cs_driver.billed_family_view / billing_basis_note |
| **By warehouse: Current $ / Prior $ / Δ% (Attribution)** | EXACT warehouse metering credits x rate, current window vs equal prior window, company-scopable. This is the trustworthy breakdown; totals include idle + unadjusted cloud-services. | USD_CURRENT=credits_to_usd(CREDITS_CURRENT,rate); USD_PRIOR likewise; DELTA_PCT=pct_delta(cur,prior). Windows equal-length, today excluded. | USD / % | run_mart(fact_warehouse_window_vs_prior(days,company)) FACT_WAREHOUSE_DAILY (IFF split by day); fallback cost_sql.warehouse_window_vs_prior -> WAREHOUSE_METERING_HISTORY |
| **Grain coverage caption (Attribution, v4.597; was Decision Studio ▸ Cost Truth)** | How much of the metered warehouse bill each finer cost lens reaches: measured object-query compute and user-allocated credits, each as a share of metered warehouse credits. These are separate lenses, not addends; their sum means nothing. | cost_coverage.metered_grain_coverage(cost_truth frame): measured_pct = MEASURED/METERED*100, allocated_pct = ALLOCATED/METERED*100. METERED = SUM(FACT_WAREHOUSE_DAILY.CREDITS_TOTAL) (includes idle; excludes serverless and AI). MEASURED = SUM(FACT_OBJECT_COST_DAILY.CREDITS WHERE COST_ARM LIKE 'QUERY_COMPUTE%' AND OBJECT_FQN <> 'UNATTRIBUTED') (object-attributed query compute; excludes idle and the unattributed residual). ALLOCATED = SUM(MART_COST_ALLOCATION_DAILY.ALLOC_CREDITS WHERE DIMENSION='USER'). All three come from ONE frame (one window, today included; one company scope), never from the today-excluded vs-prior pool. The caption renders only when METERED, MEASURED and ALLOCATED are all present (a NULL basis is no evidence, not 0) and METERED > 0. Allocated here is the owner-scoped mart, not the warehouse-share bars, so per company it can exceed 100%; it is cleanest under Company = ALL. | percent | workbench_sql.cost_truth -> FACT_WAREHOUSE_DAILY + FACT_OBJECT_COST_DAILY + MART_COST_ALLOCATION_DAILY. It rides the "Load company attribution" batch (wh, daily, grain; hourly tier), with a serial fallback; Spend first paint is unchanged. |
| **Allocated $ by user / by database** | ESTIMATE splitting scoped warehouse spend by query elapsed-time (or credit-weighted mart) share; directional, not billing. | ALLOCATED_USD = ELAPSED_SHARE * _pool, where _pool = window warehouse USD matched to the served window (full for mart, <=90d for live via _alloc_pool). Top-10 + 'Other/not shown' = pool - top_usd; coverage = top_usd/pool. | USD | run_mart(mart27_sql.alloc_xdim_attribution -> FACT_COST_ALLOC_XDIM_DAILY) / fallback cost_sql.allocated_attribution -> QUERY_HISTORY elapsed share |
| **Cost by application × user (measured)** | Measured warehouse+QAS credits attributed to the client program (Tableau/dbt/driver) and user; excludes idle/serverless/storage/AI. | USD=credits_to_usd(CREDITS,rate); grouped by APPLICATION then user. 'Measured $ by application' (v4.606) charts each application's exact totals, APP_CREDITS / APP_QUERIES = SUM(...) OVER (PARTITION BY APPLICATION), computed before the row cap — the read keeps the top 1000 application x user x company rows by credits plus each application's top row, so every application keeps a row; a frame without those columns sums its rows and, at the 1,000-row cap, says the totals are a lower bound. | USD | run_mart(app_cost_sql.app_cost_mart -> FACT_APP_COST_DAILY V077) / app_cost_live -> SESSIONS x QUERY_HISTORY x QUERY_ATTRIBUTION_HISTORY |
| **Daily anomaly check (per warehouse)** | Median/MAD z-score outliers in per-warehouse daily USD over 30 days; collapses (z<0) flagged as stalled pipelines, spikes as overspend; known-spike calendar suppresses expected spikes. | flag_anomalies on USD=credits_to_usd(CREDITS_TOTAL,rate), min_value=ANOMALY_MIN_USD, min_active_days=ANOMALY_MIN_ACTIVE_DAYS, z threshold 3.5; suppress_expected_spikes(EXPECTED_SPIKE_CALENDAR). Root-cause waterfall via explain_by_warehouse (v4.606: refused, with the reason, when fewer than 7 days before the flagged day are loaded — no baseline is not a $0 baseline). A failed FACT_WAREHOUSE_DAILY read renders unavailable (needs-setup for a true absence), never 'facts haven't loaded yet' (v4.606). Below-warehouse drill (v4.600 #27, a switch inside the waterfall expander, reads nothing until on): mart27_sql.alloc_xdim_day_drivers (FACT_COST_ALLOC_XDIM_DAILY + FACT_WAREHOUSE_DAILY) -> anomaly_explain.explain_below_warehouse, by user and by database against a zero-filled 14-day average over the loaded days, exactly additive with a 'Not allocated to a query (idle / carry-over hours)' residual, keys outside the company's view grouped, not dropped: '(unclassified users)' (COMPANY_FOR_USER = UNKNOWN, i.e. task/service logins; not emitted under the UNKNOWN scope), '(users outside <co>)', '(databases outside <co>)' (ALFA/Trexis only; under UNKNOWN only '(users outside UNKNOWN)' and no database mask); a group that carries the largest change leads the summary. The labels live in anomaly_explain (UNCLASSIFIED_USERS_LABEL / outside_company_label); the top-N is ranked by \|delta\| over the loaded baseline days. Plus warehouse setting changes seen D±1 (changes_near_day over warehouse_change_registry) and a jump to Operations ▸ Queries (sets the sticky substring Warehouse contains filter and shows an arrival filter_note; the day is not carried). | USD + z-score | run(fact_warehouse_daily(30,company)) FACT_WAREHOUSE_DAILY; complete_days_only excludes today |
| **Native anomaly feed (second opinion)** | Snowflake's managed ML cost-anomaly verdicts shown raw beside the z-score sweep. | Rendered as-is (no app math). | raw | run(cost_sql.native_anomaly_insights, probe=True) -> SNOWFLAKE.LOCAL.ANOMALY_INSIGHTS. A failed read renders by its kind (v4.605): an absent feed shows the 'isn't available on this account yet' note; a missing column, a timeout or any other failure renders unavailable with the error. |
| **Storage MTD (daily avg) / Prior full month / $ per month by database** | Per-database storage on the calendar-month billing basis: month-to-date average of daily (active+TimeTravel+failsafe) bytes vs prior full month, priced. | TiB=(DB_BYTES+FAILSAFE_BYTES)/1024^4 (builder already divides bytes by days-in-period); USD_MONTH=TiB*STORAGE_USD_PER_TB_MONTH(23); MoM=(mtd_tib-prior_tib)/prior_tib*100 with loader-watermark backfill for under-loaded prior month. Under Last month the previous complete month is the primary view, rescaled by the watermark of the read that served it (v4.606: a complete live fallback month is no longer inflated ~1.5x by the stalled fact's watermark); v4.606: Current month and Current year show the month-to-date view (only Last month, date_windows.is_prior_month_window, switches it). | TiB / USD-per-month / % MoM | run(cost_sql.storage_by_database_calendar) FACT_STORAGE_DAILY; fallback DATABASE_STORAGE_USAGE_HISTORY |
| **Account storage by tier (all tiers)** | Account-wide storage split by tier priced at per-tier SETTINGS rates (table/stage/failsafe standard, hybrid/archive own rates). | per tier: TiB=bytes/1024^4, USD/mo=TiB*rate; rates std=23, hybrid=348.16, cool=4, cold=1. Total = sum. | TiB / $/TiB/mo / USD/mo | run(cost_sql.storage_account_truth) FACT_STORAGE_ACCOUNT_DAILY (V046) / _live ACCOUNT_USAGE.STORAGE_USAGE. A failed read renders by its kind (v4.605): when the live leg is absent the 'needs migration V046 or STORAGE_USAGE access' note shows; any other failure of the live leg renders unavailable with the error. |
| **Drill to tables: Active/Time-travel/Fail-safe/Clone/Total $, STALE on-stale** | Top-50 tables in a database priced by storage split, STALE = no DML 90d; generates a retention-reduction ALTER. | each $ = GB/1024*STORAGE_USD_PER_TB_MONTH; Non-active=TT+FS+Clone; retention ALTER via remediation.retention_fix. | USD/mo | insights_sql.table_storage_breakdown -> TABLE_STORAGE_METRICS + TABLE_DML_HISTORY |
| **Unmapped entities / Unmapped spend billed blind** | Entities the loaders stamped COMPANY='UNKNOWN' (no company evidence); dollar figure = warehouse credits at rate. Empty is the goal. | EST_USD = (VALUE where MEASURE=='credits') * rate; at_stake=sum(EST_USD). DATABASE/USER rows are counts (no $). v4.606: the worklist covers the page window (a trailing window up to 365 days, MAX_MART_WINDOW_DAYS; a calendar preset its exact range) — it used to stop at the last 30 days whatever the window said. The 'Map an entity' picker lists rows as 'NAME · Grain', so a name UNKNOWN under two grains (a user and a database) maps the row picked and writes that grain's scope. | count / USD | run(mart_sql.unmapped_entities(days, bounds=bounds)) -> FACT_WAREHOUSE_DAILY + FACT_QUERY_SCHEMA_HOURLY + FACT_LOGIN_DAILY where COMPANY='UNKNOWN' |

*Columns:* **SERVICE_TYPE / CATEGORY (coverage)** — Metering service and its app category (Warehouse, serverless, AI/Cortex).; **BILLED_CREDITS / BILLED_USD / SHARE_PCT (coverage)** — Billed credits, dollars (credits x category rate), and % of window spend.; **DETAIL_GRAIN / DETAIL_METHOD / DRILL_STATUS / MATERIALITY (coverage)** — Native drill granularity available, method, whether a drill exists ('Service total only' = gap), and Material/immaterial banding.; **WAREHOUSE_NAME / COMPUTE_CREDITS / CLOUD_SVC_CREDITS / TOTAL_CREDITS / CLOUD_SVC_PCT / STATUS** — Cloud-services health: compute vs CS credits, total, CS share %, and NORMAL/WATCH/ELEVATED.; **WAREHOUSE_NAME / COMPANY / USD_CURRENT / USD_PRIOR / DELTA_PCT** — Exact warehouse spend current vs prior equal window and % change; totals row shows current+prior sums.; **DIMENSION / ALLOCATED_USD (user & database bars)** — User (display name) or database and its elapsed-share-allocated dollars; includes 'Other / not shown' remainder.; **APPLICATION / USER_NAME / QUERIES / USD (app cost)** — Client program, user, query count, measured attributed dollars.; **DATABASE_NAME / TiB / USD_MONTH (storage by db)** — Database, average TiB on billing basis, priced $/month.; **Tier / TiB / $/TiB/mo / USD/mo (account tiers)** — Storage tier, binary TiB, per-tier rate, monthly dollars.; **SCHEMA_NAME / TABLE_NAME / Active $ / Time-travel $ / Fail-safe $ / Clone $ / Total $ / STATUS / RETENTION_DAYS / LAST_DML (table drill)** — Per-table storage split priced, staleness, current retention, last write.; **GRAIN / ENTITY / MEASURE / VALUE / EST_USD / LAST_SEEN (unmapped)** — Entity grain (WAREHOUSE/DATABASE/USER), name, measure kind, raw value, estimated window $ (warehouse rows only), last-seen day.

### Contract & Forecast
Account-wide contract pacing: calendar-year straight-line projection, billing-truth vs app-model rate-card reconciliation, org accounts spend, the Snowflake remaining-balance truth panel, credits pacing, dollars-per-day steering levers, and the renewal what-if planner. No company/db scope (org data has no company grain).

| Metric | Means | Formula | Unit | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **YYYY YTD (billed) / Projected YYYY total** | Year-to-date billed credits and straight-line year-end projection, each dollarized with AI credits at the AI rate. | YTD = ytd_other+ytd_ai credits; proj = ytd + burn*(frac_left+days_left) per partition (burn = trailing-30-completed-day mean). $ via blended_billed_usd(other,ai,rate,ai_rate). frac_left = fraction of today remaining; days_left = Dec31-today. v4.606: a failed read renders unavailable (needs-setup for a missing install) and an empty year (Jan 1, before the first metering row) a no-data caption — the strip used to vanish silently. | credits + USD | run(mart_sql.fact_daily_spend_year) FACT_METERING_DAILY (CREDITS_BILLED_OTHER/AI split); rates SETTINGS |
| **Rate-card reconciliation table (Model vs org %, Effective $/cr, AI model vs org %)** | Org rate-card dollars for this account vs app model (credits x configured rate), monthly, to catch a mis-set rate; residual = storage/transfer/discounts. | model_by_month = SUM(compute CREDITS_BILLED)*rate_now; DELTA_PCT=100*(model-org)/org COMPUTE_USD; EFF_RATE=org_usd/(model_usd/rate_now); AI_DELTA_PCT vs AI_USD. Suppressed (with warning) when org currency != USD. Contract rate from RATE_SHEET_DAILY EFFECTIVE_RATE. v4.606: needs-setup only for a true absence (an ungranted org view, an uninstalled fact); a timeout or drift on either side renders unavailable with the error, and an ok-but-empty side says no data yet (USAGE_IN_CURRENCY_DAILY lags up to 72h). | USD / % / $ per credit | cost_sql.org_account_month_usd(2) + mart_sql.fact_daily_spend_compute(70) + fact_daily_spend(70) + cost_sql.org_rate_sheet |
| **Org spend (30d) / Accounts / Largest account** | Org-level billed spend in currency across all accounts (Snowsight Accounts Spend Summary equivalent). | total=sum(USAGE_IN_CURRENCY); by_account groupby ACCOUNT_NAME. Stacked chart by account; amounts are org currency not credit-rate. v4.606: needs-setup (grant ORGANIZATION_USAGE_VIEWER) only when the org view is not visible; any other failed read renders unavailable with the error. | org currency / count | run(cost_sql.org_usage_in_currency(30)) -> ORGANIZATION_USAGE.USAGE_IN_CURRENCY_DAILY |
| **Remaining balance / Burn per day / Runway at this burn / On-demand overrun** | Snowflake contract balance truth: remaining commitment, daily draw-down, days of runway, and any usage past commitment. | From contract_planner.remaining_balance_summary(bal.df): burn = total draw-down / non-top-up days (idle days included, top-ups excluded); runway = remaining/burn; on_demand<0 => overrun = -on_demand. Currency label from summary.currency. | USD (or org ccy) / days | run(cost_sql.org_remaining_balance(120)) -> REMAINING_BALANCE_DAILY; cost_sql.org_contract_items -> CONTRACT_ITEMS for end date. A failed read renders by its kind (v4.605): an ungranted or absent view says it 'isn't visible to this role'; any other failure renders unavailable with the error (pacing then uses SETTINGS); a readable view with no rows says 'Org balance view returned no usable rows'. |
| **Consumed / Contract clock / Pace / Projected term total (term ended: Final term consumption / Term result)** | Credits pacing: consumed share of contract, elapsed-time share, pace ratio (>1 = burning fast), and projected term credits + overage. v4.606: once the term is over (today >= CONTRACT_END_DATE, which is exclusive) the row shows Final term consumption (share of contract) and Term result ('+N cr over' / 'N cr unused' vs the commitment) and withholds pace, the term projection and the steering levers, with a note to set the new term's CONTRACT_CREDITS / START / END on Admin. | contract_pace(consumed, CONTRACT_CREDITS, start, end, today, trailing_daily): consumed_share=consumed/total; time_share=elapsed/term_days; pace_ratio=consumed_share/time_share; projected = consumed + trailing_daily*(term_days-elapsed). trailing_daily = mean(CREDITS_BILLED over whole days, 30d). consumed = billed credits in [CONTRACT_START_DATE, CONTRACT_END_DATE) (v4.606: end-exclusive, the pace clock's term, so a term that ended before SETTINGS was rolled stops accruing post-term credits). Consumed flagged FLOOR if metering history starts after contract start. | credits / % / ratio | run_mart(mart_sql.fact_contract_consumed(start, end) / cost_sql.contract_consumed_credits(start, end)); burn mart_sql.fact_daily_spend(30) |
| **Steering levers table + verdict (dollars/day)** | Executable savings levers (auto-suspend tuning, top-5 recurring patterns) with realizable haircuts, vs the projected overage gap. Shown only while the term is running (v4.606). | idle lever = actionable idle monthly USD * 0.70, the idle advisor dividing by the days the idle read actually covers (served_days, v4.606 — not a fixed 30, so a loader-lagged mart no longer understates it); pattern lever = pattern monthly USD * 0.40. Levers priced at compute rate; gap priced at blended eff_rate (blended_credit_rate over whole-day burn). steering.steering_plan computes coverage %/gap. | USD/month | run_mart(mart27_sql.eff_idle_analysis(30,'ALL') / insights_sql.idle_warehouse_analysis) + mart27_sql.pattern_cost(30,'ALL',10) / insights_sql.expensive_patterns_usd; SHOW WAREHOUSES for settings |
| **Renewal planner scenarios (Term consumption / Recommended commit / Daily burn)** | Straight-line what-if from trailing-30d burn: term consumption, recommended commit (consumption + buffer), and daily burn under an optional added-load slider. | daily_usd = mean(CREDITS_BILLED whole days)*eff_rate + extra_credits*eff_rate; remaining_usd = max(0,(CONTRACT_CREDITS-consumed)*eff_rate), or 0 once the term is over (v4.606: the scenarios then only size the next term and the current-contract exhaustion column is dropped); contract_planner.plan_scenarios(daily_usd_adj, term_months[12-36], buffer_pct[0-40], remaining_usd). | USD | run(mart_sql.fact_daily_spend(30)) FACT_METERING_DAILY; eff_rate blended compute+AI |

*Columns:* **MONTH / ORG_COMPUTE_USD / APP_MODEL_USD / DELTA_PCT / EFF_RATE / ORG_AI_USD / APP_MODEL_AI_USD / AI_DELTA_PCT / ORG_STORAGE_USD / ORG_TRANSFER_USD / ORG_ADJUSTMENT_USD / ORG_TOTAL_USD (recon)** — Per-month org rate-card dollars vs app-modeled dollars, drift %, realized $/credit, and the residual buckets (storage/transfer/adjustment/total).; **CONTRACT_ITEMS table** — Committed amount and term dates straight from ORGANIZATION_USAGE.CONTRACT_ITEMS.; **ACCOUNT_NAME / SERVICE_TYPE / Spend(currency) (org accounts pivot)** — Org billed spend by account and usage type in native currency.; **Steering levers: lever name -> $/month** — Named lever (auto-suspend verified targets, top-5 patterns) with its realizable monthly dollars after the haircut.; **TERM_CONSUMPTION_USD / RECOMMENDED_COMMIT_USD / DAILY_BURN_USD (planner)** — Per-scenario projected term consumption, recommended commit incl. buffer, and daily burn.

### Chargeback & AI
Department chargeback (exact warehouse metering + allocated role lens + budgets + monthly statement export), a company all-in showback, query-tag governance coverage, account-wide Cortex/AI spend, and per-user Cortex Code attribution with budget/spike exceptions, per-user AI-quota blocks plus review-only suggested quotas, and token-cache economics. Company shapes chargeback, the all-in showback and AI users; Cortex service totals remain account-wide.

| Metric | Means | Formula | Unit | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Chargeback total (Nd) / Departments / Unmapped** | Exact warehouse-compute metering x rate mapped to departments (idle stays with owning dept); unmapped = warehouses with no DEPARTMENT_MAP row (should be $0). | USD=credits_to_usd(CREDITS_TOTAL,rate); dept groupby DEPARTMENT; unmapped_usd = USD where DEPARTMENT=='Unmapped'. | USD / count | run(chargeback_sql.department_window_credits(days,company)) -> FACT_WAREHOUSE_DAILY x DEPARTMENT_MAP (the hourly-loaded mart, which the source label names since v4.606) |
| **Company all-in showback: All-in total / Company-attributed share, before adjustment / Unattributed (no company key) / Cloud-services adjustment** | Per company, warehouse + serverless + Cortex Code + estimated storage dollars over the Window's complete metered days (today and the newest, possibly unfinished, daily-metering day never count), with the cloud-services adjustment and the unattributed remainder as account-level rows, so the rows add up to billed metering plus estimated storage. Showback, not an invoice: nothing is spread by a share. Under a named Company only that company's row and its share of the account's spend before the cloud-services adjustment show; an empty UNKNOWN scope reads verified-clean only when every keyed source covers the whole span in full (otherwise a quiet caption names the sources that do not). Coverage notes name a source that starts late or ends early (its dollars stay unattributed), an object-cost or Cortex Code fact whose newest span day was loaded before that day was complete (those facts reload after the 06:45 CT metering load), a metering gap, partial storage days and any negative family residual. | Metering (billed credits, CS adjustment applied) priced by service family (AI / Cortex at the AI rate, the rest at the compute rate) and bucketed WAREHOUSE / SERVERLESS / AI / OTHER. Company rows: WAREHOUSE_USD = FACT_WAREHOUSE_DAILY credits x rate (exact metering before the CS adjustment, by COMPANY); SERVERLESS_USD = FACT_OBJECT_COST_DAILY CLUSTERING / MV_REFRESH / SEARCH_OPT / SERVERLESS_TASK / SNOWPIPE credits x rate (the QUERY_COMPUTE* and residual arms are warehouse compute, left out); AI_USD = FACT_AI_USAGE_DAILY Snowsight + CLI credits x AI rate, company = COMPANY_FOR_USER on the grouped user; STORAGE_EST_USD = database + fail-safe TiB-months x the standard storage rate (each day at 1/days-in-month). Account rows: adjustment = the metering CS adjustment per bucket; unattributed = billed family - company rows - adjustment per bucket (OTHER = all of it) + account storage (FACT_STORAGE_ACCOUNT_DAILY tiers x the Admin tier rates) - company storage. All-in total = billed metering + estimated storage; share = company rows (UNKNOWN included) / the spend before the cloud-services adjustment (all-in total - adjustment = company rows + unattributed), so the company rows and the unattributed row add up to 100% and the adjustment row has no share (company warehouse dollars are before the adjustment, so the all-in total would let the share pass 100%). Partly loaded day: the object-cost and Cortex Code COVERAGE rows carry LOADED_AT = MAX(LOAD_TS) (Central); a fact whose last load ran before its newest span day ended + 3 h (view latency) is named. The 'What the unattributed row holds' breakdown's Unattributed column sums to the unattributed row. | USD / % | run(chargeback_sql.company_allin_showback) -> FACT_METERING_DAILY + FACT_WAREHOUSE_DAILY + FACT_OBJECT_COST_DAILY + FACT_AI_USAGE_DAILY + FACT_STORAGE_DAILY + FACT_STORAGE_ACCOUNT_DAILY (marts, co-scheduled in the Department chargeback batch, hourly cache); logic.showback.company_showback. Canary: chargeback.company_allin_showback |
| **Allocated $ by role** | Execution-time share per role within each warehouse x that warehouse's exact spend (usage lens, not billing). | ALLOCATED_USD = ELAPSED_SHARE * per-warehouse USD (pool window matched to share window; live clamps <=90d). groupby ROLE_NAME. The mart leg excludes warehouse-less statements (WAREHOUSE_NAME 'NONE'), the live twin's population (v4.606: under Company = ALL a $0 'NONE' warehouse appeared in the role detail). | USD | run_mart(mart27_sql.role_share -> FACT_QUERY_ROLE_HOURLY) / chargeback_sql.role_share_within_warehouse -> QUERY_HISTORY |
| **Department budgets & pace table** | Monthly budgets from DEPT_BUDGETS; the hourly scan raises COST_DEPT_BUDGET_PACE when a dept runs ahead. | Displayed as stored; operator upsert/delete MERGE on DEPT_BUDGETS. v4.606: a failed budgets (or department-map, or role-share) read renders needs-setup for a true absence, else unavailable with the error — it used to leave the section blank under its header. | USD/month | run(mart_sql.dept_budgets) -> DEPT_BUDGETS |
| **Tagged share (exec-time) / Top untagged user** | Execution-time-weighted query-tag coverage and the user with the most untagged execution time (caps chargeback precision). | Tagged share = (1 - UNTAGGED_EXEC_SEC/EXEC_SEC)*100; ok if untagged<30%. Top user = row 0 (ordered by UNTAGGED_EXEC_SEC desc); delta = humanize_duration(untagged,'s'). | % / seconds | run_mart(mart27_sql.tag_coverage_daily -> MART_TAG_COVERAGE_DAILY) / cost_sql.tag_coverage -> QUERY_HISTORY (routes live when db/schema filter set) |
| **Cortex spend, Nd (account-wide)** | Billed AI-service credits x AI rate, per-day stacked by service. | USD = CREDITS_BILLED * ai_rate; sum for KPI. | USD | run_mart(mart_sql.fact_cortex_daily_spend -> FACT_METERING_DAILY AI predicate) / cost_sql.cortex_daily_spend -> METERING_DAILY_HISTORY |
| **AI Functions usage (optional drill)** | Per-day AI Functions credits by source. | USD=TOTAL_CREDITS*ai_rate stacked by SOURCE. v4.606: a plain live read that clamps a trailing window to 90 days, so the caption / empty state name the window served ('live view, capped at 90d' past 90); needs-setup only when the view is not readable, any other failure unavailable with the error (it called every failure 'not available'). | USD | run(cortex_sql.cortex_ai_functions_daily) -> ACCOUNT_USAGE.CORTEX_AI_FUNCTIONS_USAGE_HISTORY |
| **Active AI users / Requests / Cortex Code spend / Projected 30d / AI monthly budget** | Per-user Cortex Code (Snowsight+CLI) attribution: exact token credits x AI rate, run-rate extended to 30 days over the days the scope was actually observable. | spend_usd=sum(exact credits)*ai_rate; projected_30d = spend/eff_days*30 where eff_days=effective_window_days(df,days), counted from the scope's earliest FIRST_USAGE (the MIN across users, not per user, so a brand-new user's first afternoon is not projected at 30x) to the window end, taken as its account (Central) calendar day and capped at the asked window (v4.606: a first use after ~7pm Central was dated the next UTC day, a day short). Budget = AI_MONTHLY_BUDGET_USD or 'Not configured'. | count / USD | run(mart27_sql.ai_code_user_rollup) -> FACT_AI_USAGE_DAILY / fallback cortex_sql.cortex_code_user_daily -> CORTEX_CODE_*_USAGE_HISTORY (365d, probe) |
| **Exceptions (SEVERITY / SIGNAL)** | Per-user budget-ladder breaches (on projected 30d) + a scope-total row + cost-per-request spikes (median/MAD z outliers). | classify_exceptions with BUDGET_LADDER thresholds; CPR spike = High when z>=CPR_SPIKE_Z with >=CPR_MIN_REQUESTS and >=CPR_MIN_PROJECTED_USD. 'Track top exceptions as work items' (operators, v4.605) writes the first 10 rows through the shared Track write (fix_queue.track_entities_sql, as Optimize and Control Room triage): one item per user (SOURCE_ENTITY_TYPE USER, key = USER_NAME, the strongest signal's severity, every signal in DETAIL, COMPANY = COALESCE(COMPANY_FOR_USER(user), 'UNKNOWN'): an unmapped user stays UNKNOWN, never ALL, so its estimate is not summed into every Company's queue; ESTIMATED_USD = the user's '(all sources)' budget row's projection, else the sum over the user's sources) plus one AI_BUDGET item for the all-users breach, keyed on the Company scope (no Entity 360 page), ESTIMATED_USD = the scope projection beyond the user items tracked in the same click (NULL when they count all of it); all-users items tracked from different Company views are each priced this way and overlap, so do not add them together. OWNER 'UNASSIGNED', PERIOD 'MONTHLY' when priced. Idempotent per user / scope and scoped to SOURCE 'Cost Intelligence > Chargeback & AI > AI users': an OPEN or IN_PROGRESS item of that source blocks, including a pre-v4.605 item (no entity key) matched by its title under that SOURCE or one of the page's earlier SOURCE names ('Cost & Contract > Chargeback & AI > AI users', 'Cost & Contract > AI Users'); a Security item on the same user does not. Before the inserts, fix_queue.ai_track_escalation_sql raises the SEVERITY of at most one such open item per user, and only when the user's strongest signal now outranks every open item of that user (never lowers it): the strongest item, on a tie the entity-keyed one, else the newest (CREATED_AT); a pre-v4.605 user's other per-signal items keep their severity, so one breach counts once in Critical / high, and a re-run is a no-op. It prefixes DETAIL with 'Raised from <old> to <new> by a later Track (<new title>)'; TITLE and ESTIMATED_USD stay as first tracked. | USD / count | logic.cortex classify_exceptions/with_aggregate_budget_row on the enriched rollup |
| **Fleet cache-hit / Users measured (token economics)** | Share of prompt context served from cache vs re-sent input tokens (efficiency lever). | fleet = cache_read/(cache_read+input) token-weighted; per-user CACHE_HIT_PCT (v4.606: a user with no prompt tokens — or, in the CoCo efficiency table, a credit user with no token-grain rows — has no cache-hit / cache-write % or read-amp: '—', never 0). | % | run(cortex_sql.cortex_code_token_types()) -> CORTEX_CODE_*_USAGE_HISTORY TOKENS_GRANULAR (probe; days-independent 365d read, window sliced in-app); logic.wave2.token_economics. A failed read: an absent view / unknown function, or a missing TOKENS_GRANULAR column (the kinds a probe read does not log) shows the 'TOKENS_GRANULAR isn't available' note; any other missing column is schema drift and renders unavailable (v4.605); any other failure (a timeout) renders unavailable with the error (v4.603). Canary: cortex.code_token_types |
| **Suggested per-user AI quotas (review only): Users with 10+ active days / p95 day above the daily cap / Runaway-rule days, 90d / Back-test: USD above the limits** | Review-only per-user AI quota suggestions (Next-Fifty #37b) from each user's OWN Cortex Code usage over a FIXED history: the last 90 complete days, whatever the page Window says. Renders under Per-user AI quotas & blocks after every block outcome (blocks; no block in the window or the view absent; a failed read). OVERWATCH creates no quota; one is set in Snowsight (Cost Management). The exposure caption above it (shown only when the read succeeded with no block in the window, or the block view is absent; never on a failed read) quotes the back-test total instead of claiming no quota exists. | quotas.user_day_totals sums SOURCE per user-day and drops ACCOUNT / UNKNOWN / 'UNKNOWN (id)' and zero days. SUGGESTED_DAILY_CREDITS = ceil(p95 of active-day credits); SUGGESTED_MONTHLY_CREDITS = ceil(p95 of zero-filled rolling 30-day totals); both '—' below 10 active days. Walk-forward back-test: each active day is judged against ceil(p95) of the user's active days in the 90 days BEFORE it (a day never sets its own limit; only days with 10+ prior active days are judged); USD_OVER = credits over x AI rate. RUNAWAY_DAYS replays the COST_AI_USER_RUNAWAY test day by day over the 90 days: credits > 2x COCO_DAILY_CAP_CREDITS (the rule's seed multiple) AND robust z >= AI_RUNAWAY_ROBUST_Z (3.5) against the user's prior 90 active days (fewer than 5 = no baseline, the cap alone decides). | credits / count / USD | quotas.recommend_quotas / runaway_days / quota_summary over the cached cortex_sql.cortex_code_user_daily frame the tab already holds (no new read; company-scoped); '—' with a "needs the live Cortex Code user-day scan" note on the fact-fallback leg. Parity with SP_ALERT_SCAN_DAILY arm [28] (V163) locked by tests/test_ai_runaway_parity.py |
| **Monthly statement export (zip)** | Per-department warehouse-compute statements for a month (finance-ready prior month). | USD=credits_to_usd(CREDITS_TOTAL,rate); grouped by DEPARTMENT/DEPT_OWNER; warehouse compute only (not full invoice). | USD | chargeback_sql.department_month_credits(month,company) -> FACT_WAREHOUSE_DAILY x DEPARTMENT_MAP, calendar month |

*Columns:* **DEPARTMENT / WAREHOUSE_NAME / COMPANY / CREDITS_TOTAL / USD (chargeback)** — Department, warehouse, company, exact credits, priced dollars; a reconciliation footer captions the additive department-row sum (C37).; **WAREHOUSE_NAME / ROLE_NAME / QUERY_COUNT / ELAPSED_SHARE / ALLOCATED_USD (role detail)** — Per-warehouse role share (0-1, can sum <1) and allocated dollars.; **USER_NAME / EXEC_SEC / UNTAGGED_EXEC_SEC / TAGGED_PCT / QUERIES (tag coverage)** — Per-user total/untagged execution seconds, tagged %, query count.; **USER_NAME / FIRST/LAST_NAME / EMAIL / SOURCE / ACTIVE_DAYS / TOTAL_REQUESTS / TOTAL_CREDITS / TOTAL_TOKENS / CREDITS_PER_REQUEST / SPEND_USD / PROJECTED_30D_USD / FIRST_USAGE / LAST_USAGE** — Cortex Code per-user attribution detail; SPEND_USD and PROJECTED_30D_USD in $, credits/request as ratio.; **SEVERITY / SIGNAL / USER_NAME / SOURCE / TOTAL_REQUESTS / CREDITS_PER_REQUEST / PROJECTED_30D_USD (exceptions)** — Exception rows: budget/spike severity, signal, user, source, requests, cr/request, projected monthly $.; **USER_NAME / DISPLAY_NAME / ACTIVE_DAYS / P95_DAILY_CREDITS / SUGGESTED_DAILY_CREDITS / SUGGESTED_MONTHLY_CREDITS / DAYS_OVER_CAP / BACKTEST_DAYS / BACKTEST_DAYS_OVER / BACKTEST_CREDITS_OVER / BACKTEST_USD_OVER / RUNAWAY_DAYS (suggested quotas)** — One row per user active in the last 90 complete days, highest p95 first: active days, own p95 day (2 dp), the suggested daily and monthly limits (whole credits), days strictly above the org daily cap, the walk-forward back-test (days judged, days over their limit, credits and USD above it) and runaway-rule days; recommendation and back-test cells are '—' below 10 active days.

### Unit costs
The measured price tag on one query, one procedure CALL, one AI request (QUERY_ATTRIBUTION_HISTORY, ~8h lag, idle excluded), plus ETL pipeline and task-graph unit costs. Measured reads clamp to <=30 days independent of the page window unless opted in; the opt-in scans at most the last 90 days (the live-scan limit) on a longer trailing window, and a calendar preset reads its exact range. v4.606: the measured panels' labels and sources name the window actually scanned ('last month' / 'current year' under a calendar preset, never a raw '31d' / '272d').

| Metric | Means | Formula | Unit | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Priciest single query** | Highest single-query measured attributed cost in the (clamped) window. | format_usd(credits_to_usd(top CREDITS, rate)); CREDITS = SUM(CREDITS_ATTRIBUTED_COMPUTE + QUERY_ACCELERATION). | USD | insights_sql.measured_query_costs(uc_days<=30, ...) -> QUERY_ATTRIBUTION_HISTORY + QUERY_HISTORY |
| **Priciest procedure (per call)** | Highest $/call across stored procedures (children roll up via ROOT_QUERY_ID). v4.606: the true per-call leader across EVERY procedure CALLed in the window, not only the 50 listed (a rare, heavy monthly batch ranked past #50 by total is named now). | credits_to_usd(PC_LEADER_CREDITS, rate, round_cents=False); PC_LEADER_NAME / PC_LEADER_CREDITS = FIRST_VALUE(...) OVER (ORDER BY SUM(att credits)/COUNT(calls) DESC, PROC_NAME), window columns evaluated before the LIMIT 50; CREDITS_PER_CALL = SUM(att credits)/COUNT(calls). A frame without those columns falls back to the priciest per call among the 50 listed, and the tile's help says which. | USD per call | insights_sql.procedure_costs_usd(uc_days,...) -> QUERY_ATTRIBUTION_HISTORY rolled to CALL |
| **AI spend (window)** | Account-wide billed Cortex credits x AI rate over the page window (a total, not a unit price). | credits_to_usd(sum(CREDITS), ai_rate). | USD | run(mart27_sql.ai_costs_by_model(days) -> FACT_AI_USAGE_DAILY) / fallback cortex_sql.cortex_model_costs -> CORTEX_FUNCTIONS_USAGE_HISTORY |
| **Most expensive queries — measured $ each (table)** | Top queries by exact attributed credits (idle excluded). | USD=credits_to_usd(CREDITS,rate) at $%.4f. | USD | measured_query_costs |
| **Stored procedures — top 50 by measured spend, with $/call** | Per-proc window total $ and $/call for the top 50 procedures by TOTAL measured spend (v4.606 title; the per-call leader over every procedure is the KPI above); click prefills the trend panel. | USD_TOTAL=credits_to_usd(TOTAL_CREDITS,rate); USD_PER_CALL=credits_to_usd(CREDITS_PER_CALL,rate,round_cents=False); FAIL_PCT from statuses. | USD / USD-per-call / % | procedure_costs_usd |
| **Repeated patterns — silent spend ($ and $/run)** | Measured attribution credits per parameterized hash — cheap-but-constant queries out-billing rare expensive ones. | USD=CREDITS*rate; USD_PER_RUN=CREDITS_PER_RUN*rate; $0.01 floor. | USD / USD-per-run | run(mart27_sql.pattern_cost(days,company,25)) -> MART_PATTERN_COST_DAILY (V037); a trailing window clamps to PATTERN_COST_MAX_DAYS (90): V120 re-stamped the corrected run counts over the last 90 days only, so older mart rows can overstate runs (and understate $/run) until a 365-day re-stamp has run; a calendar preset reads its exact range, so Current year still sums those older rows. v4.606: the panel caption names the window it reads (window_label of min(days, 90): 'last month' / 'current year' under a calendar preset) and, on a trailing window past 90 days, says it reads at most the last 90 days and why; a calendar window that starts before V120's re-stamp horizon (PATTERN_COST_RESTAMP_FROM, 2026-06-04: Current year) adds that rows before Jun 4, 2026 predate the V120 run-count fix and can overstate runs (understating the cost per run); the clean state names the same window; and on a trailing page window past 90 days (unit prices on their 30-day cap) the page's window note says the repeated-pattern panel reads at most the last 90 days from its mart, as the ETL and serverless-task panels scan at most the last 90 days. A failed read renders by its kind (v4.605): an absent mart is needs_setup (apply V037); any other failure renders unavailable with the error. |
| **Trend one procedure (Total / Calls / Avg $/call)** | Day-grain measured cost for a named procedure, over the same scan window as the leaderboard (labelled by window_label: 'last month' / 'current year' under a calendar preset). | USD=credits_to_usd(CREDITS,rate); Avg=Total/Calls. v4.606: the attribution read spans the same window as the CALL scan (the bounded calendar range under a calendar preset), so under Last month / Current year every call is credited — a trailing read used to leave most days at $0 and the total below its leaderboard row. | USD | insights_sql.proc_cost_trend(name, uc_days, ...) -> QUERY_ATTRIBUTION_HISTORY day grain |
| **Price a specific CALL/session + call tree** | Measured $ for one CALL or all procs in a session, with children rolled up and a within-CALL cost tree. | USD=credits_to_usd(CREDITS,rate); tree via build_call_tree on children. | USD | insights_sql.call_cost_lookup + call_children_costs -> QUERY_ATTRIBUTION_HISTORY ROOT_QUERY_ID rollup (7d) |
| **AI — $ by function/model and $/1M tokens** | Per function/model (or Cortex Code source) billed credits priced, with unit price per 1M tokens. | USD=credits_to_usd(CREDITS,ai_rate); USD_PER_1M_TOKENS=credits_to_usd(CREDITS_PER_1M_TOKENS,ai_rate). 'n/a' model = Cortex Code (no per-model grain). | USD / USD per 1M tokens | mart27_sql.ai_costs_by_model / cortex_sql.cortex_model_costs / cortex_source_costs |
| **ETL unit costs (Tagged credit coverage / Untagged credits / per-pipeline $/run,$/M rows,$/TiB,failed-run $)** | Measured attribution credits per JSON-tagged pipeline; coverage says how much spend is attributable. | USD*=credits_to_usd(CREDITS*,rate[,round_cents=False]); TAGGED_CREDIT_PCT = tagged/total measured credits. | % / USD / USD-per-run/-M-rows/-TiB | etl_sql.etl_tag_coverage + etl_cost_by_pipeline -> QUERY_HISTORY + QUERY_ATTRIBUTION_HISTORY |
| **Task-graph: Pipeline spend / Most expensive / Worst success** | Measured warehouse credits per task-run in each graph at the rate; $/run is day-cost over day's runs (allocated). | graphs.enrich_graph_daily(df,rate) + pipeline_summary; USD=credits_to_usd; SUCCESS_PCT from runs. Serverless tasks listed separately (SERVERLESS_CREDITS*rate; v4.606: newest day first with no builder cap below run()'s, so a cut drops the oldest days and is disclosed — a LIMIT 2000 kept the oldest days silently). | USD / % | run_mart(mart27_sql.task_graphs -> MART_TASK_GRAPH_DAILY) / graph_sql.graph_daily_costs -> TASK_HISTORY + QUERY_ATTRIBUTION_HISTORY; graph_sql.serverless_task_daily -> SERVERLESS_TASK_HISTORY |

*Columns:* **QUERY_ID / USER_NAME / WAREHOUSE_NAME / DATABASE/SCHEMA_NAME / QUERY_TYPE / EXECUTION_STATUS / START_TIME / ELAPSED_SEC / CREDITS / USD / QUERY_SNIPPET / PROFILE** — Measured query rows; CREDITS = compute+QAS attributed credits, USD = credits x rate; PROFILE links to Snowsight query profile.; **PROC_NAME / DATABASE/SCHEMA_NAME / CALLS / FAIL_PCT / P95_S / TOTAL_CREDITS / CREDITS_PER_CALL / ATTRIBUTED_CALLS / USD_TOTAL / USD_PER_CALL** — Per-proc measured cost; ATTRIBUTED_CALLS = calls the attribution view matched; $0 with calls = lag or no-warehouse children.; **SAMPLE_TEXT / RUNS / USD / USD_PER_RUN / USERS (patterns)** — Parameterized pattern sample, run count, measured total and per-run dollars, distinct users.; **PIPELINE / RUNS / USD / USD_PER_RUN / RUN_ID_CREDIT_PCT / USD_PER_M_ROWS / USD_PER_TIB / WASTE_USD (ETL)** — Per-pipeline measured cost, $/run (divides only run_id-tagged share), $/M rows written, $/TiB scanned, failed-run waste.; **PIPELINE / USD / USD_PER_RUN / SUCCESS_PCT (task graph summary)** — Per-pipeline window spend, allocated $/run, success rate; label = graph root task.

### Compare
Period-vs-period (A vs B) spreadsheet-killer from facts/marts only (no live Account Usage scans): warehouse spend, queries, fail rate, queued time, account billed, and the warehouses + query patterns that moved the bill. Scoped by company + dates only.

| Metric | Means | Formula | Unit | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Warehouse spend — A (delta vs B)** | Exact warehouse metering x rate for period A vs B, company-scopable. | a_usd=TOTAL_A_CREDITS*rate; b_usd=TOTAL_B_CREDITS*rate (the scope-wide totals carried on every row, not a sum of the top-100 movers frame; sum(A_CREDITS) / sum(B_CREDITS) only for an old-shape frame); delta via _delta_chip=pct_delta(a,b). v4.606: 'no B-side data' only when the B window has no fact rows (B_DAYS = 0); a loaded B of $0 reads '0 on both sides' or 'up from 0 vs B', and those non-comparison chips render neutral, not as a red rise. | USD | run_batch/run(mart27_sql.compare_warehouse_credits(a0,a1,b0,b1,company)) -> FACT_WAREHOUSE_DAILY |
| **Queries / Fail rate / Queued** | Company-scoped activity A vs B: query count, fail rate (fails/queries), queued time humanized. | a_rate=af/aq*100; delta in pts = a_rate-b_rate; queued via humanize_duration(QUEUED_SEC,'s'). v4.606: a side with no queries has no rate — when A has none the tile value reads '—' with the delta 'no A-side queries' (or 'no A-side data' when the A window has no fact rows); when only B has none the tile keeps A's rate and the delta reads 'no B-side queries' (or 'no B-side data' when the B window has no fact rows), with 'B = —' in the help; never a made-up 0.00% or a coloured delta against it; the Queries / Queued chips follow the warehouse card's B-side rule. A failed activity read says which KPIs (and the Volume shape table) are missing and why. | count / % / duration | mart27_sql.compare_activity -> FACT_QUERY_HOURLY |
| **Account billed** | Every-service account-wide billed spend A vs B (ignores company filter — metering has no company grain), AI priced at AI rate. | blended_billed_usd(CREDITS_BILLED_OTHER, CREDITS_BILLED_AI, rate, ai_rate) per side; fallback CREDITS_BILLED*rate. | USD | mart27_sql.compare_billed -> FACT_METERING_DAILY |
| **Warehouse movers (A_USD / B_USD / Δ$ / Δ%)** | Per-warehouse spend both sides, sorted by absolute dollar move; coverage warning when a side is under-loaded. | A_USD=A_CREDITS*rate; DELTA_USD=A_USD-B_USD; DELTA_PCT=pct_delta; _coverage_warning compares A_DAYS/B_DAYS to expected window length. | USD / % | compare_warehouse_credits (returns per-side A_DAYS/B_DAYS/A_MAX_DAY/B_MAX_DAY) |
| **Pattern movers (A_USD / B_USD / Δ$)** | Silent-spend delta per parameterized hash; new-in-A patterns show B=$0. | A_USD=A_CREDITS*rate; DELTA_USD=A_USD-B_USD. A failed read is needs-setup only when the mart is truly absent; any other failure renders unavailable with the error (v4.606; it used to say 'apply V037'). | USD | mart27_sql.compare_pattern_costs -> MART_PATTERN_COST_DAILY v2 (V037) |
| **Volume shape (Queries / Fails / Queued / Remote spill)** | A vs B volume metrics with % delta. | A/B are per-cell display strings: counts '{:,.0f}', Queued = humanize_duration(QUEUED_SEC,'s'), Remote spill = humanize_gb(SPILL_REMOTE_GB); DELTA_PCT = pct_delta (None when B=0), a numeric signed 'Δ %'. v4.606: a side with no fact rows shows '—' (not 0) and no Δ%; an empty activity read says so under the heading. | count / duration / bytes / % | compare_activity |

*Columns:* **WAREHOUSE_NAME / A_USD / B_USD / DELTA_USD / DELTA_PCT (movers)** — Warehouse and its A-period, B-period spend, dollar and % change; top 15.; **SAMPLE_TEXT / A_RUNS / B_RUNS / A_USD / B_USD / DELTA_USD (pattern movers)** — Pattern sample, run counts and measured dollars each side and delta.; **METRIC / A / B / DELTA_PCT (volume shape)** — Named volume metric with A, B values and % change.

### Optimization & Savings
Spend ceilings & resource-monitor coverage (above the sub-sections), idle/right-sizing advisors, capacity forecast, serverless QAS ROI, de-duplicated total addressable savings, fleet consolidation, expensive/measured queries, recurring patterns and repeat-query candidates, object-cost ledger, storage growth/waste/clustering, guarded remediation (generate->review->execute with REMEDIATION_LOG + SAVINGS_LEDGER), and the savings ledger with realization rate. Account-wide where labeled; db/schema partial on scans.

| Metric | Means | Formula | Unit | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Idle credit waste (Nd) / Idle share / Projected monthly (section headline)** | GROSS credits billed in warehouse-hours with zero queries, priced; the account-level auto-suspend opportunity. Same number as 'Idle spend' inside Idle & sizing. | idle_waste_summary: IDLE_USD=credits_to_usd(sum(IDLE_CREDITS),rate); IDLE_SHARE_PCT=idle_cr/total_cr*100; PROJECTED_MONTHLY=IDLE_USD/served_days*30; served_days = the days the read covers — v4.606: under a calendar preset the bounds' day span on either leg (the live fallback scans the whole period, so dividing a Current-year read by the 90-day clamp, or a Current-month read by its day offset, read ~2-3x high). warn if share>=20. | USD / % | run_mart(mart27_sql.eff_idle_analysis -> MART_WAREHOUSE_EFFICIENCY_DAILY) / insights_sql.idle_warehouse_analysis -> WAREHOUSE_METERING_HISTORY x QUERY_HISTORY |
| **Spend ceilings & resource monitors (Resource monitors / Uncapped warehouses / Uncapped recent spend)** | Which warehouses have no hard Snowflake credit ceiling, shown above the sub-sections (v4.542; read-only, metadata only, no usage-history scan). The monitor count and inventory are account-wide; the uncapped list is company-scoped. Not drawn when SHOW WAREHOUSES returns nothing. | monitors.resource_monitor_inventory: ENFORCED = the monitor has a SUSPEND or SUSPEND_IMMEDIATE trigger (False = notify-only; None = the trigger columns are absent). account_monitor = the LEVEL='ACCOUNT' monitor. monitors.unmonitored_warehouses: a warehouse is uncapped when it has no monitor attached, or its monitor is known to be notify-only; a monitor whose enforcement is unknown counts as a ceiling. The list is empty when an enforcing (or unknown-enforcement) account monitor exists; a notify-only account monitor does not empty it (a warning names it). Filtered by classify_warehouse under a company scope. Uncapped recent spend = SUM(the idle-headline TOTAL_CREDITS of the uncapped warehouses) x rate, a floor (a warehouse outside the idle frame contributes 0). Uncapped warehouses / Uncapped recent spend warn when above 0. | count / USD | security_sql.show_warehouses_sql + security_sql.show_resource_monitors_sql (SHOW, metadata tier, max_rows=0) + the idle-headline frame run_mart_first(mart27_sql.eff_idle_analysis / insights_sql.idle_warehouse_analysis); logic.monitors |
| **Idle spend / Projected monthly idle / Actionable via timer / Actionable warehouses** | Idle advisor: gross idle vs recoverable net after the ~IDLE_TARGET_SUSPEND_SEC resume/suspend tail, only where SHOW WAREHOUSES confirms an above-target/disabled timer. | idle_advisor(df,rate,served_days): IDLE_USD, PROJECTED_MONTHLY_IDLE_USD (gross/day*30), ACTIONABLE_MONTHLY_USD (net, settings-verified). Actionable = advisor[ACTIONABLE]. served_days as in the headline above (v4.606: the bounds' day span under a calendar preset). v4.606: a warehouse SHOW WAREHOUSES lists with a NULL auto_suspend never suspends — the known disabled setting (insights.show_auto_suspend reads it as 0), so it counts as actionable with an 'Enable AUTO_SUSPEND' fix; only a warehouse SHOW did not list is unknown (the daily idle alert follows in a later migration). | USD / count | eff_idle_analysis + SHOW WAREHOUSES via with_auto_suspend_settings |
| **Right-sizing simulator (Add a cluster / Size up / Size up or split / Tune suspend / Idle spend / Size-down / Potential saving range / Evidence review)** | Per-warehouse sizing profile: capacity-pressure verdicts split by kind (v4.601, #38), auto-suspend-first, size-down candidates and a bounded saving range. | size_recommendations(df,rate,served_days): overload queueing >= 30 min/day with remote spill under the per-day bar -> "Add a cluster (scale out)"; remote spill -> "Size up" (both -> size up first; the rationale's add-a-cluster follow-up is gated like the scale-out verdict (review r2): with the cluster-cap check run on a multi-cluster non-Economy warehouse it says to split when no query reached the cap and to add a cluster, with the hours, when one did; a multi-cluster non-Economy warehouse already at the generator's cap of 10 clusters (sizing.CLUSTER_RANGE_CAP) is told to split the workload across warehouses whatever the check shows (review r3); otherwise it carries the cap-check rule, sizing.CLUSTER_CAP_QUALIFIER); a long peak-day p95 is context only. Scale-out rows read MIN/MAX_CLUSTER_COUNT + SCALING_POLICY (SHOW WAREHOUSES): the operator pane prefills a review-only MAX_CLUSTER_COUNT + 1 (capped at 10) or SCALING_POLICY = STANDARD on an Economy multi-cluster warehouse, executed from Operations ▸ Emergency ▸ Cluster range (the MAX_CLUSTER_COUNT prefill) or ▸ Scaling policy (the STANDARD prefill); no statement (and no lever) when the range is unknown or already at the cap; no saving booked. potential_saving low=half measured idle, high=half whole bill; scenario x0.5/x2 = rate halve/double. sizing_summary aggregates (up = scale_out + size_up + below_cap; cap_unchecked = add-a-cluster rows on a multi-cluster non-Economy warehouse below the cap of 10 whose cap was not checked). Cluster-cap gate (v4.604, #38): the 'Check cluster use' toggle reads hourly MAX(QUERY_HISTORY.CLUSTER_NUMBER) per multi-cluster warehouse in the profile over sizing.cluster_check_days (max(35, sizing window), at most 90; any 35-day window holds a month-end), read from MIDNIGHT (account time) that many days back, so the sizing window's first day is in full. An empty SHOW WAREHOUSES, or one listing none of the profile's warehouses, reads 'the cluster ranges are unknown' (needs_setup) — never 'no warehouse has MAX_CLUSTER_COUNT above 1' — and a partly-listed profile names the warehouses SHOW WAREHOUSES did not list (sizing.cluster_range_unknown, the first 10 then 'and N more'; their cluster range is unknown and they are not checked). sizing.cluster_use_summary gives PEAK_CLUSTERS / P95_PEAK_CLUSTERS (nearest rank) / AT_CAP_HOUR_COUNT (hours whose peak >= the current MAX_CLUSTER_COUNT). A multi-cluster STANDARD row suggests and prefills MAX_CLUSTER_COUNT + 1 only when AT_CAP_HOUR_COUNT > 0. Checked and never at the cap -> 'Size up or split (cluster cap not reached)' (capacity pressure, $0, no cluster statement). Toggle off / read failed -> the rationale says the cap was not checked and nothing is prefilled. No scale-in (lower-the-cap) lever: with MIN_CLUSTER_COUNT = 1 an unreached cap costs nothing. The selected row's evidence labels Peak cluster / Hours at cap with the check window ('(last N days)') and carries CLUSTER_CHECK_DAYS (Cap check days), since the other evidence columns are the sizing window's. The operator's Resize to picker offers every size resize_fix accepts (XSMALL..XXLARGE) and, on a capacity-pressure verdict (add a cluster / size up / size up or split; sizing.resize_picker_default), opens one size up where the picker offers it. Where it does not (an XXLARGE warehouse, whose next size is not offered; one larger than every option, 3XLARGE and up, where every option is a downsize; or an unknown current size) it opens with no size picked and a note says why, in the picker's spelling (XXLARGE, never 2XLARGE): no statement, saving or Execute renders until the operator picks a size (review r2). The picker's widget key carries the current size and that default, so when the selected warehouse's size changes (after an Execute, or an outside resize) it re-opens on the new default rather than keeping an earlier pick, and a successful resize clears the typed confirm, so a repeat Execute needs the name typed again (review r3). Other verdicts open on XSMALL as before. The saving / upsize captions spell both sizes as the picker does (sizing.picker_size_label). Per-day and monthly figures divide by served_days (v4.606: the bounds' day span under a calendar preset, on either leg). | USD / count | run_mart(mart27_sql.eff_sizing_profile) / insights_sql.warehouse_sizing_profile -> WAREHOUSE_METERING_HISTORY x QUERY_HISTORY; insights.multi_cluster_evident (edition caveat); insights_sql.warehouse_cluster_use -> QUERY_HISTORY (toggle-gated, historical tier) |
| **Cluster use per multi-cluster warehouse (Active hours / Peak cluster / p95 hourly peak / Hours at cap / Cap)** | The evidence behind the cluster-cap gate (v4.604, #38): how many clusters each multi-cluster warehouse in the right-sizing profile actually used, judged against its current MAX_CLUSTER_COUNT. Shown only with 'Check cluster use' on. | Per warehouse and clock hour, the highest QUERY_HISTORY.CLUSTER_NUMBER any query ran on. The hour is the one a query STARTED in (DATE_TRUNC of START_TIME), so a long query counts only in its start hour and the hour counts are a floor; Reached / Not reached is unaffected. ACTIVE_HOUR_COUNT = hours with one; PEAK_CLUSTERS = the highest; P95_PEAK_CLUSTERS = nearest-rank p95 of the hourly peaks; AT_CAP_HOUR_COUNT = hours whose peak >= the current SHOW MAX_CLUSTER_COUNT (so a since-lowered cap still counts); Cap = Reached / Not reached / No queries. Window = sizing.cluster_check_days (>= 35 days, <= 90), from midnight (account time). No saving is booked either way; lowering an unreached cap is not offered ($0 with MIN_CLUSTER_COUNT = 1). | count (hours / cluster number) | insights_sql.warehouse_cluster_use -> ACCOUNT_USAGE.QUERY_HISTORY (live, historical tier, <= 100 warehouses) + SHOW WAREHOUSES; logic.sizing.cluster_use_summary |
| **Interactive what-if (Now vs Scenario range)** | Replays observed credits under a size step + new auto-suspend as a bounded monthly range. | simulate_scenario(size, credits_window, idle_credits_window, window_days, rate, size_delta, autosuspend now/new); idle_wi=CREDITS_TOTAL*IDLE_PCT/100. | USD/month | logic.sizing.simulate_scenario; live SHOW WAREHOUSES optional |
| **Capacity pressure forecast (Pressure now / Forecast <=180d / Watch / Insufficient)** | Recurring warehouse pressure forecast with holdout validation; ETA only for real forecasts. | capacity_forecasts(df): CURRENT_PRESSURE_INDEX=max(7d median queue-min/30, remote-spill-GB/1); STATUS counts. Complete days only, 90d window. | index / count | run(mart_sql.warehouse_capacity_daily(90,company)) -> FACT_QUERY_HOURLY + FACT_WAREHOUSE_DAILY + WAREHOUSE_CHANGE_REGISTRY |
| **Serverless QAS ROI (QAS spend / Drop / Enable candidates)** | QAS credits spent vs queries eligible for acceleration; eligibility is utilization not a dollarized saving. | QAS_USD=QAS_CREDITS*rate; classify_qas_roi(usd, eligible) -> drop/enable/keep verdicts: paying (>= the spend floor, $5) with little eligible workload -> 'Paying, little benefit' (drop); eligible with no QAS spend -> 'Eligible, QAS off' (enable); v4.606: eligible with some QAS spend under the floor -> 'QAS on, low spend' (keep — any QAS credit proves QAS is on, so it is never an Enable candidate); paying and eligible -> 'Working' (keep). | USD / count | run(cost_sql.qas_roi(days,company)) -> QUERY_ACCELERATION_ELIGIBLE x QUERY_ACCELERATION_HISTORY |
| **Total addressable savings (Addressable $/mo net / Opportunities / Overlaps removed)** | De-duplicated idle-timer + right-sizing opportunities (same warehouse counted once, larger wins), plus unread maintenance (v4.604, #35) only when the Storage & waste unread-maintenance scan CONFIRMED it against access history in this session: the same Company, the Database filter clear, the same cache scope (no Refresh or global-salt write since), priced at the credit rate in force (a rate change since the scan drops it until the scan is re-run) and at most 1h since that panel was last shown (real time: the stamp and the check are aware UTC, so a DST change neither stretches nor shortens it, and a stamp over 60s in the future is stale); objects already booked on the Savings ledger as of the scan's ledger read are left out; that read is on the 'recent' tier, cached for up to 5m (savings_rollup.BOOKED_LEDGER_CACHE_SEC = CACHE_TTLS['recent']) and blind to another session's booking, so an object booked in another session keeps counting until the scan is re-run at least 5m after that booking, at most 1h 5m after it (review r2; the KPI help, savings_rollup.H_BOOKED, and the Storage & waste line, N_ELSEWHERE, say so). A 'Levers counted: … Not counted: …' caption under the KPIs names every lever and why one is missing (idle advisor empty or unreadable; right-sizing toggle off or empty; unread scan not run this session, another Company, Database-scoped, stale, credit rate changed, shortlist / access-history / Savings-ledger read failed), with 'a floor' when only the top-N shortlisted objects were checked. An object stopped in a worksheet without booking keeps counting until its last 30 days of maintenance roll off. Storage waste (v4.605, the #35 storage leg) joins the same way from the Storage & waste storage-waste scan: tables the scan's own read evidence shows nobody read in 90 days, at least 90 days old, not in a shared-out database, priced at STORAGE_USD_PER_TB_MONTH (Archive or drop = a stale table's active bytes, never a stale table that shares storage with a clone, which is 'Check clones' and unpriced; Cut retention = the Time Travel a 1-day retention would release on a still-written table, a monthly run-rate only while it keeps being written and assuming no account-level MIN_DATA_RETENTION_TIME_IN_DAYS above 1 day, which is not checked); the same Company, cache-scope and 1h rules (the Database filter never narrows that scan, so it is never Database-scoped), and a storage-rate change drops its rows until the scan is re-run; tables with a live Savings-ledger booking under RETENTION or an unread-maintenance finding type are left out (the same 5m-cached ledger read and the same other-session disclosure); not counted when the scan has no read evidence (only the DML-only fallback answered), could not be read, or the Savings-ledger read failed or was row-capped; 'a floor' when the top-50 frame is full (a table with no DML for 90 days has little Time Travel or fail-safe left, so most stale tables rank outside it). Fail-safe, clone-retained bytes, a stale table's remaining Time Travel and retention on tables someone reads never count. One saving per table: CLUSTERING, UNREAD_MAINT, STORAGE and RETENTION on the same table (unread_maintenance.object_key, so a quoted or lower-case twin merges) count once, the larger wins, and 'Overlaps removed' counts those too. A table dropped or re-set in a worksheet keeps counting until the scan is re-run after account usage shows the change. | rollup_savings(SavingsOpportunity list); each opportunity weighted by confidence_weight; effort_tier flags LOW quick ALTER-level wins (UNREAD_MAINT is LOW, MEDIUM 0.6 confidence; STORAGE and RETENTION are MEDIUM effort, MEDIUM 0.6 confidence). Overlap groups: {IDLE, RESIZE} on the raw warehouse name; {CLUSTERING, UNREAD_MAINT, STORAGE, RETENTION} on object_key(target) (the kept item keeps its raw target). The unread leg reaches the list ONLY through savings_rollup.unread_handoff (called in Storage & waste after the confirm) -> session key _ow_unread_maint_handoff (primitives, never cleared on toggle-off) -> savings_rollup.unread_lever; the storage leg ONLY through savings_rollup.storage_handoff (called in Storage & waste after the scan; rows [source, target, usd, confidence]) -> session key _ow_storage_waste_handoff -> savings_rollup.storage_lever; Idle & sizing makes no read for either. | USD/month / count | in-app rollup of idle_advisor ACTIONABLE_MONTHLY_USD + sizing POTENTIAL_MONTHLY_SAVING_USD + the handoff's confirmed-unread EST_MONTHLY_USD + the storage handoff's EST_MONTHLY_USD (storage_waste.storage_waste_verdicts); OPTIMIZATION_EXPERIMENTS for under-test flag |
| **Fleet consolidation candidates (count / Est. addressable $/mo)** | Same-size, same-company warehouses with barely-overlapping active hours that could merge; saving = retired warehouse's monthly idle tail. v4.606: never proposes merging warehouses of different companies (under the ALL scope the table adds a Company column). | consolidation_candidates(WarehouseProfile list built from hour-of-day activity + _size_by_wh + _idle_by_wh); owner = the warehouse's OWN company (the idle frame's COMPANY label; a warehouse the idle read did not return keeps the page scope), and the module pairs only same-owner warehouses; est = sum(retired idle tails). | USD/month / count | run(insights_sql.warehouse_hourly_activity(days,company)) -> WAREHOUSE_METERING_HISTORY x FACT_QUERY_HOURLY |
| **Most expensive queries (allocated $)** | Each warehouse-hour's credits spread across that hour's queries by execution-time share (idle-only hours excluded). | ALLOCATED_USD=round(ALLOCATED_CREDITS*rate,2); top-50 total + max. | USD | run(insights_sql.expensive_queries_usd(days,company,50,...)) -> QUERY_HISTORY x WAREHOUSE_METERING_HISTORY |
| **Most expensive queries (measured $, exact attribution)** | Exact per-query attributed credits (idle excluded) — complement of the allocated panel; reads below it. | MEASURED_USD=round(CREDITS*rate,2). | USD | run(insights_sql.measured_query_costs(days,...)) -> QUERY_ATTRIBUTION_HISTORY x QUERY_HISTORY |
| **Recurring cost patterns ($/day, $ Nd) + Price a pattern** | Hour-share-allocated credits by parameterized fingerprint; price-a-pattern gives observed $/run and size-step bounds. | USD_TOTAL=round(ALLOCATED_CREDITS*rate,2); USD_PER_DAY=CREDITS_PER_DAY*rate; price_per_run_bounds(credits,runs,rate,delta). Window = bounded_days (served). | USD | run(insights_sql.expensive_patterns_usd(days,company,30)) -> QUERY_HISTORY x METERING by hash |
| **Repeat-query candidates (Repeated fingerprints / Materialization candidates / Compute in repeats / Avoidable $/30d)** | Fingerprints rerun enough to justify cache/materialization, ranked by normalized avoidable cost x cache-miss share. | flag_repeat_candidates(df, served_days); candidate = >=10 runs & >=0.5h/30d & <=25% cache; AVOIDABLE_USD_PER_30D=AVOIDABLE_CREDITS_PER_30D*rate. v4.606: under a calendar preset the min-runs prefilter and every per-30d rate normalize by the bounds' day span (Current year scans ~273 days, not 90 — the rates read ~3x high before); the three tiles count the whole window from pre-LIMIT columns (FINGERPRINTS_WIN, CANDIDATES_WIN via the SQL twin of the gate, ELAPSED_HOURS_WIN), not the top 100 rows; candidates rank first, and a caption says when the table lists the top 100 of N. | count / hours / USD | insights_sql.repeat_query_fingerprints -> QUERY_HISTORY (live only, toggle-gated; LIMIT 100) |

A failed read renders by its kind (v4.605): an absent ledger is needs_setup (apply V048); any other failure renders unavailable with the error. |
| **Maintenance on objects nobody reads (Storage & waste, v4.601 #30)** | Toggle-gated: objects paying clustering, search-optimization or MV-refresh credits with no reads in a fixed trailing 90 days (the window picker does not narrow it). A mart shortlist, then an access-history confirm, then one verdict per object: Suspend clustering / Drop search optimization / Suspend MV refresh (action), Check share consumers, No recent spend, Object gone (dropped or renamed: no SQL, not in the totals), Unconfirmed, Keep. | Shortlist: FACT_OBJECT_COST_DAILY grouped on UPPER(REPLACE(OBJECT_FQN,'"','')) (a quoted read twin merges), maintenance arms >= 1 credit, READ_CREDITS = 0 (legacy QUERY_COMPUTE counts as a read), today excluded, top 50; CANDIDATES_WIN / MAINT_CREDITS_WIN are pre-LIMIT window totals; COVERAGE_START_DAY / LEDGER_LAST_DAY are ledger-wide (window only, never the company / Database scope), so a quiet database never reads as a failing load. Confirm: ACCESS_HISTORY BASE_OBJECTS_ACCESSED + OBJECTS_MODIFIED, id (TABLES bridge) UNION name match, write-wins MAX(IS_WRITE), share guard (GRANTS_TO_ROLES GRANTED_TO='SHARE'). EST_MONTHLY_USD = last 30 complete days of maintenance credits x rate (ESTIMATED); a failed confirm = every row Unconfirmed, no SQL, with the reason by error kind (only an object-not-visible error names Enterprise edition + the SNOWFLAKE grant; a timeout says it timed out), latched per SQL until 'Retry the access-history check' or Refresh so the scan does not re-run on every rerun. MATCHED_BY_ID false (no live TABLES row under the name) = Object gone. Review-only ALTER TABLE / ALTER MATERIALIZED VIEW (plain upper-case three-part names only) with the reverse (for DROP SEARCH OPTIMIZATION: capture DESCRIBE SEARCH OPTIMIZATION first and re-add each method; a bare ADD re-adds table-wide equality only); one-click operator 'Book estimated saving' = an ESTIMATED SAVINGS_LEDGER INSERT (FINDING_TYPE SUSPEND_RECLUSTER / DROP_SEARCH_OPTIMIZATION / SUSPEND_MV_REFRESH) that books nothing while the object has any of those rows not REJECTED, with a Central-pinned PROOF_SQL (maintenance credits AFTER the booking day through the ledger's newest loaded day; DAYS_MEASURED, MONTHLY_CREDITS_NOW NULL until one post-booking day has loaded); no scan settles these rows. Registered in savings_rollup as UNREAD_MAINT (overlaps CLUSTERING). v4.604 (#35): after the confirm the panel publishes a session handoff (savings_rollup.unread_handoff: the action verdicts' EST_MONTHLY_USD, less objects already booked, stamped with the credit rate they were priced at and an aware UTC time from formulas.utc_now) that Idle & sizing and Proof ▸ Pipeline count in Addressable $/mo; one caption says whether they joined (or why not: Database-scoped, no object confirmed, all already booked), when they drop out (Refresh, a credit-rate change, or 1h after the panel was last shown), and that an object booked in another session keeps counting until the scan is re-run at least 5m after that booking (the ledger read below is cached for up to 5m), at most 1h 5m after it. Booked objects come from ONE toggle-gated read of mart_sql.savings_ledger(limit=None) (Proof's full-ledger SQL + 'recent' tier, one shared cache entry up to 5m old; key booked_unread_ledger), made only with a confirmed action row and no Database filter: unread_maintenance.booked_objects = object_key(TARGET_OBJECT) where UPPER(TRIM(FINDING_TYPE)) is an unread-maintenance type and STATE is not REJECTED (the Book button's own dedupe); a failed or row-capped read renders 'unavailable' and keeps the lever out; a booking in the same run leaves its object out at once. | USD (ESTIMATED) | run(cost_sql.maintenance_on_unread) -> FACT_OBJECT_COST_DAILY; run(insights_sql.object_reads_confirm) -> ACCESS_HISTORY + TABLES + GRANTS_TO_ROLES (Enterprise; not a canary); proof = cost_sql.unread_maintenance_proof |
| **Storage growth movers (Current storage / Growth / Projected growth $/mo confident gainers)** | Least-squares storage growth slope per database, gainers-only projected to $/mo. | storage_movers(df, STORAGE_USD_PER_TB_MONTH[23]); growing = GROWTH_USD_30D>0 & PROJECTABLE (>=7 days); low-confidence flagged. v4.606: the window is bounded_days(max(days,30)) — at most 90 days, and the Growth tile label, cache key and caption name it ('Live scan capped at 90d' on a longer pick); Current storage / Growth sum every database in scope from pre-LIMIT columns (CURRENT_BYTES_WIN, GROWTH_BYTES_WIN), not the top 100 growers; the Database filter narrows in SQL. | TB / USD/mo | run(insights_sql.storage_growth_by_database(bounded_days(max(days,30)), company, database)) -> DATABASE_STORAGE_USAGE_HISTORY |
| **Query efficiency (pruning families + zero-scan answers %)** | Full-table-scan query families and the result-cache/metadata hit share trend. | poor_pruning_queries (>80% of 100+-partition tables); HIT_PCT = successful queries with BYTES_SCANNED=0 share. | % / count | ops_sql.poor_pruning_queries + ops_sql.result_cache_daily -> QUERY_HISTORY |
| **Storage waste (Tables / Stale / Stale retention GB / Stale AND never read / Reclaimable $/mo + Object TCO)** | Top tables by retention bytes, STALE (no DML 90d), never-read shortlist, and per-object storage/read/write TCO; generates a review-only retention ALTER for a worksheet (the executor's allow-list refuses ALTER TABLE, so OVERWATCH never runs it and books nothing: since the 2026-09-30 review there is no Execute button, which could only log a FAILED row). v4.605 (#35 storage leg): on the read-evidence scan each table gets a LEVER and an ESTIMATED EST_MONTHLY_USD (same rows, same order), the 'Reclaimable $/mo (unread tables)' KPI sums the Archive or drop and Cut retention rows ('top 50, ≥' and a floor legend when the frame is full), and a legend says what each LEVER means (whether a table qualifies; booked tables are then left out) and what never counts (fail-safe, clone-retained bytes, a stale table sharing storage with a clone), that Cut retention is a run-rate only while the table keeps being written, and that it assumes no account-level MIN_DATA_RETENTION_TIME_IN_DAYS above 1 day (not checked); the panel publishes the session handoff that Idle & sizing and Proof ▸ Pipeline count in Addressable $/mo, with one line saying whether its tables joined (or why not: no read evidence, none qualify, all already booked) and when they drop out; a failed or row-capped Savings-ledger read renders 'unavailable' and keeps them out; a table re-set or dropped in a worksheet keeps counting until the scan is re-run after account usage shows the change. Reads count in ANY access-history object domain (the #30 confirm's rule, the safe direction: a materialized view or dynamic table read no longer leaves its table never-read, so the 'Stale AND never read' count can only fall), a share guard flags tables in databases granted to a share, OLDER_THAN_90D flags tables at least 90 days old, and CLONE_GROUP_LIVE counts the live tables in each row's clone group (account-wide, before the Company scope and the LIMIT). The degraded (DML-only) caption says why read evidence is missing by the error kind (a timeout says it timed out; a not-visible error says ACCESS_HISTORY or GRANTS_TO_ROLES (the share guard), or another ACCOUNT_USAGE view the scan reads, is not visible, with the SNOWFLAKE grant: run() keeps no object name, so it cannot say which; an 'Insufficient privileges' error shows the error itself; only ACCESS_HISTORY and an edition / unsupported-feature error name Enterprise edition). | Stale retention GB=sum(TIME_TRAVEL_GB+FAILSAFE_GB) on stale; freed_gb=TT_GB*(cur_ret-keep)/cur_ret; est=freed_gb/1024*rate_tb. LEVER (storage_waste.storage_waste_verdicts, first match wins): Object gone (RETENTION_KNOWN false) > Unconfirmed (no read evidence) > Keep (read in 90d) > Check share consumers (SHARED_DATABASE, unknown = shared) > Newer than 90 days (OLDER_THAN_90D false) > Check clones (STALE and ACTIVE_GB > 0 but CLONE_GROUP_LIVE > 1, unknown, or CLONE_RETAINED_GB > 0: unpriced) > Archive or drop (STALE and ACTIVE_GB > 0: ACTIVE_GB/1024 x rate_tb) > Cut retention (ACTIVE, RETENTION_DAYS > 1 and TIME_TRAVEL_GB > 0: TT_GB x (R - 1)/R/1024 x rate_tb, the retention control's formula) > Nothing to reclaim (not a lever: a written table, which is not a drop candidate, already at 1 day or less of retention or with no Time Travel, or a stale table with no active bytes); rate_tb = STORAGE_USD_PER_TB_MONTH (default $23/TiB). | count / GB / USD | insights_sql.storage_reclaim (ACCESS_HISTORY any domain + GRANTS_TO_ROLES share guard + TABLE_STORAGE_METRICS.TABLE_CREATED + a live clone-group count over TABLE_STORAGE_METRICS; one statement, LIMIT 50) / storage_waste (DML-only); insights_sql.table_tco (Object TCO, 30d ACCESS_HISTORY; v4.606: matched on the quote-stripped, upper-cased objectName, so a quoted mixed-case or quote-requiring table name finds its reads and writes; a failed read shows '—' for Reads / Writes, says unavailable or needs-setup, and never fires the 'never read' warning); booked tables: mart_sql.savings_ledger(limit=None) (key booked_storage_ledger, 'recent' tier, read only when a table qualifies) -> unread_maintenance.booked_objects(finding_types=storage_waste.STORAGE_BOOKED_TYPES) |
| **Automatic clustering spend (per table)** | Serverless reclustering credits per table over the window. | Rendered as-is; $=credits x compute rate (noted in caption). | credits | run(insights_sql.clustering_by_table(max(days,30),company)) -> AUTOMATIC_CLUSTERING_HISTORY |
| **Guarded remediation (auto-suspend / schedule) + est monthly** | Turns idle findings into a reviewed fix. Auto-suspend: a type-the-warehouse-name execute that logs REMEDIATION_LOG. Its tighten-only guard (remediation.tighten_suspend_plan, shared with the alert drawer) reads the picked warehouse's AUTO_SUSPEND from ONE live SHOW WAREHOUSES row (recheck_sql.warehouse_settings_sql, 30-second tier), never the 4 h cached list, so a timer tightened in a worksheet since is not raised; a failed read leaves it unknown (no ALTER), and a NULL on the listed row is the known never-suspend 0, which gets the enable-a-timer ALTER. The daily change scan books and settles a downward timer change (V038/V145), and the app books an ESTIMATED SAVINGS_LEDGER item only for a change the scan cannot book (enabling a never-suspend timer). Off-hours schedule (v4.606): a review-only multi-statement task script OVERWATCH never runs (CREATE TASK is outside the executor's allow-list, so the old Execute always failed) — run it in a worksheet, then an operator's one-click 'Book estimated saving' adds the ESTIMATED ledger row (once per warehouse unless the earlier one was REJECTED). | auto-suspend est = advisor ACTIONABLE_MONTHLY_USD; schedule est = avg_credits_per_day*30*rate*5/7 (weekdays). Target = min(current,60s). | USD/month | idle_advisor + remediation.auto_suspend_fix/suspend_schedule; insights_sql.warehouse_hourly_activity for quiet window |
| **Savings ledger (Verified / Estimated / Realization rate)** | Booked savings: measured verified (the number to quote), unverified estimated, and the realization rate calibrating estimates. | ledger_totals: verified_usd/estimated_usd sums; realization_pct = verified_usd/verified_estimated_usd*100. Verified never mixes with estimated. | USD / % / count | run(mart_sql.savings_ledger) -> SAVINGS_LEDGER; savings_verification_runs -> SAVINGS_VERIFICATION_RUNS (auto verify via TASK_VERIFY_SAVINGS) |
| **Verify an estimated item: measured before/after (Before / day, After / day, Measured saving / mo, Volume ×)** | For a manual item whose FINDING_TYPE the marts can measure (v4.601, #46(d)), the verify form shows a before/after and prefills the monthly figure (never overwriting an edit; re-armed when the operator leaves and returns; frozen in the Verify click's own rerun, so the UPDATE writes exactly what was shown); a measured verify stamps PROOF_RESULT / PROOF_RUN_AT / PROOF_QUERY_ID. Not prefilled when another booked change on the same target falls inside the measured window (named in a warning; split the change by hand). | ledger_measure.ledger_basis: SCHEDULE / AUTO_SUSPEND / STATEMENT_TIMEOUT / RESIZE / MAX_CLUSTERS -> WAREHOUSE (credits), RETENTION -> TABLE (time-travel bytes), SUSPEND_RECLUSTER / DROP_SEARCH_OPTIMIZATION / SUSPEND_MV_REFRESH -> OBJECT (maintenance credits). Credits: (before/14 - after/after_days) x 30 x rate, before = the 14 days before the booking day, after = up to 30 complete days after it, ending at min(mart loaded-through, yesterday, booking + 30); fewer than 7 after-days = Too early; the booking day is skipped. Table: (last snapshot before - latest after) TT bytes / 1024^4 x STORAGE_USD_PER_TB_MONTH (the storage mart keeps ~14 days, so older bookings read No data). Volume × = queries/day after vs before, outside 0.7-1.3x = volume-confounded (disclosed, not adjusted). Prefill = max(0, monthly), withheld when another non-rejected, non-superseded ledger row on the same TARGET_OBJECT (a change-scan row: its CHANGE_WAREHOUSE) was booked between booking - 14 days and the after-window end (ledger_measure.ledger_overlaps over the savings-ledger page; a full 500-row page whose oldest row is inside the window cannot rule one out); PROOF_RESULT then lists them as overlapping_items. | USD / credits / bytes | mart_sql.ledger_before_after -> FACT_WAREHOUSE_DAILY + MART_WAREHOUSE_EFFICIENCY_DAILY / FACT_OBJECT_COST_DAILY / MART_TABLE_STORAGE_DAILY; logic.ledger_measure; PROOF_QUERY_ID = the read's query id (QueryResult.query_id, cache miss) |
