*Columns:* **Monitor inventory (spend ceilings)** — Monitor, Level, Resets, Quota (cr), Used (cr), Remaining (cr), Used % (%.0f%%), Hard ceiling (Yes / Notify-only / Unknown), Triggers; sorted by used % desc; account-wide.; **Uncapped warehouses (spend ceilings)** — Warehouse, Size, Ceiling ('none' / 'notify-only (<monitor>)'), Recent credits, Recent spend ($%.0f); sorted by recent spend desc.; **WAREHOUSE_NAME / RECOMMENDATION / ACTIONABLE_MONTHLY_USD / SAVINGS_CONFIDENCE / ACTION_STATUS (idle advisor)** — Warehouse, recommendation text, net actionable $/mo, confidence, current action state; selecting shows full evidence row.; **WAREHOUSE_NAME / RECOMMENDATION / RATIONALE / CONFIDENCE / MONTHLY_USD_NOW / IDLE_MONTHLY_USD (+ scenario/queue/spill/p95 on select)** — Sizing recommendation with now-cost, of-which-idle, and (on select) x0.5/x2 scenarios, queued min/day, spill GB/day, P95, timer.; **Source / Warehouse-target / $/mo / Confidence / Effort / Experiment (addressable savings)** — Opportunity source (IDLE/RESIZE/UNREAD_MAINT/STORAGE/RETENTION), target (a warehouse, or an object / table FQN for UNREAD_MAINT, STORAGE and RETENTION), monthly $, confidence weight, effort tier, and any under-test experiment (warehouses only).; **Keep / Retire / Size / Shared active hours / Est. $/mo (consolidation)** — Warehouse pair to merge, size class, overlapping active hours, conservative saving.; **ALLOCATED_USD / USER / WAREHOUSE_NAME / QUERY_TYPE / EXECUTION_STATUS / ELAPSED_SEC / START_TIME / QUERY_SNIPPET / QUERY_ID / PROFILE (allocated queries)** — Allocated per-query dollars and context; PROFILE deep-links Snowsight.; **MEASURED_USD / ... (measured queries)** — Same shape as allocated but exact QUERY_ATTRIBUTION_HISTORY credits, idle excluded.; **USD_PER_DAY / USD_TOTAL / RUNS / USERS / WAREHOUSES / QUERY_SNIPPET / PATTERN_HASH (recurring patterns)** — Per-fingerprint daily and window allocated dollars, run/user/warehouse spread.; **AVOIDABLE_USD_PER_30D / RUNS / RUNS_PER_30D / USERS / TOTAL_ELAPSED_HOURS / AVG_ELAPSED_SEC / TOTAL_TB_SCANNED / AVG_CACHE_PCT / LAST_RUN / CANDIDATE / WHY / QUERY_PREVIEW (repeat candidates)** — Normalized avoidable cost, recurrence, compute, cache %, staleness, candidacy and the engine's reason.; **OBJECT_FQN / OBJECT_DOMAIN / COMPANY / QUERY_USD / MAINT_USD / USD (object ledger top)** — Object, domain, and its query-compute vs maintenance dollars.; **DATABASE_NAME / COMPANY / CURRENT_TB / GROWTH_TB / GROWTH_TB_30D / GROWTH_USD_30D / FAILSAFE_SHARE_PCT / LOW_CONFIDENCE (storage movers)** — Per-database current/growth TB, projected growth $/mo, failsafe share, confidence flag.; **Storage waste table (DATABASE/SCHEMA/TABLE_NAME / LEVER / EST_MONTHLY_USD / ACTIVE_GB / TIME_TRAVEL_GB / FAILSAFE_GB / STATUS / NEVER_READ / SHARED_DATABASE / OLDER_THAN_90D / CLONE_GROUP_LIVE / RETENTION_DAYS)** — Per-table active/retention bytes, staleness, never-read flag, current retention for the reduction control; on the read-evidence scan, the table's LEVER (Archive or drop / Cut retention count in Addressable $/mo) with its ESTIMATED $/mo, whether its database is shared out, whether it is at least 90 days old, and how many live tables share its clone group (more than 1 makes a stale table 'Check clones').; **CREATED_AT / SOURCE / DESCRIPTION / STATE / ESTIMATED_USD / VERIFIED_USD / VERIFIED_BY (ledger)** — Savings-ledger rows; STATE ESTIMATED vs VERIFIED, estimated vs measured dollars.

## Operations
*8 sections · 70 metrics.*

### Queries
Query-health triage for the window: volume/fail-rate/latency/queue/spill KPIs, the heaviest queries, a single-query drill-through, failure taxonomy, wasted spend on failed runs, and Snowflake-authored query insights. Answers 'is the account's query traffic healthy and what is the worst offender right now?'

| Metric | Means | Formula | Unit | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Queries (Nd)** | Total queries executed in the selected window, scoped to warehouses owned by the selected company. | COUNT(*) over QUERY_HISTORY in window (live); or SUM(QUERY_COUNT) of FACT_QUERY_HOURLY (mart). Displayed f'{qcount:,.0f}'. Sparkline = daily SUM(QUERY_COUNT) over a FIXED 14 days (fact_daily_activity), not the KPI window. | count | mart: mart_sql.fact_query_window_summary -> FACT_QUERY_HOURLY.QUERY_COUNT; live fallback: ops_sql.query_window_summary -> ACCOUNT_USAGE.QUERY_HISTORY COUNT(*); schema-filter path: mart27_sql.schema_window_summary -> FACT_QUERY_SCHEMA_HOURLY.QUERIES |
| **Fail rate** | Share of window queries whose EXECUTION_STATUS was not SUCCESS. Tile turns 'warn' only above the 2% materiality threshold (rec#49). | fail_pct = FAILED_COUNT / QUERY_COUNT * 100 where FAILED_COUNT = SUM(IFF(EXECUTION_STATUS<>'SUCCESS',1,0)). delta shows f'{failed:,.0f} failed'. 'n/a' when qcount=0. | percent | mart FACT_QUERY_HOURLY.FAILED_COUNT / QUERY_COUNT; live QUERY_HISTORY. Sparkline FAILS from fact_daily_activity (14d daily) |
| **p95 runtime (mart adds '(peak hourly)')** | 95th-percentile query elapsed time. On the mart path this is the HIGHEST hourly-group p95 (a peak), not a true whole-window p95 — labeled and help-noted as such. | live: APPROX_PERCENTILE(TOTAL_ELAPSED_TIME/1000, 0.95); mart: MAX(P95_ELAPSED_SEC) across hourly rows. Displayed via humanize_duration(v,'s'). | seconds (humanized to H/M/S) | mart FACT_QUERY_HOURLY.P95_ELAPSED_SEC (MAX); live QUERY_HISTORY.TOTAL_ELAPSED_TIME |
| **Queued** | Total time queries spent queued (overload + provisioning) in the window. | SUM(COALESCE(QUEUED_OVERLOAD_TIME,0)+COALESCE(QUEUED_PROVISIONING_TIME,0))/1000 (live); SUM(QUEUED_SEC_SUM) (mart). humanize_duration(v,'s'). | seconds (humanized) | live QUERY_HISTORY.QUEUED_OVERLOAD_TIME+QUEUED_PROVISIONING_TIME; mart FACT_QUERY_HOURLY.QUEUED_SEC_SUM |
| **Remote spill** | Total bytes spilled to remote storage across window queries (memory-pressure signal). | SUM(COALESCE(BYTES_SPILLED_TO_REMOTE_STORAGE,0))/1024^3 (live); SUM(SPILL_REMOTE_GB) (mart). humanize_gb() auto-scales GiB->MB/GB/TB. | GiB in -> humanized bytes | live QUERY_HISTORY.BYTES_SPILLED_TO_REMOTE_STORAGE; mart FACT_QUERY_HOURLY.SPILL_REMOTE_GB |
| **Drill-through: Elapsed** | Total elapsed time of the single selected/pasted query, with queued time as a sub-delta. | TOTAL_ELAPSED_TIME/1000 AS ELAPSED_SEC; delta = humanize_duration(QUEUED_SEC). Scan bounded to clicked row's START_TIME +/-1 day when known (else full 365d retention). | seconds (humanized) | insights_sql.query_detail -> ACCOUNT_USAGE.QUERY_HISTORY single QUERY_ID |
| **Drill-through: Scanned** | GB scanned by the selected query, with % served from cache. | BYTES_SCANNED/1024^3 AS GB_SCANNED (humanize_gb); CACHE_PCT = PERCENTAGE_SCANNED_FROM_CACHE*100 (0-1 fraction -> percent at read). | GiB / percent | insights_sql.query_detail -> QUERY_HISTORY.BYTES_SCANNED, PERCENTAGE_SCANNED_FROM_CACHE |
| **Drill-through: Partitions** | Partitions scanned vs total for the selected query; high ratio = reads almost the whole table. | f'{PARTITIONS_SCANNED:,}/{PARTITIONS_TOTAL:,}' | count/count | insights_sql.query_detail -> QUERY_HISTORY.PARTITIONS_SCANNED, PARTITIONS_TOTAL |
| **Drill-through: Spill** | Remote spill of the selected query. | humanize_gb(REMOTE_SPILL_GB) + ' remote'; REMOTE_SPILL_GB=BYTES_SPILLED_TO_REMOTE_STORAGE/1024^3 | GiB humanized | insights_sql.query_detail -> QUERY_HISTORY.BYTES_SPILLED_TO_REMOTE_STORAGE |
| **Wasted spend (Nd)** | Dollars of warehouse compute ALLOCATED (not billed) to failed/killed/aborted queries — money spent on runs that produced nothing. | Per query: HOUR_CREDITS * EXEC_MS / TOTAL_EXEC_MS_in_hour (denominator counts EVERY query in the hour); filtered to EXECUTION_STATUS<>'SUCCESS'; rolled up by fingerprint -> WASTED_CREDITS. WASTED_USD = credits_to_usd(WASTED_CREDITS, rate, round_cents=False) rounded to 2dp; SUM shown via format_usd. | USD | insights_sql.wasted_query_spend_usd -> QUERY_HISTORY x WAREHOUSE_METERING_HISTORY; rate=settings CREDIT_PRICE_USD (default 3.68, compute) |
| **Wasted: Monthly-ized** | Wasted spend projected to 30 days over the days the scan actually covers (v4.606: no longer up to 2x high early in a calendar period). | SUM(WASTED_USD)/max(served,1)*30, via format_usd; served = the calendar preset's day span (end − start — Current month / Current year pass a day offset one less than the span), else min(days, 90) (the live QUERY_HISTORY clamp). When the scan returns its LIMIT 50 the sum (and the tile label) is the top-50 fingerprints, not the whole window. | USD/month | app-side from wasted_query_spend_usd result |
| **Optimization opportunities ▸ Open in Optimize → (v4.600 #28)** | Opens the selected query family in Operations ▸ Optimize: its observed 30-day cost, one diagnosis and Track, with the live query profile switched on. A family Optimize does not queue gets an explanation in its detail pane. | request_navigation('Operations', 'Optimize', context={'fingerprint', 'live_profile'}); Optimize resolves the fingerprint case-insensitively (fix_queue.resolve_deep_link) and consumes the link once, including on an empty queue (explained there); only a failed queue read keeps it. | action | Same scan as the board (ops_sql.query_opportunity_fingerprints), one cache entry |
| **Wasted: Repeat offenders** | Number of query fingerprints that failed 5+ times in the window (broken query on a retry loop). | COUNT of fingerprints where FAILED_RUNS>=5; FAILED_RUNS = COUNT(DISTINCT non-success QUERY_ID) | count | wasted_query_spend_usd result FAILED_RUNS |

*Columns:* **Heaviest queries: QUERY_ID** — Query identifier; leads the table as the drill target, row-click fills the drill box (mart union-of-hourly-top-50s or live top-N by elapsed; the mart is exact for Company = ALL, and v4.606 serves a company-scoped read from the mart only when an exactness certificate proves the stored rows hold that company's true top-N — every hour the loader cut keeps a lightest row below the company's N-th heaviest — else the live top-N; a trailing Window over 90 days reads the last 90 days on either leg, and the section note says so); **Heaviest queries: START_TIME (Started)** — Query start timestamp, formatted MMM DD, HH:mm; **Heaviest queries: USER / USER_NAME** — Executing user (raw) and resolved display name via with_user_names; **Heaviest queries: WAREHOUSE_NAME** — Warehouse the query ran on; **Heaviest queries: ELAPSED_SEC (Elapsed)** — TOTAL_ELAPSED_TIME/1000, humanized to Hr/Min/Sec by the shared table machinery (table is elapsed-ranked); **Heaviest queries: QUEUED_SEC (Queued)** — (QUEUED_OVERLOAD+QUEUED_PROVISIONING)/1000, humanized to Hr/Min/Sec; **Heaviest queries: SPILL_REMOTE_GB (Spill Remote)** — BYTES_SPILLED_TO_REMOTE_STORAGE/1024^3, byte-humanized (MB/GB/TB, e.g. 30.7 MB); **Heaviest queries: EXECUTION_STATUS** — SUCCESS / FAIL / INCIDENT etc.; **Heaviest queries: QUERY_PREVIEW** — LEFT(QUERY_TEXT,180); **Heaviest queries: PROFILE** — Injected Snowsight query-profile deep-link (snowsight_profile_column); **Failures by error: ERROR_CODE** — COALESCE(ERROR_CODE,'UNKNOWN'); **Failures by error: ERROR_MESSAGE** — LEFT(ERROR_MESSAGE,140) family text; **Failures by error: FAILURES** — COUNT(*) of non-success queries in that code/message family; **Failures by error: USERS_AFFECTED** — live: COUNT(DISTINCT USER_NAME); mart: HLL_ESTIMATE(HLL_COMBINE(USERS_HLL)) approx-distinct; **Failures by error: LAST_SEEN** — MAX(START_TIME) for the family; **Wasted spend: WASTED_USD (Wasted $)** — Allocated compute $ on non-success runs of this fingerprint, format $%.2f; **Wasted spend: FAILED_RUNS** — COUNT(DISTINCT QUERY_ID) of non-success runs; **Wasted spend: EXECUTION_STATUS / ERROR_CODE / QUERY_TYPE** — MAX() representative status/code/type per fingerprint; **Wasted spend: QUERY_SNIPPET / FINGERPRINT** — LEFT(QUERY_TEXT,140); QUERY_PARAMETERIZED_HASH (or QUERY_ID fallback); **Query insights: INSIGHT_TYPE / OCCURRENCES / QUERIES / SAMPLE_MESSAGE / LAST_SEEN** — Snowflake-authored QUERY_INSIGHTS rolled up by INSIGHT_TYPE_ID over 7d: count, distinct QUERY_IDs, one sample message, latest seen (probe-gated optional view)

### Tasks
Nested Health / Graph / Runs views: task run outcomes and failure rate, a root-cause failure timeline with error families and incident routing, the DAG topology with per-run critical-path/bottleneck analysis and version compare, and per-node loader timing.

| Metric | Means | Formula | Unit | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Task runs (Nd)** | Total task executions in the window for the scope. | SUM(RUNS) across FACT_TASK_DAILY rows (or COUNT(*) grouped live). f'{total_runs:,.0f}' | count | mart_sql.fact_task_daily -> FACT_TASK_DAILY.RUNS; live fallback ops_sql.task_runs -> ACCOUNT_USAGE.TASK_HISTORY |
| **Failed runs** | Task runs with STATE=FAILED, with fail % as a delta. | SUM(FAILED); delta = total_failed/total_runs*100. delta_color inverse when any failures. | count / percent | FACT_TASK_DAILY.FAILED (SUM(IFF(STATE='FAILED',1,0)) at load); live TASK_HISTORY |
| **Failures (7d) [timeline]** | Count of task failures in the last 7 days for the scope (root+cascade). | v4.606: TOTAL_FAILURES_WIN = COUNT(*) OVER () before the builder's LIMIT 500 (was len(build_failure_timeline(df)), which pinned at 500); df = STATE='FAILED' rows over 7d (scan bounded on SCHEDULED_TIME), each task's first failure in the window kept before any repeat, then the newest (the incident RCA feed keeps the ones nearest the onset); when the total exceeds the rows read, a caption says root causes, families and the table cover N of M. Under Last month the 7-day scan always runs (a failure-free month's mart zero no longer short-circuits it). | count | insights_sql.task_failure_details -> ACCOUNT_USAGE.TASK_HISTORY |
| **Root causes** | First failure per task-graph run (fix these, cascade follows). | count of ROLE_IN_GRAPH=='Root cause'; build_failure_timeline marks earliest QUERY_START_TIME per GRAPH_RUN_GROUP_ID as root. | count | logic.insights.build_failure_timeline over task_failure_details |
| **Top error family** | Most common classified error family among the failures. | timeline['ERROR_FAMILY'].mode(); classify_task_error regex-maps ERROR_MESSAGE to families (Permission/auth, Concurrency/live version, Session not set up, Missing object, Timeout, Resource, Data quality, Syntax, Other...) | category | logic.insights.classify_task_error |
| **Incidents / High severity / Unrouted** | Distinct root-cause failing tasks collapsed to one incident each; count CRITICAL+HIGH by catalog criticality; count with no owner/on-call. | route_incidents(timeline, ENTITY_CATALOG): one incident per DB.SCH.TASK, FAILURES=count, SEVERITY from catalog CRITICALITY (unknown->STANDARD), ROUTED_TO=on_call\|owner\|'unassigned'. summarize_incidents counts. | count | logic.incident.route_incidents/summarize_incidents; catalog via workbench_sql.entity_catalog -> ENTITY_CATALOG |
| **Graph KPIs: Tasks / Dependencies / Failed tasks (recent) / Suspended** | For the selected root graph snapshot: node count, edge count, tasks with recent failures (today+yesterday), suspended nodes. | len(frame); len(shape.edges) (inspect_task_graph); COUNT RECENT_FAILURES>0; COUNT STATE contains 'suspend'. RECENT_FAILURES = SUM(FAILED) from MART_TASK_NODE_DAILY for DAY>=yesterday. | count | ops_sql.task_graph_nodes -> TASK_VERSIONS snapshot + TASKS + MART_TASK_NODE_DAILY; logic.task_graph.inspect_task_graph |
| **Run analyzer: Run wall time / Dispatch queue / Failed tasks / Critical path** | For one selected graph execution: end-to-end wall time, scheduled-to-start dispatch delay, failed node count, longest observed dependency path duration. | WALL_SEC=DATEDIFF(sec, MIN(SCHEDULED_TIME), MAX(COMPLETED_TIME)); QUEUE_SEC=SUM(GREATEST(sched->start ms,0))/1000; critical path = max cumulative PATH_SEC (analyze_task_run, PATH_SEC=sum of node RUN_SEC along heaviest predecessor chain). humanize_duration. | seconds (humanized) / count | ops_sql.task_graph_recent_runs + task_graph_run_nodes -> TASK_HISTORY; logic.task_graph.analyze_task_run |
| **Version compare: Before/After tasks, Added, Removed, Rewired** | Node/edge diff between two coherent graph versions of the selected root. | compare_task_versions(before,after): ADDED/REMOVED/DEPENDENCIES_CHANGED counts by canonical TASK_FQN + sorted predecessor tuples. | count | ops_sql.task_graph_version_nodes -> TASK_VERSIONS; logic.task_graph.compare_task_versions |

*Columns:* **Health table (fact_task_daily)** — DAY, DATABASE_NAME, TASK_NAME, COMPANY, RUNS, FAILED, AVG_SEC (avg sched->complete seconds), LAST_STATE, LAST_ERROR; sorted failed desc; **Failure timeline: QUERY_START_TIME** — When the failing task run started; **Failure timeline: ROLE_IN_GRAPH** — 'Root cause' (first failure in its GRAPH_RUN_GROUP_ID) or 'Cascade'; **Failure timeline: ERROR_FAMILY** — classify_task_error regex family of ERROR_MESSAGE; **Failure timeline: DATABASE_NAME/SCHEMA_NAME/TASK_NAME** — Failing task identity; **Failure timeline: RUN_SEC** — DATEDIFF(second, QUERY_START_TIME, COMPLETED_TIME); **Failure timeline: ERROR_MESSAGE** — LEFT(ERROR_MESSAGE,300); **Failures by family chart** — bar_count of failures grouped by ERROR_FAMILY; **Incident routing: ENTITY/ERROR_FAMILY/FAILURES/LAST_FAILED/SEVERITY/OWNER/ON_CALL/ROUTED_TO/REMEDIATION** — DB.SCH.TASK; family; root-cause count; last failure ts; severity from catalog criticality; owner+on-call from ENTITY_CATALOG; ROUTED_TO=on_call\|owner\|unassigned; REMEDIATION first-response text keyed by error family; **Node run timing (task_nodes)** — DAY, DATABASE_NAME, SCHEMA_NAME, TASK_NAME, RUNS, FAILED, FAIL_PCT (FAILED/RUNS*100, app-computed), AVG/P95/MAX_QUEUE_SEC (dispatch queue), AVG/P95/MAX_EXEC_SEC (execution), FIRST_START, LAST_COMPLETED; from MART_TASK_NODE_DAILY, ordered p95 dispatch queue desc; **Run analyzer ranked nodes** — TASK_FQN, STATE, CRITICAL_PATH, DOWNSTREAM_TASKS (transitive child count), QUEUE_SEC, EXEC_SEC, RUN_SEC, BOTTLENECK_SCORE (duration*(1+log2(downstream+1))), ERROR_MESSAGE, PROFILE deep-link; **Graph roots picker label** — ROOT_TASK_FQN, NODE_COUNT tasks, graph vN, and '⚠ N recent failed runs' (RECENT_FAILURES = today+yesterday mart run count); ordered failures-first then node count

### Warehouses (incl. Contention)
Warehouse spend trend + daily anomalies, concurrency peaks, on-demand utilization/right-size profile with health chips and cost-per-query peer outliers, quiet-hours schedule opportunity, adaptive-compute candidacy, and (folded in) contention: queue/spill pressure and lock waits.

| Metric | Means | Formula | Unit | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Spend trend (by day) + anomaly rules** | Account/company daily warehouse spend over 30 days (fixed), with dashed rules on anomalous warehouse-days; click a day to break down by warehouse. | USD per warehouse-day = credits_to_usd(CREDITS_TOTAL, rate) [rate=compute 3.68]; daily = groupby(DAY).sum(USD). Anomalies via flag_anomalies(complete_days_only(df),'USD',group=WAREHOUSE_NAME, min_value=$50, min_active_days=10, robust z>=3.5) then suppress_expected_spikes(EXPECTED_SPIKE_CALENDAR). | USD/day | mart_sql.fact_warehouse_daily(30) -> FACT_WAREHOUSE_DAILY.CREDITS_TOTAL; logic.anomaly.flag_anomalies |
| **Anomalous warehouse-days table (Spend $, Robust z)** | Each warehouse-day flagged anomalous, ranked by \|z\| so collapses (large negative z, stalled loader) surface beside spikes. | IS_ANOMALY rows; USD format $%.0f, Z_SCORE format %.1f (modified median/MAD z). | USD / z-score | FACT_WAREHOUSE_DAILY via logic.anomaly |
| **Concurrency peaks: PEAK_RUNNING / PEAK_QUEUED** | Highest concurrent running / queued load per warehouse over 14 days; sustained PEAK_QUEUED>~1 signals add-a-cluster (on a multi-cluster warehouse, raise MAX_CLUSTER_COUNT only if its queries reach the current maximum — Cost Intelligence ▸ Optimization & Savings ▸ Idle & sizing ▸ Check cluster use checks it). | MAX(AVG_RUNNING), MAX(AVG_QUEUED_LOAD) rounded 1dp; QUEUED_INTERVALS=COUNT(AVG_QUEUED_LOAD>0.5) over the whole 14-day read. v4.606: the Warehouses attention list (anomaly.warehouse_attention_ranking) calls queueing sustained, and ranks it above spend anomalies, only when PEAK_QUEUED >= 1 AND QUEUED_INTERVALS reach sizing's 30 min/day sustained-overload rate across the window (ceil(30 x 14 / 5) = 84 five-minute intervals); the reason then reads 'queued ~X sustained (~N/day over 14d)'. A warehouse whose PEAK_QUEUED reaches 1 but whose QUEUED_INTERVALS stay under that rate (or a peaks frame with no QUEUED_INTERVALS column, an older shape) is KEPT — demoted, never dropped, so the opener cannot read clean over real queueing: its reason reads 'peak queued ~X' and its queue earns no priority (a queue-only row ranks after every spend anomaly; one that also has a spend anomaly ranks by its z). The Queueing KPI counts both kinds and its help reads 'N of M sustained (queued at least 30m/day across the 14-day read)', adding that the rest peaked at the queue floor but stayed under that rate, ranked after spend anomalies, when any row is under it. | concurrent count (avg-of-interval) | ops_sql.warehouse_concurrency_peaks -> ACCOUNT_USAGE.WAREHOUSE_LOAD_HISTORY |
| **Utilization: Add a cluster, Size up, Tune auto-suspend first, Size-down candidates, Idle spend on those** | Counts of right-size verdicts across the fleet plus measured idle monthly $ on the auto-suspend-first rows. Add a cluster = overload queueing without remote spill (concurrency) — not checked against the cluster cap here (Cost ▸ Optimization & Savings ▸ Idle & sizing checks it), which a visible caption under the table says whenever such rows exist (sizing.unchecked_cap_note on sizing_summary's cap_unchecked; v4.604 review); Size up = remote spill (per-query memory); its help says to add a cluster only if the queue persists, with the cap-check rule (sizing.CLUSTER_CAP_QUALIFIER; review r2) — v4.601 split of the old merged "Size up / add cluster" card (#38). | size_recommendations(profile+SHOW WAREHOUSES autosuspend, rate, served_days — v4.606: the bounds' day span under a calendar preset, as Cost ▸ Optimize divides, so per-day / monthly figures are no longer up to 2x high early in a calendar period): per-warehouse verdict from QUEUED_MIN_PER_DAY/SPILL_GB_PER_DAY/P95/IDLE_PCT thresholds. sizing_summary counts (scale_out / size_up); idle_saving_usd = SUM(IDLE_MONTHLY_USD) on SUSPEND rows (MONTHLY_USD_NOW*IDLE_PCT/100). | count / USD | run_mart_first(mart27_sql.eff_sizing_profile -> MART_WAREHOUSE_EFFICIENCY_DAILY, insights_sql.warehouse_sizing_profile -> WAREHOUSE_METERING_HISTORY x QUERY_HISTORY); logic.sizing |
| **Warehouse Health (0-100) chip** | Transparent grade per warehouse = 100 minus capped penalties for queueing/remote spill/long p95/low utilization; names which penalty fired. | 100 - cap((QUEUED_MIN_PER_DAY-10)*1.5,25) - cap((SPILL_GB_PER_DAY-1)*4,20) - cap((p95-120)/120*20,20) - cap((50-util%)*0.4,20) [util gated on >=3 active days]. GRADE: >=85 Healthy,>=70 Watch,>=50 Degraded,else At risk. | score 0-100 | logic.wh_health.warehouse_health over size_recommendations frame |
| **Cost-per-query outliers: Warehouses compared / outliers** | Warehouses whose $/query is a robust-z outlier vs their company fleet (cross-sectional, not temporal). | USD_PER_QUERY = credits_to_usd(CREDITS_TOTAL,rate,round_cents=False)/QUERY_COUNT; within-COMPANY robust z (median/MAD) >=3.5 = outlier; floor >=100 queries and >=1 credit; needs >=5 warehouses to z-score. MULT_OF_MEDIAN = usd_per_query/fleet median. | USD/query, x-median, z | logic.cost_peers.cost_per_query_peers over the sizing profile |
| **Quiet-hours windows: QUIET_WINDOW / HOURS / AVG_CREDITS_PER_DAY** | Warehouses with a defensible 4h+ recurring window burning credits with ~no queries — an off-hours suspend/resume opportunity. | hour-of-day heatmap of AVG_CREDITS vs AVG_QUERIES; remediation.propose_quiet_window per warehouse. AVG_CREDITS = SUM(CREDITS_USED)/days (per calendar day, America/Chicago hour). | hour window / credits/day | insights_sql.warehouse_hourly_activity -> WAREHOUSE_METERING_HISTORY x FACT_QUERY_HOURLY; logic.remediation.propose_quiet_window |
| **Statement-timeout posture: STATUS / EFFECTIVE_TIMEOUT_SEC / CAP_SOURCE / TIMEOUT_FIRED (Fired at) / SUGGESTED_TIMEOUT_SEC / WOULD_CANCEL_RUNS (+ Warehouses read, Uncapped, Timed out (Nd), Account value)** | Toggle-gated (v4.601, #33): each warehouse's effective STATEMENT_TIMEOUT_IN_SECONDS, flagged Uncapped when one runaway statement could bill for 48h or more, plus a review-only tightening script. Nothing runs, is logged or booked. | effective = min(enforced(warehouse value), enforced(account value)), enforced(0) = 604800 (the 7-day max); Uncapped = effective >= 172800 (the default). Suggested cap (Uncapped rows only) = the smallest of 300/600/900/1800/3600/7200/14400/28800/43200/86400 s at or above p99 x 3 of completed statements over max(Window, 30) days capped at 90, with >= 100 runs (none when p99 x 3 > 24h). WOULD_CANCEL_RUNS = completed statements in the window longer than that cap. Each script ALTER is followed by its exact undo as a comment (UNSET, or SET = the warehouse's own value). Timed out = statements cancelled by a statement/warehouse timeout (SQL window total), at whichever ceiling was lowest for that statement (warehouse, account, user, session, client or task), so it is not 'the effective cap fired'. Fired at (v4.603, #33 D5) = the humanized lowest-highest ceiling those cancels fired at (TIMEOUT_FIRED_MIN_SEC / _MAX_SEC: N parsed from the message's 'timeout of N second(s)', thousands commas stripped; dash when none), marked 'below cap' when the lowest is under the row's effective cap (a lower ceiling fired: a user, session, client or task value, or an earlier, lower warehouse or account value -- the message gives the number of seconds, not which of these set it; the rows are named in a caption). Status Unread = its SHOW failed; Not visible = ran in the window but SHOW WAREHOUSES does not list it (dropped, renamed, or not visible to the app role); Managed compute (v4.603, #33 D4) = a COMPUTE_SERVICE_WH* name SHOW does not list (Snowflake-managed serverless-task / upgrade pools: no role can SHOW them, no warehouse timeout to set, task statements capped by USER_TASK_TIMEOUT_MS; its own caption, out of the Not visible count; a warehouse SHOW lists is read whatever its name). Under a company scope only warehouses active in the window are listed (a failed runtime tail then reads as unavailable, not empty). Account value = the ENFORCED account value (0 reads 168h, '0 = 7-day max'). A user/session value can lower the cap; task statements are also capped by USER_TASK_TIMEOUT_MS. | seconds / count | ops_sql.warehouse_timeout_tail -> QUERY_HISTORY (live, historical tier; + TIMEOUT_FIRED_MIN_SEC / _MAX_SEC); ops_sql.warehouse_stmt_timeout_sql (SHOW PARAMETERS ... IN WAREHOUSE, one per warehouse, metadata tier, at most 40 — longest completed runs first — so the reads stay within a third of the 128-entry metadata cache) + account_stmt_timeout_sql (IN ACCOUNT; derived from the warehouse rows when unreadable); logic.stmt_timeout |
| **Adaptive-compute candidacy: Scored / Strong / Consider (+SCORE, Peak÷mean)** | Which warehouses would benefit from auto-scaling clusters (bursty + material volume) vs auto-suspend/fixed-size. | SCORE=round(100*burst*volume_gate*idle_discount); burst=clamp((peak_to_mean-1.6)/(5.0-1.6)); mean=daily_credits/24 (absent hours=0 credits); volume_gate=clamp(daily/10); idle_discount=1-0.5*clamp(idle%/100). VERDICT: idle>=50% -> Auto-suspend first; >=60 Strong; >=35 Consider. v4.606: a warehouse with no idle evidence (the idle read failed, or it is missing from that read's rows) gets IDLE_PCT '—' and 'idle n/a' in the rationale, no idle discount and no Auto-suspend-first override (unknown idle is not 0% idle), and the panel says the idle read failed. | score 0-100 / ratio | logic.adaptive.adaptive_compute_candidacy over warehouse_hourly_activity + idle_warehouse_analysis |
| **Contention: queue & spill pressure (Avg queue per query)** | Per-warehouse queueing and spill for the window; ranked by average queue per query (user-felt stall). | AVG_QUEUE_SEC = QUEUED_SEC/QUERY_COUNT (app-computed). QUEUED_SEC = SUM overload+provisioning /1000 (live) or SUM(QUEUED_SEC_SUM) (mart); HAVING queued>0 OR spill>0. P95 is peak-hourly on mart path. v4.606: the mart leg reads up to 365 days (MAX_MART_WINDOW_DAYS; it was cut to 90), but FACT_QUERY_HOURLY is not backfilled, so it holds only the days loaded since OVERWATCH started: the reader carries COVERED_DAYS (the fact's own span, over the whole fact) and, when that starts after the Window does (trailing, or a calendar window's first day), a caption says the hourly fact holds N days of history. The live fallback reads at most 90 days of a trailing window, and a caption says so when it answered. | seconds / GiB | run_mart_first(mart_sql.fact_warehouse_pressure -> FACT_QUERY_HOURLY, ops_sql.warehouse_pressure -> QUERY_HISTORY) |
| **Contention: Lock waits (WAIT_EVENTS, ACQUIRED_WAIT_SEC, NEVER_ACQUIRED)** | Lock contention per object; never-acquired (aborted/timed-out) locks ranked first. | COUNT(*) wait events; SUM(IFF(ACQUIRED_AT NOT NULL, DATEDIFF(sec,REQUESTED_AT,ACQUIRED_AT),0)); COUNT_IF(ACQUIRED_AT IS NULL). v4.606: both legs narrow to the Company and the Database filter in SQL, before their LIMIT 50 (the mart on its COMPANY column, plus account-level 'ALL' rows; the live fallback on the object's database company, COMPANY_FOR_DATABASE), so a database ranked past an account-wide top 50 no longer reads as 'no lock waits'. The live fallback reads at most the last 7 days of the window (cost cap; clipped on the 7-day cap, so a calendar period that began less than 7 days ago is read from its first day). | count / seconds | run_mart_first(mart27_sql.lock_wait_daily -> MART_LOCK_WAIT_DAILY, ops_sql.lock_contention -> ACCOUNT_USAGE.LOCK_WAIT_HISTORY) |

*Columns:* **Utilization table** — WAREHOUSE_NAME, SCORE (Health %d), GRADE, RECOMMENDATION, WHY (health penalties), IDLE_PCT (%.0f%%), QUEUED_MIN_PER_DAY, SPILL_GB_PER_DAY, ACTIVE_DAYS_PER_30D, P95_ELAPSED_SEC (peak-day on mart), MONTHLY_USD_NOW (Now $/mo $%.0f); **Cost-per-query outliers table** — WAREHOUSE_NAME, COMPANY, USD_PER_QUERY ($%.4f), MULT_OF_MEDIAN (%.1fx), Z_SCORE (Peer z %.1f), QUERY_COUNT, IS_OUTLIER; **Adaptive candidacy table** — WAREHOUSE_NAME, SCORE, VERDICT, PEAK_TO_MEAN (%.1fx), DAILY_CREDITS (%.1f), IDLE_PCT (%.0f%%), RATIONALE; **Concurrency peaks table** — WAREHOUSE_NAME, PEAK_RUNNING, PEAK_QUEUED, QUEUED_INTERVALS, INTERVALS; **Contention pressure table** — WAREHOUSE_NAME, QUERY_COUNT, QUEUED_SEC (humanized H/M/S), AVG_QUEUE_SEC, SPILL_REMOTE_GB, P95_ELAPSED_SEC; **Lock waits table** — DATABASE_NAME, SCHEMA_NAME, OBJECT_NAME, LOCK_TYPE, WAIT_EVENTS, ACQUIRED_WAIT_SEC (humanized H/M/S), NEVER_ACQUIRED, LAST_SEEN; **Day breakdown (spend click)** — WAREHOUSE_NAME, USD (Spend $ $%.0f) for the clicked day; each row drills to Entity 360

### Optimize
The recurring-query fix queue (v4.597; the old Decision Studio ▸ Portfolio, rebuilt under Operations). It lists every measured query family in scope with its observed mart dollars, ONE diagnosis and a first fix, plus a one-click **Track** into Action Center. Scope: Company and Window, and the optional live profile also honors Warehouse, User, Database and Schema. There is no live read on first paint: the queue, the tracked set and the watchlist come from app and mart tables in one mixed-tier round trip. OVERWATCH's own Streamlit runtime family is excluded. OVERWATCH's own loader and mart families are shown with an "OVERWATCH's own traffic" tag and are never bulk-tracked.

| Metric | Means | Formula | Unit | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **(chip) Act now** | ACT NOW families with a specific diagnosis that have no open Action Center item and were not recently dismissed or done (a done family whose measured outcome reads Re-broke or Not fixed counts again, v4.601), with their summed observed 30-day cost. Also the section's pill count. When the Action Center read fails, the chip counts every such family and says the status is unknown. | count where LANE='ACT NOW' AND SPECIFIC AND TRACK_STATUS not in ('Tracked (open)','Dismissed','Done'); SUM(IMPACT_USD_30D) over those rows; stash_section_count(dims=company, days) | count / USD | logic.fix_queue.diagnose_workloads + with_track_status over optimize_queue + tracked_actions |
| **(chip) Failure risk** | Families at or above a 2% observed failure rate. | count where FAIL_PCT>=2; FAIL_PCT=FAILS/FAMILY_RUNS*100, clamped to 100 (v4.606: the family mart's own runs, the same population as its FAILS — the pattern mart's RUNS counts only warehouse-attributed runs, so FAILS/RUNS read high, past 100% or across the 2% gate on a 1% family); RUNS only when FAMILY_RUNS is missing or zero. | count | MART_QUERY_FAMILY_DAILY FAILS / FAMILY_RUNS (decision.prioritize_workloads) |
| **(chip) Needs validation** | Families in the VALIDATE lane: confidence below 0.5, or no measured cache, latency or failure evidence at all. | count where LANE='VALIDATE' (the lane itself, not a re-derived confidence filter) | count | prioritize_workloads.LANE |
| **Measured families** | Recurring query families with measured cost in scope. This is the uncapped scope total; the queue lists the top 200 by credits and says so when that cap binds. | max(SCOPE_FAMILIES_TOTAL, rows listed); SCOPE_FAMILIES_TOTAL = COUNT(*) OVER () computed before the LIMIT | count | workbench_sql.optimize_queue -> MART_PATTERN_COST_DAILY (costs) + MART_QUERY_FAMILY_DAILY (behaviour + advisor columns) |
| **Observed cost (30d)** | Measured pattern credits for EVERY family in scope, priced and normalized to 30 days. This is observed cost, not promised savings. | SCOPE_CREDITS_TOTAL x rate / span x 30. SCOPE_CREDITS_TOTAL = ROUND(SUM(c.CREDITS) OVER (),4), computed before the LIMIT. span = the window's real day span (bounds end - start) for Last month / Current month / Current year, else the Window days (W12 fix: calendar presets used to divide by a day offset). | USD | optimize_queue SCOPE_CREDITS_TOTAL; rate=CREDIT_PRICE_USD (default 3.68) |
| **Specific diagnosis** | Listed families with a named diagnosis and first fix, out of all listed. | SPECIFIC.sum() of len(queue); SPECIFIC = DIAG_SOURCE in {live, mart, heuristic} | count of count | logic.fix_queue.diagnose_workloads |
| **Tracked (open)** | Listed families with an OPEN or IN_PROGRESS Action Center item. | count where TRACK_STATUS='Tracked (open)'; open = OPEN_N>0 or the latest item's status is OPEN/IN_PROGRESS | count | workbench_sql.tracked_actions -> ACTION_QUEUE (SOURCE_ENTITY_TYPE='QUERY_FINGERPRINT'; recent tier; the "queue" cache salt refreshes it after a write) |
| **Evidence coverage** | Average share of the three behavioural evidence signals (cache, latency, fail rate) present per family. Low coverage means more calls rest on partial evidence. | mean(EVIDENCE_COVERAGE)*100; EVIDENCE_COVERAGE = (has_cache + has_latency + has_fails)/3 from notna of the raw AVG_CACHE_PCT / P95_SEC / FAILS; ok at >= 80% | percent | prioritize_workloads; raw nullable columns from the MART_QUERY_FAMILY_DAILY join |
| **Watching** | Query families on the viewer's personal watchlist, pinned to the top of their lane. | SUM(WATCHED); WATCHED = fingerprint on the watchlist (case-insensitive QUERY_FINGERPRINT match) | count | workbench_sql.watchlist(viewer) -> USER_WATCHLIST (recent tier); logic.workbench.mark_watched |
| **Diagnosis (per family)** | ONE named diagnosis and first fix per family, taken from the best evidence available. | Precedence: (1) **live**: the Operations ▸ Queries optimization profile found a pathology for this FINGERPRINT (a clean 'None' row falls through); DIAGNOSIS = its PATHOLOGY, FIRST_FIX = its breakdown's lead fix. (2) **mart**: query_advisor.advise(advisor_input(row)) over the family mart's per-run averages (ELAPSED/EXECUTION/COMPILE_SEC = totals / FAMILY_RUNS, GB_SCANNED, CACHE_PCT, SAMPLE_TEXT and the two compile-run proxies); DIAGNOSIS = query_opt.pathology_label(lead finding), FIRST_FIX = its detail. (3) **heuristic**: NEXT_MOVE in {Cache or materialize, Stabilize failures, Reduce recurrence} with a canned first fix. (4) **none**: 'Validate evidence' or 'Profile it'; the first fix names what the marts cannot see (spill, partition pruning, queueing, rows returned). The ranking is prioritize_workloads' own and is never re-ranked. | label + text | logic.fix_queue.diagnose_workloads; live = query_opt.score_opportunities over ops_sql.query_opportunity_fingerprints |
| **Confidence (evidence)** | Diagnosis confidence 0-1: the live profile's confidence when it named the diagnosis, else the family's evidence heuristic. NOT statistical confidence and NOT an authored belief. | live: CONFIDENCE/100 clipped to [0,1]; otherwise the portfolio CONFIDENCE = 0.35*min(RUNS/30,1) + 0.35*min(ACTIVE_DAYS/min(window,30),1) + 0.30*(CREDITS>0) | ratio 0-1 (bar) | DIAG_CONFIDENCE from diagnose_workloads |
| **Priced (detail pane)** | The only dollar figure a Track writes as an estimate: the failed-run share of observed cost, and only for a "Stabilize failures" diagnosis. Every other diagnosis is unpriced. | PRICED_USD_MO = IMPACT_USD_30D x min(FAIL_PCT,100)/100 when DIAGNOSIS='Stabilize failures' and both > 0, else NaN ("unpriced") | USD per month | diagnose_workloads PRICED_USD_MO |
| **Track (detail pane, operators)** | Queue ONE family into Action Center. A row click only selects; Track is a separate button in the detail pane behind the operator gate and a per-family C48 latch. The statement is shown in a "Statement" expander first. | fix_queue.track_fingerprints_sql(bulk=False): one INSERT ... SELECT ... FROM (VALUES ...) AS v (...) WHERE NOT EXISTS an ACTION_QUEUE row with UPPER(SOURCE_ENTITY_TYPE)='QUERY_FINGERPRINT' AND UPPER(SOURCE_ENTITY_KEY)=UPPER(fingerprint) AND STATUS IN ('OPEN','IN_PROGRESS'). The key is never the title or company. Writes COMPANY (the selected company, or the family's TOP_COMPANY under ALL), SEVERITY MEDIUM for ACT NOW else LOW (never HIGH/CRITICAL), TITLE `<diagnosis>: <fp[:12]>… (<top database>)`, DETAIL = first fix + evidence + observed cost + "confidence is OVERWATCH's evidence score, not an authored belief", OWNER 'UNASSIGNED', STATUS 'OPEN', SOURCE 'Operations > Optimize', CONFIDENCE 0-1, ESTIMATED_USD + PERIOD 'MONTHLY' only when priced (else NULL / NULL), UPDATED_BY = the viewer. A single Track has no dismissed/done cooldown, so a human can re-track on purpose. track_fingerprints_sql is a thin wrapper over the shared fix_queue.track_entities_sql (v4.601), byte-identical without re-broke keys; Control Room triage tracks tasks and warehouses through the same statement, and Cost > Chargeback & AI exceptions track users and the all-users budget scope through it (v4.605, source-scoped, severity kept; its v4.605 options leave this statement byte-identical). | write (one statement) | logic.fix_queue.track_items + track_fingerprints_sql -> ACTION_QUEUE |
| **Track all ACT NOW (operators)** | Queue every eligible ACT NOW family in one capped, idempotent statement. The button reads "Track all ACT NOW (n)" and is disabled at n=0 (and while the Action Center status read has failed); non-operators see only a read-only caption. | Eligible = LANE='ACT NOW' AND SPECIFIC AND not own traffic (OW_SELF) AND no open item AND not DROPPED or DONE in the last 90 days (the trailing mart still carries a fixed family's pre-fix runs), ordered by PRIORITY_SCORE, capped at TRACK_ALL_CAP=25 per click. The bulk statement also carries the 90-day DROPPED/DONE cooldown in its NOT EXISTS. Items are deduped on the fingerprint in Python. Cooldown exception (v4.601, Next-Fifty #46): a family marked DONE whose measured outcome (Held?) reads Re-broke or Not fixed re-enters — track_all_eligible(rebroke=...) drops it from the done set and the NOT EXISTS gains AND NOT (STATUS='DONE' AND key IN (...)) inside the DROPPED/DONE group; Too early, Unavailable and Not checked never lift it, and a dismissal (DROPPED) never does. | write (one statement, ≤ 25 rows) | fix_queue.track_all_eligible + track_fingerprints_sql(bulk=True) |
| **Live query profile (toggle, off on first paint)** | Adds the live optimization profile, which sees what the daily marts cannot (spill, pruning, queueing, rows returned), and uses its diagnosis where it finds one. The dollars stay the marts'. | The exact Operations ▸ Queries call (same builder arguments, key `q_opp_<company>_<days>` and historical tier), so the two share one cache entry: whichever runs first pays for both. At most the last 90 days; honors Warehouse/User/Database/Schema. The caption counts the families diagnosed from it. | toggle | ops_sql.query_opportunity_fingerprints -> QUERY_HISTORY (live); query_opt.score_opportunities |
| **Portfolio map (expander chart)** | Impact x confidence scatter; bubble size = blast-radius proxy; color = lane. | x=IMPACT_USD_30D, y=CONFIDENCE (0-1), size=BLAST_RADIUS=USERS+DATABASES, color=LANE | USD x ratio | charts.workload_portfolio |

*Columns:* **FINGERPRINT** — QUERY_HASH, the query-family identity (MART_PATTERN_COST_DAILY.QUERY_HASH = the live QUERY_PARAMETERIZED_HASH); the master-detail id and the Track key.; **DIAGNOSIS (Decision)** — The named diagnosis (see Diagnosis above).; **EVIDENCE (Why)** — The measured behaviour behind it, with durations humanized: P95, cache %, GB per run, compile per run, warehouses and fail %, naming only the signals present ("No behaviour evidence in the family mart" when blind; prefixed "OVERWATCH's own traffic ·" for own-traffic families).; **IMPACT_USD_30D (Impact)** — Measured pattern credits x compute rate normalized to 30 days over the window's real span; observed cost, never a savings figure.; **DIAG_CONFIDENCE (Confidence (evidence))** — See Confidence above.; **TRACK_STATUS (Status)** — Tracked (open) / Dismissed / Done / Untracked, from the family's Action Center items (open wins over a newer closed item); Unknown for every family when the Action Center read fails; v4.601: a Done family whose measured outcome did not hold reads Re-broke or Not fixed, its done cooldown is lifted, and it counts in Act now again when it is an ACT NOW family with a specific diagnosis; Track all takes it only when it is also not own traffic — otherwise the detail pane says a single Track re-queues it.; **Held?** — v4.601: a listed Done family's measured outcome since it was marked done (its failure rate when it was failing, else attributed credits): Held N days / Re-broke <date> / Not fixed / Too early / Not measurable / Unavailable / Not checked. Present only when a Done family is listed (up to 40 measured, one gated mart read); absent when the Action Center status read fails (every family then reads Unknown); Unavailable when the outcome read itself fails.; **LANE** — ACT NOW (top-20% priority AND confidence >= 0.65) / PLAN / VALIDATE (confidence < 0.5, or no behavioural evidence).; **WATCHED** — On the viewer's watchlist (★); pinned to the top of its lane.; **RUNS / FAIL_PCT / AVG_CACHE_PCT / P95_SEC / EVIDENCE_COVERAGE** — Total runs (MART_PATTERN_COST_DAILY), FAILS/FAMILY_RUNS*100 capped at 100 (v4.606: the family mart's own runs; RUNS only when FAMILY_RUNS is missing or zero), run-weighted cache hit %, worst family P95 in seconds, share of the three signals present.; **Detail pane** — diagnosis · source ("the live query profile" / "the daily marts (day-grain averages; coarser than the live profile)" / "the portfolio heuristic" / "nothing specific yet") · confidence; first fix; a Signal / Points / Fix breakdown (additive, per-driver-capped findings); observed vs priced $/mo; the SQL sample (QUERY_PREVIEW); the Action Center status; "Open in Action Center →" when the family has an open item (a closed item is named, not linked; an open item tracked under another company is named with the Company to switch to, since Action Center lists only the scope's own and 'ALL' items) and "Open Entity 360 →", both only for profiles that can open Control Room; then Track.; **Advisor columns (not displayed)** — FAMILY_RUNS, TOTAL_EXEC_SEC, TOTAL_COMPILE_SEC (Σ COMPILE_MS_AVG x RUNS / 1000), TOTAL_ELAPSED_SEC, GB_SCANNED (run-weighted per run), WAREHOUSES, COMPILE_RUN_PCT / COMPILE_DOMINANT_RUN_PCT (0-1 run shares on compile-heavy / compile-dominated days, so one compile-storm day cannot label a family), TOP_COMPANY / TOP_DATABASE (MAX_BY credits), OW_SELF (the preview names DBA_MAINT_DB.OVERWATCH., or TOP_DATABASE = 'DBA_MAINT_DB', where the app's own reads run).

### Change impact
Did a procedure/task or a warehouse setting change make things worse? Daily-scan registries freeze a 14-day pre-change baseline and track 14 days after, with per-metric before/after deltas, verdicts, and a 28-day run-history drill.

| Metric | Means | Formula | Unit | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Object changes: Changes tracked (90d) / Regressed / Improved / Still accumulating** | Counts of tracked procedure/task changes by stored verdict. | len(df); count VERDICT REGRESSED / IMPROVED / PENDING. Verdicts computed by SP_CHANGE_IMPACT_SCAN (baseline vs after credits/call, p95, fail rate). | count | change_impact_sql.change_registry -> OBJECT_CHANGE_REGISTRY |
| **Object change row deltas: Δ calls / Δ p95 / Δ cr/call** | Signed after-minus-baseline change per tracked object (what you scan). | D_CALLS=AFTER_CALLS-BASELINE_CALLS; D_P95_S=AFTER_P95_S-BASELINE_P95_S (BASELINE_P95_MS/1000); D_CPC=AFTER_CREDITS_PER_CALL-BASELINE_CREDITS_PER_CALL. AFTER_CALLS format %d, Δ calls %+d, Δ cr/call %+.4f; D_P95_S humanized to signed Hr/Min/Sec (e.g. -2m 30s). | count / duration / credits per call | OBJECT_CHANGE_REGISTRY baseline/after columns |
| **Warehouse changes: Changes tracked (90d) / Regressed / Improved / Still accumulating** | Counts of detected warehouse setting changes by verdict (snapshot-diff of daily SHOW WAREHOUSES). | wh_change.registry_kpis (v4.606): the builder's untruncated window totals — TOTAL_CHANGES / TOTAL_REGRESSED / TOTAL_IMPROVED / TOTAL_PENDING = COUNT(*) / COUNT_IF(VERDICT …) OVER (), computed before the LIMIT 200 (the tiles used to count only the newest 200 rows); the frame is counted only when those columns are absent. Still accumulating = PENDING only (NO_BASELINE is a final verdict the scan never revisits). When the window holds more than 200 changes a caption says the table shows the latest 200 of N and the tiles count all of them. Verdict by SP_WAREHOUSE_CHANGE_SCAN (14d baseline vs after: $/day, p95, queue, spill, fail rate). | count | change_impact_sql.warehouse_change_registry -> WAREHOUSE_CHANGE_REGISTRY |
| **Warehouse change per-metric deltas (credits/day, p95, queue min/d, spill GB/d, fail %)** | Before->after for the selected change with a signed % and worse/better direction; P95_S humanized. | change_deltas(row): delta_pct=(after-base)/base*100; direction worse(>+5%)/better(<-5%)/flat; P95_S shown via humanize_duration, others f'{v:g}'. base_v=0 & after>0 -> 'new load'. | mixed ($/day, seconds, min/day, GB/day, %) | logic.wh_change.change_deltas over WAREHOUSE_CHANGE_REGISTRY |
| **28-day run history (object / warehouse)** | Daily credits/day or p95 line with a dashed rule at the detected change date. | object: MEDIAN/APPROX_PERCENTILE(elapsed)/1000 P95_S per day; warehouse: SUM(CREDITS_USED) per day + p95/queries/fails. | credits or seconds per day | change_impact_sql.object_run_history / warehouse_daily_series -> QUERY_HISTORY / TASK_HISTORY / WAREHOUSE_METERING_HISTORY |

*Columns:* **Object change table** — VERDICT, OBJECT_TYPE (Type), DATABASE_NAME, SCHEMA_NAME, OBJECT_NAME, CHANGE_SEEN_AT (Changed), Changed by (resolved), AFTER_CALLS, D_CALLS, D_P95_S (header renders 'D P95': the shared table machinery drops a duration column's caller config, label included; signed Hr/Min/Sec, e.g. -2m 30s), D_CPC (Δ cr/call); **Warehouse change table** — VERDICT, WAREHOUSE_NAME, SETTING, OLD_VALUE, NEW_VALUE, CHANGE_SEEN_AT; CHANGE_SOURCE (MANAGED = CHANGED_BY is a whole member of the comma-separated DEPLOY_ACTORS setting — v4.606: an exact member match, never a substring, so 'SVC_FLYWAY' no longer marks 'SVC_FLYWAY_PRD' managed / MANUAL=human / UNKNOWN = no CHANGED_BY); VERDICT_DETAIL shown on select (both tables; v4.606: its p95 and queue durations re-rendered in Hr/Min/Sec — 'p95 30m → 40m', 'queue 2h 25m → 3h 20m/day' — by the shared wh_change.humanize_verdict_detail, which also feeds the Control Room auto-investigation's Magnitude column and Workbench Entity 360's Recent changes DETAIL; the scan's raw-seconds text stays in the stored row and the alert DETAIL); **Run-history table** — DAY, RUNS, FAILS, MEDIAN_S, P95_S (seconds)

### Pipeline SLA
Metadata-driven table freshness SLAs with a forward-looking on-track/at-risk/overdue/breached forecast, table registration, the two read-only built-in objectives at the top of Tonight (v4.597: nightly cycle done by the target, tasks on cadence; they replace the retired Decision Studio SLO editor), plus file-load failures, volume-drop detection, robust-z row-volume anomalies, dynamic-table refresh health, and stream staleness.

| Metric | Means | Formula | Unit | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **SLA compliance** | Share of registered tables currently meeting their freshness SLA. | met/total*100 where met=SUM(SLA_MET); delta shows met/total tables. SLA_MET from PIPELINE_SLA_STATUS (LAST_ALTERED age vs MAX_AGE_HOURS). | percent | insights_sql.pipeline_sla_forecast -> PIPELINE_SLA_STATUS + TABLE_DML_HISTORY cadence |
| **Breaching now** | Registered tables past their freshness SLA right now. | total - met (NULL SLA_MET counts as breaching). | count | PIPELINE_SLA_STATUS.SLA_MET |
| **Trending to miss (overdue + at risk)** | Tables meeting SLA now but overdue vs their own refresh cadence or within one cycle of the deadline. | logic.insights.pipeline_sla_forecast: Overdue when hours_since*60 > 1.5*MEDIAN_GAP_MIN; At risk when RUNWAY_HOURS<=cycle_hours (or <=15% of MAX_AGE when no cadence). RUNWAY_HOURS=MAX_AGE_HOURS-HOURS_SINCE. | count | logic.insights.pipeline_sla_forecast over the SQL forecast frame |
| **File-load failures (7d)** | Failed/partial COPY & Snowpipe loads per target table with sample error. | COUNT(*) where STATUS IN ('Load failed','Partially loaded'); MAX(FIRST_ERROR_MESSAGE). | count (failed files) | ops_sql.copy_load_failures -> ACCOUNT_USAGE.COPY_HISTORY |
| **Volume drops (yesterday vs prior-7d avg)** | Steady-mover tables whose rows-added yesterday collapsed vs their prior-7-day average; 50%=FAILED, 30-50%=WATCH. FAILED and WATCH rows list first (v4.606), so weekday-only tables cannot push a failed table out of the top 50. | DROP_PCT=(1 - Y_ROWS/AVG_ROWS)*100; AVG_ROWS=SUM(prior-day rows)/7; steady gate AVG_ROWS>=1000 AND DAYS_ACTIVE_7D>=3; a 0-row yesterday whose same weekday last week was also 0 is NORMAL (weekday-only load). Ordered FAILED, WATCH, NORMAL, then DROP_PCT desc; LIMIT 50. | percent | ops_sql.volume_deltas -> ACCOUNT_USAGE.TABLE_DML_HISTORY |
| **Row-volume anomalies: Monitored / Low-volume loads / High-volume loads / Products affected** | Registered-product tables whose most recent load broke their own robust-z volume baseline in either direction. | robust z = (latest - median)/max(MAD*1.4826, 0.15*median) over prior loads (rows-added>0 days only); \|z\|>=3.5 flags SPIKE/DROP; scored only with >=10 prior loads and median>=100 rows/day. The read keeps every registered table's whole 28-day series up to 600 tables (DENSE_RANK on FQN), and v4.606 lifts run()'s row cap to 600 x 28 rows (dq_sql.DQ_MAX_ROWS) — the default 5,000-row cap cut the series at ~179 daily-loading tables, mid-table. | count | dq_sql.product_row_volume -> TABLE_DML_HISTORY x ENTITY_CATALOG; logic.dq.row_volume_anomalies/summarize_row_volume |
| **Dynamic table refresh health (7d)** | Refresh outcomes per dynamic table; any FAILED means downstream serves stale data. | COUNT(*) refreshes, COUNT_IF(STATE='FAILED'); STATUS FAILED if any failure. | count | ops_sql.dynamic_table_health -> ACCOUNT_USAGE.DYNAMIC_TABLE_REFRESH_HISTORY |
| **Stream staleness: Streams / Stale** | Live SHOW STREAMS count and how many are STALE (passed retention unconsumed). | len(streams); len(STALE=='true'). | count | ops_sql.show_streams_sql -> SHOW STREAMS IN ACCOUNT |
| **Explain a task (Tonight, v4.600 #14)** | On-demand drill under Workflow runtimes and Run inventory: why one ETL task failed or slowed down, from Snowflake's side. | The task's CALL matched by exact procedure name inside its CONTROL_STATUS window (±5 min), preferring CALLs in CONTROL_STATUS's own database (CALL_DATABASE shown; when only another database's CALL matched it waits out the ~45-minute lag, then leads with a warning and never reports that CALL's failure as an error); both Tonight sites bind the run their table shows (workflow_runtimes_scan projects RUN_ID); its children = the other statements in the CALL's session while it ran. Verdict lines: Failed (error text + profile link), failed then retried, a failed statement inside a successful CALL, 'likely on the Informatica side' (task failed, every matched CALL succeeded with its statements seen and succeeded, ended ≥45 min ago; otherwise hedged), a retried-but-still-failed task gets its latest CALL's reading, queued (resume vs overload; the overload advice raises MAX_CLUSTER_COUNT on a multi-cluster warehouse only if its queries reach the current maximum, checked on Cost ▸ Idle & sizing ▸ Check cluster use), spill, compile-heavy, time outside Snowflake (only after the ~45-minute lag); hedged inside QUERY_HISTORY's ~45-minute lag. The hedges name the lag and the task's age humanized (v4.605: 'lags up to ~45m and the task ended 12m ago'); a task with no end time is aged from now (the builder fills the missing end time with now via COALESCE, so END_AGE_MIN = 0) and reads as ended moments ago or still running; a NULL END_AGE_MIN (defensive only: the builder never emits one) reads as not recent instead of raising OverflowError. | text + durations | etl_control_sql.run_task_evidence_scan (CONTROL_STATUS × QUERY_HISTORY, recent tier, behind its own toggle); logic.etl_evidence |
| **Built-in objectives: Nightly cycle done by the ETL target (Tonight, v4.597)** | Read-only objective that needs no setup: of the recent nights the SLA finish forecast judges, how many finished COMPLETE at or before the ETL target (07:00 by default). It replaces the retired Decision Studio SLO editor on the surface where the pipeline is watched, and it does not page. | cycle_target_attainment(fc) over fc['nights'] (the newest SLA_FORECAST_FIT_NIGHTS = 14). MET = RUN_STATE COMPLETE AND MARGIN_SEC >= 0. Misses: late (COMPLETE past target), failed (FAILED) and hung (INCOMPLETE and not tonight's in-flight run). The newest night is left out of the judged count only while it is INCOMPLETE with live_runway_sec > 0 ("tonight still running"). Spike-calendar nights are judged. Tile = '{met}/{judged} nights', delta 'late · failed · hung'; '—' plus a needs-setup hint when unconfigured, never 0/0 — v4.606: also when the starter workflow (ETL_CYCLE_START_WORKFLOW) has no runs in CONTROL_STATUS (a misnamed or renamed anchor), which the SLA finish forecast now names as a setup prompt instead of a green 'no completed cycles'. | nights (count/count) | Reuses the SLA finish forecast (etl_cycle_sla_forecast over CONTROL_STATUS; _sla_finish_forecast_panel returns it): zero extra reads. Target = ETL_SLA_TARGET_HHMM. |
| **Built-in objectives: Tasks on cadence (Tonight, v4.597)** | Read-only objective: scheduled tasks on time against their OWN cadence (median gap, judged against their longest normal gap). Late is about one cadence overdue; stale is about two (silently stopped). | task_cadence_attainment(task_freshness_status(fresh)): on_time = STATUS 'On-time', total = rows, late = 'Late', stale = 'Stale'. Tile = '{on_time}/{total}', delta 'late · stale'. capped = rows >= 200 (the builder's LIMIT 200, v4.606: most overdue against each task's own cadence first — minutes since success ÷ max(longest normal gap, median gap) — not most-silent in absolute minutes), and the caption then says the ratio covers the 200 tasks most overdue against their own cadence; a capped tile adds '· top 200 read' and is never green. '—' with 'no derivable cadence yet' / 'unavailable' when empty or failed. | tasks (count/count) | ops_sql.task_freshness_sla(max(days,14), company, database, schema) -> ACCOUNT_USAGE.TASK_HISTORY (live, recent tier). One batch member, byte-identical SQL and tier to Tasks ▸ SLA's, so the two share the batch-member cache. Honors Company/Database/Schema. |
| **Tonight's projected finish (Tonight, v4.601 #36)** | While tonight's cycle is in flight: when it will likely finish, the usual range, the projection vs the target, and a separate 'Pace so far' tile. Painted under Tonight at a glance; the Brief's Nightly cycle tile shows the same projection ('In flight · projected ~05:12 (04:50–05:35)', 'At risk', 'Running long'). | insights.etl_cycle_eta: projected = tonight's cycle start + the MEDIAN start-to-finish of fc['nights'][1:] that are COMPLETE and not EXPECTED_SPIKE (at least SLA_FORECAST_MIN_RUNS = 4), floored at the read's snapshot; range = their 25th-75th percentile (ETA_BAND_QUANTILES); on a labelled night the forecaster's spike_extra_sec (> 0) is added. Pace = the night read's PACE_LATE_SEC (the clean-finished workflow that usually ends latest before the terminal, >= 4 clean nights, and at least as many tasks dispatched as on its leanest clean prior night) moves the projection; on a labelled night its expected share of the extra (extra x its usual end / the median cycle) is allowed first so the extra is never counted twice; it never moves the headline. Risk from the later of the two: breach past the hard deadline, miss past the target, running long past the range. {} when not in flight / failed / 12h past the hard deadline; 'terminal not due' (the terminal is absent from the night read AND did not run on this night last week, TERM_RAN_LAST_WEEK, V156's term_lw) and 'short history' say so instead of guessing. Differs from the PIPE_ETL_CYCLE_LATE alert (V156) on purpose and the caption says how (14 prior clean nights incl. month-end, first clean finish, warns past the hard deadline). | time (HH:MM), duration | Zero extra reads: the SLA finish forecast (fc, + snapshot_ts) and the shared whole-night read (attention.cycle_night_read -> etl_control_sql.cycle_night_health_scan, now with START/END offsets and PACE_*; if those columns fail it falls back to the pre-#36 roll-up so the whole-night signals never blank). |
| **Cycle timeline (Tonight, v4.601 #36)** | Toggle 'Show tonight's cycle timeline': every workflow tonight against its usual start and end, chronological, with notes (Starts the cycle, Finishes the cycle, Pace marker, Finished last so far). | insights.cycle_timeline_frame over the night read: START_OFFSET_SEC / END_OFFSET_SEC from tonight's cycle start (end only for a clean finish with at least its fewest clean-night task count dispatched); USUAL_START_OFFSET_SEC = the median start offset; USUAL_END_OFFSET_SEC = the median end over prior CLEAN finishes, shown once there are 4; LATE_VS_USUAL_SEC = end - usual end (+ = late); RUNNING_FOR_SEC = snapshot - first start for running rows. Capped at the night read's 200 rows (failed / unfinished first) with a caption; per-task detail is Explain a task. | duration | Re-renders the Tonight at a glance read; no query. Hidden when the read lacks the offset columns (unconfigured, failed, or the fallback). |

*Columns:* **SLA forecast/breaching table** — DATABASE_NAME, SCHEMA_NAME, TABLE_NAME, OWNER, FORECAST (On track/At risk/Overdue/Breached), DETAIL (v4.606: a registered table ACCOUNT_USAGE.TABLES cannot find — dropped, renamed, mis-registered, quoted mixed-case, or new within ~2h — reads 'no LAST_ALTERED — not found in ACCOUNT_USAGE.TABLES …', never 'already 0s old'), HOURS_SINCE, MAX_AGE_HOURS; **File-load failures table** — DATABASE_NAME, SCHEMA_NAME, TABLE_NAME, PIPE_NAME, FAILED_FILES, LAST_FAILURE, SAMPLE_ERROR, STATUS; **Volume drops table** — DATABASE_NAME, SCHEMA_NAME, TABLE_NAME, Y_ROWS, AVG_ROWS_PRIOR_7D, DAYS_ACTIVE_7D, DROP_PCT, STATUS; **Row-volume anomalies (flagged) table** — FQN, DATA_PRODUCT, OWNER_NAME, CRITICALITY, LATEST_DAY, DAYS_STALE, LATEST_ROWS, BASELINE_MEDIAN, ROBUST_Z, DIRECTION (SPIKE/DROP/NORMAL), FLAGGED; **Dynamic table health table** — DATABASE_NAME, SCHEMA_NAME, NAME, REFRESHES, FAILURES, LAST_STATE, LAST_REFRESH, STATUS; **Register-a-table form** — Builds a MERGE into PIPELINE_SLA_CONFIG (DATABASE/SCHEMA/TABLE uppercased, MAX_AGE_HOURS, OWNER): an upsert on DATABASE_NAME/SCHEMA_NAME/TABLE_NAME that updates MAX_AGE_HOURS and OWNER when the table is already registered and inserts it otherwise, so re-registering never writes a second config row; SQL previewed then executed by operator

### Release compare
Before/after a deploy date: overall query-health verdicts and per-task regressions, with auto-detected candidate deploy days from DDL bursts.

| Metric | Means | Formula | Unit | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Detected deploys (last 90d)** | Candidate deploy days ranked by schema-change DDL volume to pre-fill the picker. | COUNT(*) of successful CREATE/ALTER/DROP per day (excl. CTAS/ALTER_SESSION and OVERWATCH's own tag); rank_release_candidates keeps top-8 by DDL_COUNT, most-recent first. | object-change count | insights_sql.detect_release_days -> QUERY_HISTORY; logic.insights.rank_release_candidates |
| **Query health verdicts (Queries, Failure %, p95, Queued, Remote spill)** | Better/Worse/Flat verdict per metric comparing N days before vs after the release. | compare_release_periods: delta_pct=(after-before)/abs(before)*100; Flat within +/-10%; Better/Worse by lower-is-better direction. FAIL_PCT=FAILED_COUNT/QUERY_COUNT*100. AFTER window includes the release day. | percent delta / verdict | insights_sql.release_query_compare -> QUERY_HISTORY; logic.insights.compare_release_periods |
| **Task regressions (NEW_FAILURES, RUNTIME_DELTA_PCT, GOT_WORSE)** | Per-task before/after; a task 'got worse' if it gained failures or slowed >25%. | task_release_deltas pivots BEFORE/AFTER by DATABASE_NAME+TASK_NAME: NEW_FAILURES=clip(FAILED_AFTER-FAILED_BEFORE,0); RUNTIME_DELTA_PCT=(AVG_SEC_AFTER-AVG_SEC_BEFORE)/abs(before)*100; GOT_WORSE = NEW_FAILURES>0 OR delta>25%. v4.606: the read keeps WHOLE tasks (both periods), the ones that got worse first (the same rule scored in SQL, then new failures, then the runtime ratio), up to RELEASE_MAX_TASKS = 500, with TOTAL_TASKS counted before the cut (was an alphabetical 1,000-row cut with no flag); a capped compare says 'Compared the N most-regressed of M tasks with runs in the windows'. | count / percent | insights_sql.release_task_compare -> TASK_HISTORY; logic.insights.task_release_deltas |

*Columns:* **Query health table** — Metric (v4.605: 'p95 runtime' and 'Queued per query', the stale '(s)' units dropped), Before, After (unit-formatted display strings: durations humanized, per-query queue e.g. '500ms/q', GB/%/count), Delta %, Verdict; **Task regressions table** — DATABASE_NAME, TASK_NAME, RUNS_BEFORE, RUNS_AFTER, FAILED_BEFORE, FAILED_AFTER, NEW_FAILURES, AVG_SEC_BEFORE, AVG_SEC_AFTER, RUNTIME_DELTA_PCT, GOT_WORSE

### Emergency
Live incident-response console: reference lever catalogue, a validated-SQL generator (suspend/resume WH, timeouts, cluster range, scaling policy, pause pipe, suspend task, disable user, Cortex allowlist, account timeout) with type-to-confirm execution and REMEDIATION_LOG audit, and a per-warehouse running-query kill-switch. v4.606: a type-to-confirm click that arrives after the target or the typed text changed (a stale render) runs nothing and says 'Nothing ran: the typed confirmation does not match …'.

| Metric | Means | Formula | Unit | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Generated lever SQL** | Exact ALTER statement built from validated identifiers for the chosen lever; ACCOUNT-level levers warn to run as SNOW_ACCOUNTADMINS. Warehouse statement timeout: 0 = Snowflake's 7-day maximum, not 'no cap' (v4.603, #33 D3) — the lower non-zero of it and the session value (the account's unless a user or session sets its own) is what Snowflake enforces. | logic.remediation builders (suspend_warehouse, statement_timeout_fix, cluster_range_fix, scaling_policy_fix, pause_pipe, suspend_task_fqn, disable_user, cortex_allowlist, account_statement_timeout). Execution gated by confirm_gate('EMERGENCY'). | SQL statement | logic.remediation; execute_statement + REMEDIATION_LOG insert |
| **Running queries (kill-switch)** | Live in-flight/queued statements on a chosen warehouse, cancellable via SYSTEM$CANCEL_QUERY with audit. | EXECUTION_STATUS IN RUNNING/QUEUED/RESUMING_WAREHOUSE/BLOCKED; ELAPSED_S=TOTAL_ELAPSED_TIME/1000. Per-warehouse because SiS owner's-rights procs can't use current-user scoping. v4.606: each selected query needs its own typed CANCEL (the confirm and its write latch are keyed by the full QUERY_ID, so a confirm never carries over to another row); the cancel counts as done only on Snowflake's own 'query [<id>] terminated.' answer — any other answer (e.g. the query already finished) is shown and audited in REMEDIATION_LOG as FAILED with Snowflake's message. | live rows / seconds | ops_sql.running_queries -> INFORMATION_SCHEMA.QUERY_HISTORY_BY_WAREHOUSE |

*Columns:* **Running queries table** — QUERY_ID, USER_NAME (+resolved), WAREHOUSE_NAME, EXECUTION_STATUS, START_TIME, ELAPSED_S, QUERY_PREVIEW, PROFILE deep-link; **Emergency lever catalogue** — Reference markdown of levers, exact statement templates, and when to use each (kill-switch guidance)

## Proof
*2 sections · 25 metrics.* Renamed from Decision Studio in v4.597 (Option C). It answers three questions: does OVERWATCH pay for itself, what does each saving rest on, and what is ahead. The page is **read-only** (it has no write path, which is why EXECUTIVE can open it) and sits in the Analyze nav group for every profile. Its page-open verdict line is `proof_verdict` over the same shared, per-render-memoized reads the Proof tab uses. Old Decision Studio links and saved views remap here or to the tab's new home (see the next section). The module files and the `decision_section` key kept their names.

### Proof
The director-facing proof, merging the old Scorecard and ROI tabs. **Account-wide**: SAVINGS_LEDGER has no company grain, so the page's Company and Window filters do NOT apply. The verified run-rate, Saved to date, Added this quarter, the ROI multiple and the attribution split are whole-ledger SQL aggregates (savings_summary_quarter, ledger_attribution); Realization, Settling and the other counts, the "estimated, awaiting proof" figure and the per-item rows come from the newest ≤5,000 ledger rows, and the page says so when that cap binds. An unconfigured ledger shows a needs-setup state (apply the V051+ action layer; a privilege error says to re-run the grants in snowflake/roles.sql). v4.606: only a true absence is needs-setup — any other failed ledger read renders unavailable with its error, the page verdict says the proof record could not be read, and Proof ▸ Pipeline's Settling delta reads 'ledger unavailable'. A failed acceptance (ACTION_QUEUE) or precision (ALERT_EVENTS) read is its own Watch line in the page verdict ('team follow-through / alert precision could not be read', or 'needs setup' for a true absence), never listed as 'not yet measured'.

| Metric | Means | Formula | Unit | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Pays for itself (hero)** | Verified savings run-rate as a multiple of OVERWATCH's own run cost; ≥1× means it pays for itself. The delta names both sides. | roi_multiple(verified_active, run_cost): RATIO = round(verified_active/run_cost,1) (None when the run cost is 0 or unknown). verified_active = VERIFIED_ACTIVE_MONTHLY_USD (VERIFIED, superseded twins excluded, VERIFIED_AT within SAVINGS_ACTIVE_MONTHS=12 months, so it does not reset when a quarter starts, and not reverted: a booked warehouse change the daily scan later saw made costlier drops out — or a change the same scan saw with it, one measured window, was undone — v4.600 #31, mart_sql._ledger_counts_predicate). run_cost = APP_CREDITS_30D x rate (the app warehouse's trailing 30 days). | multiple (x) | mart_sql.savings_summary_quarter (SQL, uncapped) + mart_sql.app_cost_last_30d -> FACT_WAREHOUSE_DAILY; logic.proof.roi_multiple |
| **Verified savings run-rate** | The monthly savings run-rate of items verified in the last 12 months and not reverted (v4.600: a booked warehouse change the daily scan later saw undone — or a change the same scan saw with it — leaves it; the delta counts the undone items verified in the last 12 months, whole-ledger SQL REVERTED_ACTIVE_ITEMS). Each verified amount is a recurring MONTHLY saving measured after the change, never an estimate. It is a $/mo run-rate, not cumulative dollars (that is Saved to date). | Value = VERIFIED_ACTIVE_MONTHLY_USD from SQL (the same number as the hero numerator). The delta "across all N verified" uses ledger_totals over the ledger rows. | USD per month | mart_sql.savings_summary_quarter; SAVINGS_LEDGER via mart_sql.savings_ledger(limit=None) |
| **Saved to date (v4.600)** | Dollars already saved, not a run-rate. The delta splits what was measured in the change-scan windows from what is carried forward at the verified rate, and names the part saved before a change was undone; the chip reads 'accrued'. Never feeds the ROI multiple or the verdict; Proof only. | Σ over VERIFIED, non-twin rows of VERIFIED_USD / 30 × whole days from the day the scan saw the linked change (VERIFIED_AT for rows booked by hand) to the earliest of today, VERIFIED_AT + 12 months and the day the scan saw the change (or a co-attributed change seen with it) undone. Measured part = VERIFIED_USD / 30 × LEAST(days, AFTER_DAYS, days from the change to VERIFIED_AT); carried = total − measured. '—' when the whole-ledger summary read fails (never the capped frame). At $0: 'nothing verified yet' only when no item is verified, else 'nothing accrued yet'. | USD | mart_sql.savings_summary_quarter SAVED_TO_DATE_USD, SAVED_MEASURED_USD, SAVED_BEFORE_REVERT_USD, SAVED_SINCE_DATE; logic.proof.saved_to_date / saved_to_date_card |
| **Added this quarter** | Run-rate of the items verified since the current calendar quarter began. | VERIFIED_QTD_USD from SQL, shown with '/mo' | USD per month | mart_sql.savings_summary_quarter |
| **Realization rate** | Verified $ as a share of what those same verified items were estimated to save up front. Near 100% means the estimates held up; above 100% means savings beat the estimate. | realization_pct = realized_verified_usd / realized_estimated_usd x 100 over VERIFIED rows with a positive ESTIMATED_USD. 'n/a' when nothing verified carried an estimate; the delta then counts those items as "N auto-measured, M verified by hand" (auto = SOURCE_CHANGE_ID set or SOURCE='auto'; fixed in v4.597, it always read 0 before). It also shows "carried: X% vs OVERWATCH's own estimate" when a carried figure exists. | percent | logic.actions.ledger_totals over mart_sql.savings_ledger(limit=None) |
| **Carried realization** | Realization against OVERWATCH's OWN up-front estimate, carried onto the auto-measured row. Autobooked rows book $0 up front, so the plain ratio usually cannot see them. It is a separately labelled figure and never overwrites the Realization rate. | proof.carried_realization(ledger_with_attribution(ledger, attribution)). Per live row, the effective estimate is the first POSITIVE of ESTIMATED_USD (the row's own), TWIN_ESTIMATED_USD (the superseded manual twin's), REMEDIATION_EST_USD (the executed remediation's) and REC_EST_USD (the idle alert's recommended $/mo), all recorded before the change. Eligible rows are VERIFIED or REJECTED with a positive effective estimate, excluding LBA-1 co-attributed $0 rows. A REJECTED row counts as 0 realized. carried_pct = Σrealized/Σestimate x 100. | percent | mart_sql.savings_ledger + mart_sql.ledger_attribution; logic.proof.carried_realization |
| **Settling** | Change-scan rows still measuring: they settle themselves when their 14-day window closes, so nobody has to verify them by hand. | auto_settle_pending_count = ESTIMATED rows with SOURCE 'auto', excluding reverted rows (v4.600); the delta names the next settle date (~ TRACKING_UNTIL + 1 day). | count | logic.actions.ledger_totals; TRACKING_UNTIL from the linked WAREHOUSE_CHANGE_REGISTRY row |
| **Acted on** | Of the recommendations the team DECIDED on in the last 90 days, the share acted on (DONE) rather than dismissed (DROPPED). Open items are still undecided and count neither way. | ACCEPTANCE_PCT = DONE_N/(DONE_N+DROPPED_N) x 100; delta 'done · dismissed · open'; '—' when nothing is decided. v4.606: a failed read is not a measured zero — the card shows '—' with 'ACTION_QUEUE needs setup / unavailable (read failed)' and a state below the cards says which read failed. | percent | mart_sql.action_acceptance(90) -> ACTION_QUEUE; logic.proof.acceptance_summary |
| **Alert precision** | When a rule fires and is resolved, how often it was real. | PRECISION_PCT = ACTIONED/(ACTIONED+NOISE) x 100 (EXPECTED closes excluded); ok at ≥ 70%; the delta adds the unlabeled share (a high share means the number is not trustworthy yet). v4.606: a failed read shows 'ALERT_EVENTS needs setup / unavailable (read failed)', never '0 actioned · 0 noise'. | percent | mart_sql.rule_precision(90) -> ALERT_EVENTS resolution kinds (probe); logic.proof.account_precision |
| **On solid evidence** | Share of the recommendation board's three evidence signals (cache, latency, fail rate) present per family: how much of the advice rests on complete evidence. | mean(EVIDENCE_COVERAGE) x 100 over prioritize_workloads(workload_portfolio(30,'ALL',200)) | percent | workbench_sql.workload_portfolio -> MART_PATTERN_COST_DAILY + MART_QUERY_FAMILY_DAILY (historical, probe) |
| **Superseded-twin caption** | Manual bookings superseded by the auto-measured row for the same warehouse change are excluded, so one change is never counted twice. | superseded_count / superseded_estimated_usd from split_superseded | count / USD | ledger_totals; clean-up on Cost ▸ Optimization & Savings ▸ Remediation & ledger |
| **Volume-confounded caption** | Verified items whose 14-day measured window shows query volume outside 0.7–1.3x of baseline: part of that delta may be workload, not the lever. Disclosure only; the dollars are never adjusted. | volume_confounded_count / _usd over VERIFIED rows with VOLUME_CONFOUNDED | count / USD per month | mart_sql.savings_ledger VOLUME_CONFOUNDED |
| **What each saving rests on (per-item evidence table)** | One row per live ledger item: what was changed, what the change scan measured, how long it was measured, and who gets credit. Superseded manual twins are excluded, as from every total. | proof.evidence_rows(ledger, attribution): ledger_with_attribution merges on ITEM_ID; sorted by VERIFIED_USD descending (NULL last, shown as '—'). Per-row display only: no headline is summed from it. | rows | mart_sql.savings_ledger(limit=None) (the v4.597 evidence columns) + mart_sql.ledger_attribution (recent tier, probe) |
| **Attribution split sentence** | The active verified run-rate split by who gets credit, plus the REGRESSED / NEUTRAL $ and the short-window $. It sits beside the ROI multiple, which is unchanged and still counts every class. | proof.evidence_split(attribution) reads the SQL window columns on row 0: ACTIVE_USD, EXECUTED_ / RECOMMENDED_ / BOOKED_ / ELSEWHERE_ / EXPERIMENT_ACTIVE_USD, REGRESSED_ / NEUTRAL_ACTIVE_USD, SHORT_WINDOW_ACTIVE_USD, ACTIVE_ITEMS, TOTAL_ITEMS. Anchored on active-verified rows exactly like the ROI numerator and computed with window functions before any row cap, never summed in pandas. | USD per month | mart_sql.ledger_attribution -> SAVINGS_LEDGER x WAREHOUSE_CHANGE_REGISTRY x REMEDIATION_LOG x ALERT_EVENTS (joins the alerts cache domain on purpose; kept separate from savings_ledger so the verdict gate does not re-cold on acks) |
| **Newly verified savings — by month (chart)** | The run-rate ADDED each calendar month over the last 12 months (this month so far). The active run-rate above is the trailing sum of these. | savings_month_calendar(ledger, 12): VERIFIED rows, non-superseded and not reverted (v4.600 split_reverted), bucketed by VERIFIED_AT month, SUM(VERIFIED_USD) | USD per month added | SAVINGS_LEDGER; charts.monthly_bars_usd(MONTH_LABEL, VERIFIED_USD) |
| **Where the realized savings come from — by lever (chart + expander table)** | Verified savings grouped by lever (FINDING_TYPE), most valuable first. | savings_by_lever: VERIFIED non-superseded rows grouped by FINDING_TYPE -> VERIFIED_USD=SUM and ITEMS=count over rows not reverted (a lever whose every item was undone is not listed); REALIZATION_PCT = SUM(VERIFIED_USD where ESTIMATED_USD>0) / SUM(positive ESTIMATED_USD) x 100 over every verified row, reverted included (estimate accuracy, the headline Realization rate's population) | USD / count / percent | SAVINGS_LEDGER; charts.bar_usd + styled_table |
| **90-day funnel (caption)** | Savings items estimated → fixes executed → verified (with verified $ and any rejected) over the last 90 days. | SAVINGS_ESTIMATED, FIXES_EXECUTED, SAVINGS_VERIFIED, VERIFIED_USD, SAVINGS_REJECTED | counts / USD | mart_sql.acceptance_funnel(90) -> REMEDIATION_LOG + SAVINGS_LEDGER (probe) |

*Links:* "Change detail → Operations ▸ Change impact" (shown only when the profile can open Operations), "Per-rule alert precision → Alerts ▸ Rules" (only when it can open Alerts, which fixes the old READER dead link) and "Priced pipeline → Pipeline".

*Columns (evidence table):* **STATE** — ESTIMATED / VERIFIED / REJECTED.; **VERIFIED_USD** — Verified monthly run-rate; NULL shows '—'.; **LEVER** — FINDING_TYPE (the savings lever).; **TARGET** — TARGET_OBJECT, else the linked change's warehouse.; **CHANGE** — 'old → new' from the linked WAREHOUSE_CHANGE_REGISTRY row.; **VERDICT** — The change scan's performance verdict for that change.; **MEASURED_AFTER_DAYS** — Days of measured actuals after the change.; **WINDOW** — Change-scan rows only: 'full 14-day window' (settled under V153), `settles ~<TRACKING_UNTIL + 1>` (still measuring), `awaiting settle (window closed <date>)`, 'short window (pre-V153)' (settled on about 3 days and never rewritten) or 'not measurable' (closed with no metered credits after the change). Blank on app-booked rows.; **ATTRIBUTION** — Who gets credit, first match wins: 'Experiment (verified by hand)' (FINDING_TYPE EXPERIMENT); 'Booked in OVERWATCH' (no SOURCE_CHANGE_ID: booked in the app); 'Executed by OVERWATCH' (an adopted row, an alert closed-loop row, an EXECUTED REMEDIATION_LOG ALTER on the same warehouse and setting from LEDGER_TWIN_MATCH_DAYS before to 1h after the scan saw it, or an auto row that superseded a manual twin); 'Recommended by OVERWATCH, executed elsewhere' (a COST_IDLE_OPPORTUNITY alert on the warehouse in the 30 days before an AUTO_SUSPEND change made elsewhere); 'Detected elsewhere' (any other detected change: Snowsight, Terraform, ...).
